GI-SAE supplements the reconstruction loss with an Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) loss that trains the encoder to produce similar activations for traces of the same problem, regardless of language or token position. We then test whether the resulting shared features are functionally interchangeable by swapping their values between languages during the model's forward pass and measuring the resulting change in output, quantified by Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence per feature. Although GI-SAE yields higher CKA and Jaccard similarity at nearly every layer, higher geometric similarity does not consistently imply greater functional interchangeability. We find that cross-language feature sharing is model- and architecture-dependent in this sample and appears at different depths in different models. GI-SAE primarily amplifies cross-language structure already present: the pattern is model-specific, with strengthening in Qwen, no functional benefit in Gemma, and mixed layer-dependent effects in Llama and Phi.

</details>


### [68] [Learning to Grade Efficiently: A Bandit-Driven Prompt-Selection Framework for Low-Cost LLM Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23814)
*Olga Manakina,Igor Bogdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）框架的成本感知提示词选择方法，用于大规模语言模型的自动作文评分（AES）。该方法将每种提示词类型视为一个"臂"，在推理过程中自适应选择最优提示策略，在保持评分准确性的同时将LLM调用次数减少78.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自动作文评分方法通常使用固定的提示词选择，无法应对运营成本问题和不断变化的最优配置需求，缺乏在推理过程中动态调整提示策略的能力。

Method: 将每种提示词类型建模为多臂老虎机控制器中的一个臂，实现推理过程中提示策略的自适应选择。实现了四种评分方案（多步骤vs单步骤评估，有无校准示例），并跟踪token使用量、延迟和一致性指标以生成成本-可靠性学习曲线。

Result: 在IELTS写作任务2的实验中，MAB框架达到了与穷举网格搜索相当的评分准确度，同时将LLM调用次数减少了78.4%。多步骤结合校准示例的方法取得了最高的准确度。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个将在线控制机制应用于AES提示策略自适应选择的研究，将提示词选择从离线超参数优化问题转化为高效的在线学习任务，为教育技术平台在运营成本与评估有效性之间取得平衡提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated essay scoring (AES), but contemporary approaches typically employ fixed prompt selection, failing to address operational cost concerns and evolving optimal configurations. We propose a novel cost-aware approach that treats each prompt type as an arm in a multi-armed bandit (MAB) controller, enabling adaptive selection of optimal prompting strategies during inference. Our experiments on IELTS Writing Task 2 essays show that the MAB framework achieves comparable scoring accuracy to exhaustive grid search while reducing LLM calls by 78.4\% to find the best grading approach. We implemented four distinct grading recipes (multi-step vs. single-step assessment, with vs. without calibration examples) and found that the multi-step approach with examples achieves the highest accuracy. By tracking token usage and latency alongside agreement metrics, we produce the first cost-reliability learning curves for essay scoring, providing actionable insights for educational technology platforms that must balance operational costs against assessment validity. This work represents the first application of online control mechanisms to adaptively select prompting strategies in AES, transforming prompt selection from an offline hyperparameter optimization problem into an efficient online learning task.

</details>


### [69] [Revenge of Monosemanticity: Specialized Neurons Improve Data Efficiency in MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24007)
*Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Enric Boix-Adsera,Fivos Kalogiannis,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多层感知机（MLPs）在回归任务中面对聚类数据时，会自然地发展出"单语义特化神经元"——即单个神经元与特定输入区域的预测特征强对齐。MLPs学习的不是单一的全局低维表示，而是一组局部低维表示，这些表示可以共同跨越高维空间，并且这种特化在数据效率上优于基于全局低维表示的特征学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征学习理论主要关注全局低维预测几何结构的涌现，但作者认为这一图景不完整。需要理解神经网络在更复杂数据分布（如聚类数据）下实际的组织和学习方式。

Method: 通过在聚类数据的回归问题中对MLPs进行分析，证明MLPs会自然地出现单语义特化神经元（monosemantic specialized neurons），即神经元与特定输入区域相关的预测特征强对齐。作者从理论上证明了这种局部特化机制的优越性。

Result: MLPs不是学习单一的全局低维表示，而是学习一组局部低维表示，这些表示合在一起可以跨越高维空间。这种特化机制被证明能给MLPs带来数据效率优势，优于基于全局低维表示的特征学习方法。

Conclusion: 神经网络的表示学习机制比现有理论所描述的更为复杂——在聚类数据下，网络倾向于发展局部特化而非全局统一的低维结构，这对理解神经网络行为以及设计更高效的特征学习方法具有重要启示。

Abstract: Understanding how neural networks learn and organize features is central to understanding their behavior. Much existing theory of feature learning has focused on the emergence of a global low-dimensional predictive geometry. We show that this picture is incomplete. In regression problems with clustered data, we demonstrate that multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) naturally develop monosemantic specialized neurons: individual neurons become strongly aligned with a specific predictive feature relevant to a particular region of the input space. Rather than learning a single global low-dimensional representation, MLPs learn a collection of local low-dimensional representations that can collectively span a high-dimensional space. This specialization provably gives MLPs a data-efficiency advantage over feature-learning methods based on a global low-dimensional representation.

</details>


### [70] [AQLoRA: A Zero-Search Recipe for Fast Quantized LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23816)
*Md Romyull Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AQLoRA是一种自适应的量化微调方法，通过一次CPU遍历根据NF4重建误差选择顶层K个层保留在fp16（跳过反量化以节省时间），无需搜索或校准数据。在速度设置下训练比QLoRA快11.1±2.7%，精度损失约1个点；质量设置下快4.8±2.4%且精度与QLoRA持平。论文还总结了共享硬件上计时的三条规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLoRA虽然节省内存但不节省时间，因为每个4-bit权重需要实时反量化，导致训练速度比fp16 LoRA慢。作者希望找回这部分时间，同时不牺牲过多精度。

Method: 通过一次CPU遍历（无需搜索和校准数据）对权重按NF4重建误差排序，在内存预算下将前K层保留在fp16以跳过反量化。提供两种设置：质量设置（自适应所有层）和速度设置（只自适应顶部块，使反向传播提前停止）。

Result: 在Commonsense-170K上评估六种模型、四种架构家族（1.4B到14B）。速度设置训练快11.1±2.7%，比QLoRA最差也快7%；质量设置快4.8±2.4%，精度与QLoRA持平，比fp16 LoRA差不到1个点，仅多0.2GiB内存。

Conclusion: AQLoRA能复现Unsloth手工挑选的动态4-bit选择，且只需数秒而无需重复校准。实验还发现两个失败的对照组方案（按权重密度选适配器层、按量化误差选保护层均不比随机更好），保护层的数量而非身份才是速度效果的关键。

Abstract: Quantized fine-tuning (QLoRA) saves memory but not time. It dequantizes every 4-bit weight on the fly, so it trains more slowly than fp16 LoRA. We present AQLoRA (Adaptive-Quantization LoRA), a recipe that buys part of that time back. One CPU pass over the weights sets everything, with no search and no calibration data. The pass ranks layers by NF4 reconstruction error and keeps the top-K in fp16 under a memory budget. Those layers skip dequantization, which is where the speed comes from. A quality setting adapts every layer. A speed setting adapts only the top blocks, so the backward pass stops early. The rule reproduces Unsloth's hand-curated dynamic-4bit selection exactly, in seconds, where search-based allocation needs repeated calibration passes.
  We evaluate on Commonsense-170K across six models and four architecture families, from 1.4B to 14B. The speed setting trains 11.1 +/- 2.7% faster than well-tuned QLoRA and gives up about one accuracy point. It was faster in all nine independent timing sessions, at worst by 7%. The quality setting trains 4.8 +/- 2.4% faster. Its accuracy is level with QLoRA on every model and within a point of fp16 LoRA, for 0.2 GiB more memory. These error bars are measured between independent sessions, not within one. Earning them taught us three rules for timing on shared hardware. Fix the measurement duration, not the step count. Measure the noise floor from a duplicated arm, not a nearly identical method. Repeat whole sessions: a floor computed inside one sweep understates the real uncertainty several times over, and the random seed controls almost none of it.
  We validate the recipe with controls and report the two that failed. Choosing adapter layers by weight density is no better than random. Choosing protected layers by quantization error is not either. The count of protected layers, not their identity, carries the speed effect.

</details>


### [71] [ChorusTIC: Training-Free Multivariate Time Series Classification via Chorus In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24033)
*Juntao Fang,Shifeng Xie,Ruichu Cai,Shengji Zheng,Zijian Li,Keli Zhang,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChorusTIC是一个面向时间序列分类的原生基础模型，通过in-context learning在异构通道配置下实现跨数据集分类，无需目标任务的参数更新。仅用合成数据进行预训练，在UEA-30和UCR-128基准上展现出强大的全上下文和低标签性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要支持预测和迁移表示学习，但分类任务通常仍需要在每个目标数据集上拟合特定分类器，且多变量输入的各个通道往往被独立编码，无法处理异构通道配置的跨数据集分类问题。

Method: ChorusTIC结合了三个关键组件：1) episode一致的随机子通道槽拼接（Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation），将可变通道配置映射为固定宽度表示；2) 共享双轴编码器建模时间与跨通道交互；3) 基于上下文分布的特征轴校准和防泄漏的in-context学习来预测查询标签。模型仅使用合成标注episode（含共享任务背景的上下文集和查询集）进行预训练，类别通过稀疏时间或跨通道规则区分。

Result: 在完整的UEA-30和UCR-128基准评测中，ChorusTIC展现出强大的全上下文性能和低标签性能，无需对每个目标数据集拟合专门的分类器。

Conclusion: ChorusTIC证明了一种分类原生的时间序列基础模型范式：通过合成数据预训练和in-context learning，可以在没有目标任务参数更新的情况下实现异构通道配置下的跨数据集分类，为时间序列分类基础模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Time series classification underpins applications in healthcare, sensing, and industrial monitoring. Although time series foundation models support forecasting and transferable representation learning, classification still typically requires fitting a task-specific classifier on each target dataset, while individual channels of multivariate inputs are often encoded independently. We introduce ChorusTIC, a classification-native foundation model for in-context classification across heterogeneous channel configurations without target-task parameter updates. ChorusTIC combines episode-consistent Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation with a shared dual-axis encoder to model temporal and cross-channel interactions and map variable channel configurations into a fixed-width representation independent of the original channel count. It then calibrates feature axes using context-derived distributions and predicts query labels through leakage-protected in-context learning. We pretrain ChorusTIC solely on synthetic labeled episodes comprising context and query sets that share a task background, with classes distinguished by sparse temporal or cross-channel rules. Evaluations on the complete UEA-30 and UCR-128 archives show strong full-context and low-label performance without target-specific classifier fitting.

</details>


### [72] [Generating Intervention Hypotheses using Explainable Explanations on Graphs: G2I, a Two-Stage Greedy Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23835)
*Mulin Tian,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将反事实解释重新框架为干预设计问题，提出了一种基于贪心搜索的局部反事实生成方法（可直接识别最小化、可操作的节点特征和邻居条件变化）和基于DNF覆盖问题的网络级干预选择方法（在预算约束下具有次模性保证）。实验表明该方法相比掩码优化的反事实GNN解释器更高效、更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实GNN解释器（如CF-GNNExplainer和CF²）依赖连续掩码优化，存在三个问题：隐含假设边操作可行、可能将精力投入不可变或不可操作的属性、计算开销大。此外，解释方法本身难以让非AI专家的领域专家理解。作者质疑简单方法是否能生成好的解释。

Method: 1) 局部层面：将反事实解释重构为干预设计问题，通过贪心搜索直接识别最小化、可操作的节点特征和邻居条件改变，并推导贪心搜索提供保证的条件（经验上近似满足）。2) 将反事实转换为可解释规则以支持实际干预。3) 网络层面：将干预选择形式化为预算约束下的析取范式（DNF）覆盖问题，该问题非递减且近似次模，从而支持带理论保证的贪心算法。

Result: 在合成图和真实自杀风险网络上的实验表明，该方法能产生可扩展、成本效益高的干预策略，相比基于掩码的反事实方法在效率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 简单方法确实可以生成好的解释。通过将反事实解释与干预设计直接对接，并在局部和网络层面都提供理论保证，该方法在实用性、可解释性和计算效率上均优于现有掩码优化方法。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making in public health and social science can greatly benefit from predictive models, yet translating predictions into effective interventions requires explaining the model behavior. While Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are well-suited for modeling relational data, existing explanation methods largely operate at the node level and fall short of supporting actionable, network-level intervention design. Existing counterfactual GNN explainers, such as CF-GNNExplainer and CF$^2$, rely on continuous mask optimization over features and edges, which implicitly assume feasible edge manipulation, may allocate effort to immutable or non-actionable attributes, and incur substantial computational overhead. Further, the method of arriving at the explanation itself is difficult to explain to a domain specialist who is not an AI expert. Can simple methods generate good explanations? To explore this, we reframe counterfactual explanation as an intervention design problem. At the local level, we generate counterfactuals via a greedy search that directly identifies minimal, actionable changes to node features and neighbor-level conditions. We derive conditions under which the greedy search provides guarantees, and empirically show that these conditions are approximately met. These counterfactuals are converted into interpretable rules suitable for real-world intervention. At the network level, we formulate intervention selection as a Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) coverage problem under a budget constraint, which is nondecreasing and approximately submodular, enabling a greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world suicide risk networks demonstrate that our approach produces scalable, cost-effective intervention strategies with significantly improved efficiency over mask-based counterfactual methods.

</details>


### [73] [Knowing When to Ask for Help: Bayesian Self-Escalation in Hierarchical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24087)
*Nadeem Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新的LLM智能体委托范式：在推理过程中（而非推理前或推理后）识别自身难以成功并动态转移控制权给更强模型，通过贝叶斯最优停止框架和在线能力后验估计实现了代内委托。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统中的模型委托存在两种局限：要么在推理开始前由路由器决定（无法根据实际推理进程调整），要么在完整响应后由验证器评分重试（浪费了推理过程中可用的信号）。作者提出第三种范式——在推理过程中实时识别自我能力不足并转移控制，从而更高效地利用推理过程中的信息，降低计算成本同时保持任务成功率。

Method: 将代内委托形式化为一个贝叶斯最优停止问题，其核心是一个从标注轨迹中学习的能力后验（competence posterior）——在线估计智能体最终任务成功的概率，充分统计量来自标注轨迹而非原始熵。方法包括：1) 闭式推导近视升级阈值（myopic escalation threshold）；2) 通过动态规划刻画最优策略；3) 证明最优策略是时变阈值且不对原始信号做形状假设；4) 提供指数分离、校准相关的遗憾界以及有限样本保证（n条标注校准轨迹下遗憾以1/sqrt(n)衰减）。

Result: 受控仿真实验验证了理论的每个预测，包括1/sqrt(n)的衰减率。在Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B代码级联（MBPP数据集，257个任务）的真实模型验证中，确认了预先注册的三个预测中的两个：升级边界（escalation frontier）在相同成本下优于事后路由（post-hoc routing），且累积能力信念的区分度随生成过程提高。

Conclusion: 代内委托是一种可行且理论上可刻画的新型模型委托范式。通过贝叶斯最优停止和在线能力后验估计，智能体可以在推理过程中自适应决定是否升级到更强模型，在相同成本下取得优于事后路由的性能，为LLM智能体的自适应计算分配提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Current LLM agent systems decide delegation before reasoning begins (a router picks a model) or after a response is complete (a verifier scores it and may retry). We study a third regime: an agent that recognises, during its own reasoning, that it is unlikely to succeed and transfers control to a stronger model. We formulate intra-generation delegation as a Bayesian optimal-stopping problem over a learned competence posterior -- an online estimate of the agent's eventual task success whose sufficient statistics are learned from labelled trajectories, not read off raw entropy. We derive the myopic escalation threshold in closed form, characterise the optimal policy via dynamic programming, and prove that the optimal policy is a time-varying threshold with no shape assumption on the raw signal. We further prove exponential separation of the oracle belief at the Chernoff-information rate of the signal, a regret bound governed by the calibration of the posterior, and a finite-sample guarantee: with n labelled calibration trajectories the deployed plug-in policy's regret decays as 1/sqrt(n). A controlled simulation study confirms each prediction of the theory, including the predicted 1/sqrt(n) rate. We additionally report a real-model validation on a Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B->7B code cascade (MBPP, 257 tasks), confirming two of three pre-registered predictions: the escalation frontier dominates post-hoc routing at equal cost, and the cumulative competence belief's discrimination rises over generation.

</details>


### [74] [PuzzleKV: Page-Wise Low-Rank Decomposition for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23843)
*Zizhong Wang,Jieying Wang,Zhao Zhang,Jiajia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PuzzleKV是一种免训练、免校准的KV缓存压缩方法，将每个头的KV缓存按固定长度逻辑页划分，将每个已完成的页视为独立的压缩单元，利用页内低秩结构进行分解压缩。在约60%存储下达到96%以上的Full KV性能，结合量化仅用18.7%存储即可保留93%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受限于KV缓存的内存开销。现有低秩压缩方法（基于模型权重、校准激活或共享基座）无法捕捉页内细微但重要的信息，导致压缩精度受限。

Method: 将每个头的KV缓存划分为固定长度的逻辑页，发现页内存在显著低秩结构。将每个完成的页作为独立压缩单元，按层和KV头对页进行分解，在自回归解码中直接对稠密页和分解页计算注意力，并增量压缩新完成的页。

Result: 在约60%原始KV缓存存储下，PuzzleKV在两个模型和所有基准设置中达到96%以上的Full KV性能；在RULER上显著优于Global SVD，在LongBench上性能相当。与量化结合后，仅用18.7%存储即可保留93%以上性能。

Conclusion: PuzzleKV证明利用页内局部低秩结构进行独立压缩是有效且免训练、免校准的KV缓存压缩方案，可在极端压缩比下保持良好性能。

Abstract: Long-context inference in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly limited by the memory required for the key-value (KV) cache. KV cache compression addresses this problem by reducing the storage cost of previous tokens. Among existing approaches, low-rank compression is particularly attractive because it represents every token in reduced dimensions. Previous low-rank methods typically derive fixed projection spaces from model weights, construct fixed spaces from calibration activations, or construct a shared basis over a broad cache region. Such representations may not capture detailed but important information. We partition each per-head KV cache into fixed-length logical pages and observe substantial low-rank structure within individual pages. Based on this observation, we propose PuzzleKV, a training- and calibration-free method that treats each completed page as an independent compression unit. PuzzleKV decomposes pages within each layer and KV head, computes attention directly over dense and factorized pages, and incrementally compresses newly eligible pages during autoregressive decoding. Experiments across models, context lengths, and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of PuzzleKV under matched storage budgets. At approximately 60% of the original KV cache storage, PuzzleKV achieves more than 96% of Full KV performance across both evaluated models and all benchmark settings, with substantial gains over Global SVD on RULER and competitive performance on LongBench. To achieve a more aggressive compression ratio, PuzzleKV can be further combined with quantization while retaining more than 93% of Full KV performance using only 18.7% of the original storage.

</details>


### [75] [Robust Data-Collection Policy Learning for Low-Variance Online Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24146)
*Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Licheng Luo,Rohan Chandra,Nan Jiang,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双循环梯度算法，用于学习对转移函数不确定性鲁棒且高效的行为策略，以降低强化学习策略评估中的在线评估方差。理论推导了转移方差梯度表达式并建立了全局收敛保证，实验表明该方法对转移扰动比现有方法更不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的on-policy策略评估方法在估计策略性能时通常面临高方差问题。已有行为策略搜索方法虽然能降低评估方差，但未考虑转移函数的不确定性——仿真环境中的转移函数常因建模误差与真实世界存在差异，导致在仿真中训练的行为策略部署到真实环境时仍会产生高方差，从而需要昂贵的真实环境采样。

Method: 提出一种双循环梯度算法（double-loop gradient-based algorithm），用于学习对转移不确定性既高效又鲁棒的行为策略。理论部分推导了新颖的转移方差梯度表达式（transition-variance gradient expressions），并证明了算法具有全局收敛保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，与现有方法相比，该方法对转移扰动（transition perturbations）的敏感性更低，为其实际应用提供了支持性证据。

Conclusion: 本文通过将转移不确定性纳入行为策略学习框架，成功构建了兼具评估效率和鲁棒性的行为策略学习方法，在理论上提供了收敛保证，在数值上验证了其相对于现有方法的优势。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning policy evaluation, classic on-policy methods often suffer from high variance when estimating policy performance. To mitigate this issue, behavior policy search has been proposed to learn data-collecting policies tailored to reduce online evaluation variance. However, these approaches do not account for uncertainties in the transition functions. In practice, simulator transitions often differ from the real world due to modeling errors or approximation limitations. As a result, behavior policies trained in simulation may still yield high variance when deployed in real environments, leading to costly reliance on real-world evaluation samples. In this work, we propose a double-loop gradient-based algorithm for learning behavior policies that are both efficient and robust to transition uncertainty. Theoretically, we derive novel transition-variance gradient expressions and establish global convergence guarantees for the algorithm. Numerically, we demonstrate that our method is less sensitive to transition perturbations than existing approaches, providing supportive evidence for its practical utility.

</details>


### [76] [FlowNeg: GFlowNet-Guided Diverse Hard Negative Sampling for Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23849)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Naoshin Anzum Hridi,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlowNeg，一种基于上下文条件的分层生成流网络（GFlowNet）用于知识图谱嵌入中的负采样。FlowNeg 通过先选类型再选实体的分层方式，以奖励比例进行摊销采样，无需对整个实体集进行归一化。其终止奖励结合了基于模型的难度与训练专用结构分数（用于避免与保留正样本冲突）。在 5 个架构 × 5 个基准的 25 个网格单元中，FlowNeg 的 MRR 均值在 24 个单元中优于 EMU 和 IF-NS。在固定消融控制实验中，FlowNeg 在 FB15k-237/RotatE 上取得 0.359 MRR，显著优于 EMU 的 0.346。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负采样决定 KGE 模型是从信息丰富的反例中学习，还是浪费在不可信的损坏样本上。均匀负采样多样性好但过于简单；硬负样本挖掘器则过度集中于少数实体，且与保留的正样本碰撞更多。现有方法难以在"多样性"与"难度"之间取得良好平衡，且硬负采样器常把结构相似性当作开放世界的真实标签，导致采样偏差。

Method: FlowNeg 采用上下文条件的分层生成流网络：给定正三元组和损坏侧，首先生成类型（type），再生成实体（entity）。其终止奖励结合了有界模型硬度分数和仅用于训练的结构分数（用于避免与保留正样本冲突），构建关系特定的类型兼容支持集。作者推导了奖励设计，特化了标准轨迹平衡（Trajectory Balance），并界定了残差不平衡对终止概率和模式概率的乘法扰动。

Result: 在五个架构和五个基准的 5 种子网格实验中，FlowNeg 的平均 MRR 在 25 个单元中 24 个优于 EMU（平均 +0.0172）和 IF-NS（平均 +0.0160）。在固定负样本数量、诊断预算和计算量的 15 种子对照实验（FB15k-237/RotatE）中，FlowNeg 达到 0.359±0.001 MRR，EMU 为 0.346±0.002，且 FlowNeg 具有近均匀的固定分区多样性、高梯度信息量和低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持"模式覆盖式"（mode-covering）负采样策略：即在生成负样本时覆盖多种类型/实体模式，而不将结构相似性视为开放世界的真值预言机。FlowNeg 在多样性和难度之间取得了更优平衡，且其分层生成方式天然支持关系特定的类型约束，避免了硬负采样器常见的碰撞问题。

Abstract: Negative sampling determines whether a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model learns from informative counterexamples or wastes updates on implausible corruptions. Uniform negatives are diverse but easy, whereas hard-negative miners concentrate on few entities and collide more with held-out positives. We introduce FlowNeg, a context-conditioned hierarchical generative flow network that amortizes reward-proportional sampling without normalizing a composite reward over the entity set: given a positive triple and corruption side, it selects a type, then an entity. Its terminal reward combines bounded model-based hardness with a training-only structural score for held-out-positive collision, over a relation-specific type-compatible support. We derive the reward, specialize standard trajectory balance, and bound multiplicatively how residual imbalance perturbs terminal and mode probability. Across a descriptive five-seed grid of five architectures and five benchmarks, FlowNeg has higher mean MRR than EMU and than IF-NS in 24 of 25 cells ($+0.0172$ and $+0.0160$ on average). A separate 15-seed FB15k-237/RotatE control fixing negative count, diagnostic budget, and compute gives FlowNeg $0.359\pm0.001$ MRR against $0.346\pm0.002$ for EMU, with near-uniform fixed-partition diversity, high gradient informativeness, and low collision. The evidence supports mode-covering negative generation without treating structural similarity as an open-world truth oracle.

</details>


### [77] [UHI-Bench: Benchmarking Dual-Source Urban Heat Island Modeling Across Cities in Diverse Climate Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23857)
*Wanyun Ling,Chenxi Liu,Yi Xie,Aopu Xu,Zhuoqi Zeng,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UHI-Bench，这是首个用于双源（地表温度和近地表气温）城市热岛建模的基准数据集，整合了动态气象驱动和静态城市形态特征，在20个城市和9个Köppen气候区上评估了20多个基线模型的5项任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市热岛（UHI）有两类物理本质不同的观测（LST-UHI和AirT-UHI），但多数研究仅依赖单一数据源，混用会严重偏差热暴露评估。此外，动态气象与静态城市形态数据的时空不匹配、LST云隙和AirT站点稀疏等问题，都制约了双源UHI建模。目前缺乏一个标准化的双源UHI基准来系统比较不同建模方法。

Method: 构建UHI-Bench基准，采用统一的"信号-机制-迁移"框架，整合动态（气象）与静态（城市形态）环境上下文，在20个城市、9个Köppen气候区上，对4个模型家族的20多个基线模型在5项任务上进行系统评估。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有任务上统一最优，但基础模型（foundation models）表现持续稳定且有竞争力。环境协变量总体上能提升性能，但其效用因数据源和任务而异。跨城市迁移能力更多由UHI机制模式的重叠程度决定，而非气候区相似性。

Conclusion: UHI-Bench提供了实用的UHI建模指导，促进气候数据公平性，并为未来气候研究中的UHI建模发展奠定基础。研究强调双源UHI建模的必要性以及标准化基准对推进该领域的重要性。

Abstract: Urban heat islands (UHIs) are intensifying under climate change, exacerbating thermal exposure risks. Their two primary observations, land surface temperature UHI (LST-UHI) and near-surface air temperature UHI (AirT-UHI), capture physically distinct aspects of urban heat. However, most studies rely on a single source, and substituting one for the other can substantially bias the magnitude and spatial variability of human heat exposure. Accurate UHI modeling also requires dynamic meteorological drivers and static urban morphology features, but spatiotemporal incompatibilities hinder their alignment. Cloud gaps in LST observations and sparse AirT station networks further limit dual-source UHI modeling, motivating cross-city transfer across diverse climates. To bridge these gaps, we introduce UHI-Bench, the first UHI benchmark for dual-source UHI modeling that integrates dynamic and static environmental context. Following a unified signal, mechanism, and transfer framework, it evaluates over 20 baselines from four model families on five tasks across 20 cities and nine Köppen climate classes. Results show that no model is uniformly best, although foundation models remain consistently competitive and stable. Environmental covariates generally improve performance, but their utility varies across sources and tasks. Cross-city transferability is better explained by overlap in UHI regimes than by climate-zone similarity. With the dataset and standardized pipeline, our work provides practical guidance for urban heat modeling, promotes climate data equity, and supports future advances in climate research.

</details>


### [78] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中最小最大最优的交替遗憾（alternating regret）界。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，提出具有O(log d)常数交替遗憾的算法（不随时间T增长），并给出匹配下界；对于一般d维紧凸集上的OCO，给出O(d log(1+T/d))的交替遗憾上界及匹配下界。由此首次获得两玩家一般和博弈中O(1/T)收敛到粗相关均衡（CCE）的非耦合学习动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替后悔（alternating regret）是由博弈中交替学习动力学启发的遗憾概念，此前在OLO上的最好结果为O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})，在OCO上为Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})，存在明显改进空间。同时，在一般和博弈中获得O(1/T)收敛到CCE的非耦合动力学是长期未解问题，已有方法都带有额外的log T因子。

Method: 对于OLO在单纯形上的情形，设计了一种实现常数交替遗憾的算法（不依赖时间T），并证明了匹配的下界；对于一般OCO情形，提出算法达到O(d log(1+T/d))的交替遗憾，同时通过构造证明Ω(d log(1+T/d))的下界，说明log T因子的不可避免性。

Result: - OLO（单纯形上）：O(log d)常数交替遗憾 + 匹配下界，显著改进此前O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})的结果。- 由此获得两玩家零和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到纳什均衡，以及两玩家一般和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到CCE。这是首次O(1/T)收敛到CCE的非耦合学习动力学。- OCO（一般d维紧凸集）：O(d log(1+T/d))交替遗憾 + 匹配下界Ω(d log(1+T/d))，改进此前Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了OLO和OCO中交替遗憾的最小最大最优界问题。在OLO中实现了不随时间增长的常数遗憾，在OCO中证明了log T因子的不可避免性。这一成果在博弈论上具有重要意义，首次实现了两玩家一般和博弈中O(1/T)收敛到CCE的非耦合动力学。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [79] [From Relaxed Indexability to Exact Indexability: A $t$-Step Approach for Partially Observable Restless Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24167)
*Qizhen Jia,Keqin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Liu [10]的线性近似Whittle指数方法，提出t步前瞻阈值策略，通过t步有限时域值迭代定义活跃-被动优势阈值，在部分可观测的restless多臂老虎机问题中逼近精确Whittle指数，并证明其几何收敛速率O(β^t)，数值实验验证了2,715个三态实例的可索引性及显著降低的指数误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的restless多臂老虎机问题中，精确计算Whittle指数需要求解无限时域信念状态问题，没有闭式解。Liu [10]通过线性化决策边界获得闭式近似，但仅使用一步活跃-被动比较，未考虑更长时域的延续价值，导致近似精度受限。本文旨在克服这一局限，提出更精确且可扩展的近似方法。

Method: 提出t步前瞻阈值策略：对每个补贴m，阈值通过t步有限时域值迭代下的活跃减被动优势来定义。t=1时恢复Liu的线性阈值（m独立）；t>1时阈值依赖补贴m，通过首通结构更紧密地逼近精确决策边界。算法不要求可索引性作为输入，并包含可索引性验证机制。

Result: 在原始Whittle可索引性下，证明了t步近似Whittle指数以O(β^t)几何收敛到精确Whittle指数。数值上，2,715个三态实例均通过可索引性验证；P95指数误差从t=1时的2.18×10^-2降至t=8时的8.93×10^-4。在β=0.9999的精确可比实例中，t=2即恢复精确Whittle指数排序。中等深度阈值策略优于一步基线，接近最优动态规划基准，运行时间随t温和增长。

Conclusion: t步前瞻阈值策略有效弥补了Liu [10]一步近似的不足，在保持可扩展性的同时大幅提升Whittle指数近似精度，具有几何收敛保证，且实际计算成本可控。该方法为部分可观测restless多臂老虎机问题提供了更实用的近似求解方案。

Abstract: Whittle index policies offer a scalable method for restless multi-armed bandits, but under partial observability even determining the indifference subsidy at a single belief requires solving an infinite-horizon belief-state problem with no closed-form value function. Liu [10] addresses this difficulty by linearizing the unknown decision boundary, leading to a linear system and a closed-form approximate Whittle index. However, the resulting threshold uses only a one-step active--passive comparison and does not account for longer-horizon continuation values.
  We extend this framework to a \emph{$t$-step lookahead threshold policy}. For each subsidy $m$, the threshold is defined by the active-minus-passive advantage under $t$-step finite-horizon value iteration. At $t=1$, the threshold is $m$-independent and recovers the linear threshold of Liu [10]; for $t>1$, it becomes subsidy-dependent through the induced first-crossing structure and tracks the exact decision boundary more closely. The proposed algorithm does not require indexability as an input and includes an indexability verification. Under the original Whittle indexability, we prove that the $t$-step approximate Whittle index converges geometrically to the exact Whittle index, \[ |\widehat W_t(ω)-W(ω)|=O(β^t). \] Numerically, all 2,715 tested three-state instances are verified as indexable according to the proposed criterion. The P95 index error decreases from $2.18\times10^{-2}$ at $t=1$ to $8.93\times10^{-4}$ at $t=8$. In an exact-comparable instance with $β=0.9999$, $t=2$ already recovers the exact Whittle-index ordering. Moderate-depth threshold policies also outperform the one-step baseline and remain close to the optimal dynamic-programming benchmark, while runtime grows mildly with $t$.

</details>


### [80] [Every Layer Counts: An Exponential $L_2$ Depth Hierarchy for ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23877)
*Itay Safran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了ReLU神经网络在深度上的指数级分离层次结构：每增加一层ReLU层，可以指数级地节省神经元数量。对于任意ℓ≥3，存在一个深度为ℓ、宽度为O(d⁴)的网络实现的函数，但任何深度为ℓ-1且宽度至多2^d/[2d(ℓ-2)]的网络在该函数上的L₂误差至少为1/24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究ReLU网络在固定深度之间的表达能力差异，特别是证明深度层次结构的指数分离。此前缺少较浅深度≥3的固定深度之间的指数分离结果，本文填补了这一空白，并回应了Safran, Eldan, and Shamir (2019)提出的关于深度3对深度2分离的开放问题。

Method: 作者构造了特定的[0,1]值、1-Lipschitz函数，证明了深度ℓ网络可以用O(d⁴)宽度实现该函数，但深度ℓ-1网络在绝对连续分布下需要指数级宽度才能达到较小的L₂误差。通过精确构造和严格的下界分析，建立了跨所有相邻固定深度的指数层次。此外还构造了一个更良性的函数，用多项宽度深度4网络计算，但深度3网络在单位超立方体上需要指数级神经元。

Result: 首次证明了ReLU网络中两个固定深度（较浅深度≥3）之间的指数分离，以及所有相邻固定深度之间的首个指数层次结构。同时回答了Safran等人提出的深度3对深度2分离问题，但指出对应的分布质量集中在指数半径处，不在能导出阈值电路下界的正则性范围内。另一个更良性函数的精确分离也已建立。

Conclusion: ReLU网络的深度层次结构得到严格证明：每增加一层可以节省指数级神经元。这加深了对深度在神经网络表达力中作用的理解，但同时也指出部分构造处于特定正则性范围之外，因此不能直接推导出重大阈值电路下界。

Abstract: We prove a depth hierarchy for ReLU neural networks in which every additional ReLU layer can save exponentially many neurons. For every $\ell\geq 3$, a globally $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz function is realized by a depth-$\ell$ network of width $\mathcal{O}(d^4)$, whereas every depth-$(\ell-1)$ network with unrestricted weights and width at most $2^d/[2d(\ell-2)]$ has squared $L_2$ error at least $1/24$ under an absolutely continuous distribution. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first exponential separation for ReLU networks between two fixed depths whose shallower depth is at least $3$, and the first exponential hierarchy across all adjacent fixed depths. The lower bound also immediately yields the corresponding hierarchy for exact computation. Moreover, the case $\ell=3$ gives a compactly supported depth-$3$-versus-depth-$2$ separation with unrestricted shallow-network weights, answering a question raised by Safran, Eldan, and Shamir (2019, Sec. 2.3). The corresponding distribution nevertheless has all its mass at exponential radius, so the construction falls outside the regularity regime in which such a separation would imply major threshold-circuit lower bounds. We also prove an exact separation for a more benign function. It is computed by a polynomial-width depth-$4$ network, whereas every depth-$3$ network agreeing with it on the unit hypercube requires exponentially many neurons in its first hidden layer, again without any restriction on the weights. The function is globally $[0,1]$-valued and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt d)$-Lipschitz, and maps the unit hypercube onto $[0,1]$.

</details>


### [81] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等距贝尔曼校准（isotonic Bellman calibration），一种一维、模型无关的后处理方法，通过等距（保序）变换修正离线强化学习中边缘化重要性加权（MIW）的占据率估计，减少占据平衡违反而无需重新训练，同时保留初始估计的排序信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小-最大、原始-对偶、拟合不动点等估计器因函数类逼近、正则化或不完全优化，会残留占据平衡违背（occupancy-balance violations）。这些问题难以诊断和降低，因为目标函数缺乏直接的有监督验证损失来指导超参数调优、模型选择和早停。

Method: 将贝尔曼校准刻画为条件不动点性质，等价于校准比率对每个测试函数满足占据平衡。方法通过对一维非递减变换类施加FORE（拟合占据率评估）来校正初始估计的尺度和形状。理论上推导了校准-细化界，证明校准误差小的拟合比率几乎与最优后处理相当；对等距贝尔曼校准建立了有限样本校准保证和相对最优单调变换的KL oracle不等式。

Result: 等距贝尔曼校准能够在统计误差范围内实现较小的校准误差和KL风险（相对于最优单调校正），并为下游目标占据泛函（包括策略价值估计）提供保证。

Conclusion: 等距贝尔曼校作为一种简单高效的后处理工具，可系统性地减少占据平衡违规，且理论上有据可依，为MIW估计器的诊断、调参和模型选择提供了可靠途径。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [82] [Partial Optimal Transport on the Circle for All Transported Masses in O(N log N)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23910)
*Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAWC算法，在圆上精确求解部分最优传输的完整配置文件（所有传输基数下的最优代价），时间复杂度O(N log N)，空间复杂度O(N)。通过自由缺口不变量在每一步找到全局有效的切割，避免了对每个支撑间隙重复运行线算法带来的O(N² log N)额外开销。算法可推广到高维球面S^{d-1}，在遮挡、杂波场景下表现显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分最优传输相比完整OT对异常值、遮挡和杂波更鲁棒，但实际中需要知道整个配置文件（所有传输基数下的代价），而非单一传输量。在实线上PAWL算法可以O(N log N)高效求解，但大量数据（角度、相位、方向、一天中的时间、色调等）本质上是周期性的，位于圆上。圆上的全局环流（或等价于优化切割）使得朴素精确方法需对每个支撑间隙运行一次线算法，代价高达O(N² log N)。消除这个额外的N因子是核心动机。

Method: 核心思想是证明圆上的线结构可以以"无切割"形式保留下来。通过引入自由缺口不变量，算法在每一步都能找到一个切割位置，使得所有之前的局部更新在该切割下仍然保持为合法的线更新。这样无需为每个间隙重新运行线算法，而是单次运行即可同时返回所有K+1个代价、嵌套活动集和计划，以及一个对所有基数同时最优的单一缺口。通过大圆切片，算法可扩展到高维球面S^{d-1}。

Result: PAWC时间复杂度O(N log N)、空间O(N)，在N=4096时完整配置文件的求解仅需0.56ms，而通用求解器对单一传输分数需要1.5秒。在MPEG-7遮挡杂波形状检索中，固定描述符仅改变代价函数时，PAWC保留66%的干净数据检索分数，而平衡圆形OT仅保留16%。在S²上，对抗污染目标时，PAWC将球形切片Wasserstein的拟合误差减半（合成和真实数据均如此）。

Conclusion: 圆上的部分最优传输可以在与实线相同的复杂度下精确求解，无需额外的N因子。自由缺口不变量是使这成为可能的关键理论洞察，它保持了圆形数据上线更新的有效性。该算法在效率（比通用求解器快数千倍）和鲁棒性（在遮挡、杂波场景下显著优于平衡OT）两方面都展现出明显优势，并有潜力扩展到更一般的流形结构。

Abstract: Partial optimal transport compares two measures while leaving part of the mass unmatched, which is what makes it robust to outliers, occlusion, and clutter. The quantity of interest is usually the whole profile - the optimal cost at every transported cardinality - because the right amount to transport is rarely known in advance, and on the real line the PAWL algorithm returns that profile in $O(N\log N)$. Much data is periodic rather than linear: angles, phases, orientations, time of day, hue, and every direction obtained by projecting onto a great circle. On the circle the same problem acquires a global circulation, or equivalently an optimized cut, which the naive exact method handles by running the line algorithm once per support gap, at $O(N^{2}\log N)$. We show that this factor $N$ is unnecessary. The line structure survives in cut-free form, and a free-gap invariant supplies, at every step, a cut at which all previous local updates remain valid line updates. This yields PAWC: an exact $O(N\log N)$ time, $O(N)$ memory algorithm returning all $K+1$ costs, nested active sets and plans in one run, together with a single gap that is simultaneously optimal for every cardinality. Slicing over great circles extends it to $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. Empirically the whole profile costs $0.56$ms at $N=4096$ against $1.5$s for a single transported fraction from a general solver; on occluded, cluttered mpeg-7 shapes, holding the descriptor fixed and varying only the cost, it retains $66\%$ of the clean-data retrieval score against $16\%$ for balanced circular OT, and on $\mathbb{S}^{2}$ it halves the fitting error of spherical sliced Wasserstein against contaminated targets, synthetic and real. Code is available at https://github.com/mint-vu/Partial_Wasserstein_on_Circles.

</details>


### [83] [The Loss Floor of Denoising Score Matching: Fisher Geometry from Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23916)
*Avinash Raju,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型训练中去噪得分匹配目标的冗余部分（excess loss）本质上是条件端点族的Fisher-Rao度量沿扩散轨迹的积分，通过Schrödinger桥变分原理建立了精确的方差分解，并将训练损失下限与信息几何和互信息损失率联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练中使用条件得分回归（去噪得分匹配），但生成最终需要边际得分。尽管两者具有相同的最优解，条件目标在固定噪声状态下仍是随机的，引入不可约的训练损失冗余（excess），作者希望精确刻画并理解这一冗余项的几何与信息论本质。

Method: 作者从Schrödinger桥变分原理出发，将理想目标看作路径空间相对熵的冗余项；对一般污染核在温和正则假设下证明excess精确等于条件端点族Fisher-Rao度量的迹沿扩散轨迹的积分；在Gaussian情形推导闭式解并分析重参数化不变性与高信噪比发散；对比训练中的二阶几何与数值采样误差中的三阶条件统计量。

Result: 得到了去噪目标的条件方差精确分解；Fisher项与噪声状态丧失关于干净数据的互信息的速率成正比；Gaussian情形给出闭式下限，恢复连续时间目标的重参数化不变性，并将高信噪比发散与数据的信息维度关联；证明不同噪声范围或加权下的原始损失因含不同加性下限而不能一致地排名模型。

Conclusion: 去噪得分匹配的训练损失下限可分解为数据驱动的信息流与污染调度/目标决定的权重之积；训练所见的是二阶几何（Fisher-Rao），而数值采样误差涉及三阶条件统计量，两者需要区分；原始损失在不同配置下不可直接比较。

Abstract: Denoising score matching trains diffusion models by regressing onto a conditional score, although generation ultimately requires the marginal score. The two objectives share the same population minimizer, but the conditional target remains random at fixed noisy state and introduces an irreducible excess in the training loss. We isolate this excess and show that, for a general corruption kernel under mild regularity assumptions, it is exactly the trace of the Fisher--Rao metric of the conditional endpoint family, integrated along the diffusion trajectory. This gives an exact conditional-variance decomposition of the denoising objective and identifies the information geometry observed in diffusion latent spaces as an intrinsic component of the training loss. We derive the result from a Schr"odinger bridge variational principle, in which the ideal objective arises as excess path-space relative entropy. For corruption diffusions, the Fisher term is proportional to the rate at which the noisy state loses mutual information about the clean data, separating the loss floor into an information flow determined by the data and a weight determined by the corruption schedule and objective. In the Gaussian case, this yields a closed form for the floor, recovers reparametrization invariance of the continuous-time objective, and relates its high-SNR divergence to the information dimension of the data. Finally, we show that raw losses obtained with different noise ranges or weightings need not rank models consistently because they contain different additive floors, and contrast the second-order geometry seen by training with the third-order conditional statistics entering numerical sampling error.

</details>


### [84] [GATNextHop: A GAT for Shortest Path Routing with Cross-Topology Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23917)
*Chia-Hong Chou,Katerina Potika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GATNextHop模型，利用图注意力网络（GNN）近似最短路径路由计算，在合成图上训练并在真实ISP网络（Internet Topology Zoo）上评估其跨拓扑泛化能力，与传统Dijkstra算法进行精度、推理速度和泛化性能的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最短路径算法（如OSPF使用的Dijkstra SPF）虽然能提供精确路由，但每次网络拓扑变化都需要重新计算，在动态或大规模网络中扩展性受限，因此需要探索更高效、可泛化的路由学习方法。

Method: 构建GATNextHop模型，采用图注意力网络（Graph Attention Network）架构，在合成图上训练模型学习最短路径路由启发式，然后在真实ISP网络拓扑上进行评估，验证跨网络结构的迁移能力。

Result: 模型性能将在精度、推理速度和泛化能力三个维度进行评估，并与Dijkstra算法进行对比，以量化学习式路由与经典路由方法之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在验证GNN能否近似最短路径计算并在不同拓扑间泛化，为动态、大规模网络中高效路由计算提供新的可能性，同时明确学习式方法与传统精确算法各自的优劣。

Abstract: Common shortest-path algorithms, such as Dijkstra's (SPF), that OSPF uses, provide exact routing solutions but must be recomputed for each network topology, limiting scalability in dynamic or large-scale networks. This paper proposes the GATNextHop model to determine whether a Graph Neural Network, namely the Graph Attention Network, can approximate shortest paths and generalize across topologies. By training on synthetic graphs and evaluating on real-world Internet Service Provider networks from the Internet Topology Zoo, we aim to benchmark our model's ability to learn routing heuristics that transfer across network structures. Performance will be evaluated in terms of accuracy, inference speed, and generalization, comparing the GNN against Dijkstra's algorithm to quantify trade-offs between learned and classical routing approaches.

</details>


### [85] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为(DNN)²的新方法，通过特征值最大化程序在非唯一的乘子空间中搜索有效的全局最优性证书，使得严格更紧的DNN（双非负规划）松弛能够通过Burer-Monteiro分解在大规模ReLU神经网络验证中可扩展地求解，显著缩小了与精确CPP解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LP和SDP松弛用于ReLU神经网络验证时存在显著的松弛间隙，产生过于保守的安全保证。虽然CPP公式可以消除该间隙，但它是NP难的。其最便宜的易处理松弛DNN虽然比SDP更紧，但规模超出内点法的处理能力。BM分解虽已用于SDP验证，但由于DNN中额外的非负性约束导致对偶乘子不唯一，标准认证方法无法直接应用于DNN，因此缺少严格的DNN可扩展验证方法。

Method: 提出一个新颖的特征值最大化程序，用于在非唯一的乘子空间中搜索有效的全局最优性证书。该程序通过Burer-Monteiro分解处理DNN松弛问题，并在优化过程中同时求解对偶乘子以提供全局最优性的严格证明。

Result: 实验表明(DNN)²方法产生的界一致地比标准SDP方法更紧，通常接近精确解；当有效证书存在时，所提出的认证程序能确认全局最优性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为提供紧致、可认证且计算可扩展的神经网络验证保证迈出了关键一步，对于在安全关键的自主系统中部署神经网络控制器和感知模块具有重要意义。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [86] [MnemoDyn: Learning Resting State Dynamics from 40K FMRI sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23936)
*Sourav Pal,Viet Luong,Hoseok Lee,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu,Richard Davidson,Won Hwa Kim,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动力系统的静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）模型MnemoDyn，在约4万条rs-fMRI序列上训练，覆盖多种公开和许可数据集。与主流Transformer架构不同，该方法采用跨脑区分割的多分辨率时间建模，在计算效率、跨人群与扫描协议的泛化能力以及重建质量上均优于现有SOTA Transformer方法。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rs-fMRI建模方法大多基于Transformer骨干网络，但Transformer在计算效率和跨数据集泛化方面存在局限。研究者希望利用大规模公开rs-fMRI数据进行预训练，构建一个既高效又能广泛应用的高性能模型，以支持下游神经影像任务，尤其是小样本研究。

Method: 提出MnemoDyn模型，采用基于动力系统的方法对rs-fMRI序列建模。方法核心是跨脑区（parcellated brain regions）的多分辨率时间建模，而非Transformer架构。模型在约4万条来自多样公开和许可数据集的rs-fMRI序列上进行大规模预训练。

Result: MnemoDyn在计算效率上更具优势，在不同人群和扫描协议间展现出优异的泛化能力；在与当前SOTA基于Transformer的方法对比中，持续获得更好的重建质量；在小样本研究场景下也表现有效。

Conclusion: 大规模公开rs-fMRI预训练可以训练出高效且高性能的模型，适用于多种下游任务。这为神经影像研究——尤其是以rs-fMRI为常见成像模态、但样本量普遍有限的领域——提供了重要的方法和实践意义。

Abstract: We present a dynamical-systems based model for resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI), trained on a dataset of roughly 40K rs-fMRI sequences covering a wide variety of public and available-by-permission datasets. While most existing proposals use transformer backbones, we utilize multi-resolution temporal modeling of the dynamics across parcellated brain regions. We show that MnemoDyn is compute efficient and generalizes very well across diverse populations and scanning protocols. When benchmarked against current state-of-the-art transformer-based approaches, MnemoDyn consistently delivers superior reconstruction quality. Overall, we find that with such large-scale pre-training on (non-proprietary) rs-fMRI datasets, we get a highly performant model for various downstream tasks. Our results also provide evidence of the efficacy of the model on small sample size studies which has implications for neuroimaging studies at large where resting state fMRI is a commonly acquired imaging modality.

</details>


### [87] [CoDrift: Compositional Drifting for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23939)
*Xiewei Ni,Ruofeng Mei,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoDrift是一个基于"漂移模型"启发的组合式单步生成策略学习框架，将离线强化学习的多个目标（行为保持、高价值偏好）统一表示为动作空间中的运动场，并通过场组合将它们融合为一个统一的策略场，最终吸收进一个随机生成器中实现单次前向传播即可生成动作。在73个任务（OGBench和D4RL）的离线及离线到在线设置中均取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习本质上是一个多目标问题：策略既需要与固定数据集的行为支持保持一致（行为克隆），又需要优先选择高价值动作（价值优化）。传统方法通常以不同方式分别处理这两个目标，缺乏统一的框架来自然地组合这些异构的学习目标。

Method: CoDrift将每个学习目标重新表示为动作空间中的运动场（motion field），描述生成动作应如何移动。通过场组合（field composition）将三个目标级别的场合并为一个统一策略场：(1) 条件场（conditional field）保持状态依赖的行为结构；(2) 边际场（marginal field）跨状态池化动作以在单正样本场景下提供更稳定的生成信号；(3) 价值场（value field）将生成动作推向更高价值区域。组合后的场被吸收进一个随机生成器，部署时只需一次前向传播即可产生动作。

Result: 在OGBench和D4RL共73个任务上，分别在离线设置和离线到在线设置下进行评估。CoDrift与最先进方法相比表现良好，在两种设置下均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: CoDrift通过运动场组合的视角统一了离线强化学习中的多种异构目标，证明基于漂移模型的组合式方法能有效解决连续控制离线RL中的单正样本学习问题，并实现了简洁高效的单步生成部署。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning is intrinsically multi-objective: a policy must remain compatible with the behavioral support of a fixed dataset while preferentially selecting high-value actions. We recast these objectives in a common form by viewing each as an action-space motion field that specifies how generated actions should move. This perspective enables heterogeneous learning objectives to be combined directly through field composition. Inspired by drifting models, we propose CoDrift, a compositional framework for one-step generative policy learning. CoDrift combines three objective-level fields into a unified policy field. The conditional field preserves state-dependent behavioral structure, while the marginal field pools actions across states to provide a more stable generative signal in the single-positive-sample regime of continuous-control offline RL. The value field moves generated actions toward higher-value regions. The composed field is absorbed into a stochastic generator that produces an action with a single forward pass at deployment. We evaluate CoDrift on 73 tasks from OGBench and D4RL in both offline and offline-to-online settings. CoDrift compares favorably with state-of-the-art methods and achieves the best average rank in both settings.

</details>


### [88] [Low-Latency Activation-Regularized Sparse Neural Operators with Distillation Assistance Towards Real-Time Edge-Deployable Virtual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23987)
*William Howes,Farid Ahmed,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏激活ReLU（SAR）层作为单步替代方案，在不使用代理梯度训练的情况下促进激活稀疏性，并与事件驱动计算兼容。在基于trunk的NOMAD架构中，SAR相比VSN和LIF实现，在延迟-误差-能量（LEE）综合指标上提升超过五倍；通过合成知识蒸馏进一步将LEE分数降低两倍以上；并通过ReLU尖峰损失和图邻域阈值改进了VSN方法，在热交换器数据集上将L2误差降低两倍以上甚至近七倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟传感需要实时重建和预测时空物理，但传统计算和数据驱动方法在泛化、延迟和边缘部署能效方面存在挑战。神经算子虽具潜力但依赖高功耗硬件，而脉冲神经网络虽能提升效率，却面临代理梯度训练和多步脉冲带来的收敛与延迟问题。

Method: 提出Sparse-Activation-ReLU（SAR）层作为单步替代方案，促进激活稀疏性且无需代理梯度训练，兼容事件驱动计算；在trunk-based NOMAD架构中集成SAR；引入合成知识蒸馏；改进VSN采用ReLU-based尖峰损失和图邻域阈值化方法。

Result: 在LEE综合指标上，SAR相比VSN和LIF实现提升超五倍；合成知识蒸馏使LEE分数降低两倍以上；改进的VSN方法在热交换器数据集上将L2误差降低两倍以上甚至近七倍，同时减少尖峰和空间聚合。

Conclusion: 该工作为能效虚拟传感提供了一种替代框架，可定位于神经形态或边缘设备集成，为未来基于稀疏性或脑启发脉冲的高效设计提供了延迟、能量和误差性能的比较基准。

Abstract: Virtual sensing enables digital twins and safety-critical systems to reconstruct and forecast spatial-temporal physics in real time. However, conventional computational and data-driven methods often face challenges in generalization, latency, and energy efficiency for edge deployment. Neural operators offer a promising alternative but remain reliant on power-intensive hardware. Spiking neurons and neuromorphic computing can improve efficiency, yet surrogate-gradient training and multi-step spiking introduce convergence and latency challenges. We propose the Sparse-Activation-ReLU (SAR) layer, a single-step alternative that promotes activation sparsity without surrogate-gradient training while remaining compatible with event-based computing. Within a trunk-based NOMAD architecture, SAR achieves over a fivefold improvement in the combined Latency-Error-Energy (LEE) metric compared with Variable Spiking Neuron (VSN) and Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF) implementations. We further analyze spiking entropy and feature usage and introduce synthetic knowledge distillation, reducing the LEE score by more than twofold. Finally, we improve VSN through a ReLU-based spiking loss and graph-neighbor thresholding. On the Heat Exchanger dataset, these approaches reduce L2 error by more than twofold and nearly sevenfold, respectively, while reducing spiking and spatial aggregation. Overall, the work presented is a step towards energy-efficient virtual sensing by providing an alternative framework that can be positioned towards neuromorphic or other edge device integration that can be a gold standard to compare latency, energy, and error performance for future efficient designs that are sparsity or brain-inspired spiking based.

</details>


### [89] [PinSieve: Production Selective VLM Serving and a Governed Memory Flywheel for Enterprise Content-Quality Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24040)
*Chuqing Gao,Yuanfang Song,Jonathan Zhang,Yifan Wu,Vishwakarma Singh,Qinglong Zeng,Andrey Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PinSieve 是一个大规模内容质量管道的生产案例研究，其部署组件是一个选择性 VLM 服务代理，仅处理上游轻量模型无法解决的灰色地带切片，并暴露在线标量路由分数、保留受控的人工升级机制。该系统在灰色切片上过滤的非可操作项数量是先前模块的 2.05 倍，同时略微降低估计漏检率；推广后提升审查生产力 25.7%、降低归一化运营成本 16.2%，并将信号交付从次日提升至当日。论文还通过受治理的记忆飞轮研究维护过程，在选择性反馈下（升级项默认审查、自动通过项主要通过审计抽样标注），Feedback Memory 记录路由迹线、观察路径、审计倾向和重放元数据。数据策展代理采用有界提议-验证器循环，覆盖代表性、不确定性、时效性和新审查重放，并在批量接受前设置正率和分数分箱护栏。在六个月生产数据的连锁月度刷新中，该方法将平均 FNR@50% 从代表性随机重放下的 17.73% 降至 13.29%。推理审查代理审计教师生成的推理并支持保留/修复/删除决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的企业 AI 代理通常需要是有界的、有状态的、可观察的和可治理的，而不是完全自主的。论文旨在展示如何在真实大规模生产管道中实现这种受控的代理系统，并研究其维护与迭代机制。

Method: 提出 PinSieve 架构，包含：(1) 选择性 VLM 服务代理，仅处理灰色地带切片并暴露路由分数；(2) 治理记忆飞轮，在选择性反馈下记录路由迹线、观察路径、审计倾向和重放元数据；(3) 数据策展代理，采用有界提议-验证器循环，结合代表性、不确定性、时效性与新审查重放，并设置正率和分数分箱护栏；(4) 推理审查代理，审计教师生成推理并支持保留/修复/删除。

Result: 部署系统在灰色切片上过滤 2.05 倍更多非可操作项且略微降低误报率；推广后审查生产力提升 25.7%，归一化运营成本下降 16.2%，信号交付从次日变为当日。在六个月生产数据的月度刷新中，平均 FNR@50% 从 17.73% 降至 13.29%。该服务代理方案已推广至多个其他内部信号，显示可迁移性。

Conclusion: PinSieve 验证了有界、可观察、可治理的生产代理设计在真实大规模管道中的有效性。生产声明仅归于已部署的服务代理，重放和推理审查结果作为离线或抽样治理证据。该架构可迁移至多个内部信号任务。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents in production often need to be bounded, stateful, observable, and governable rather than fully autonomous. We present PinSieve, a production case study in a large-scale content-quality pipeline. Its deployed component is a selective vision-language-model (VLM) Serving Agent that operates only on the grey-zone slice left unresolved by lightweight upstream models, exposes a scalar routing score online, and preserves controlled human escalation. On this slice, the deployed system filters 2.05x more non-actionable items than the previous production module while slightly reducing estimated miss rate; after promotion, it improves review productivity by 25.7%, reduces normalized operating cost by 16.2%, and moves signal delivery from next-day to same-day. We then study maintenance through a governed memory flywheel under selective feedback, where escalated items are reviewed by default and auto-passed items are labeled mainly through audit sampling. Feedback Memory records routing traces, observation paths, audit propensities, and replay metadata for evaluation and debugging. The Data Curation Agent uses a bounded proposal-verifier loop over representative, uncertainty, recency, and fresh-review replay, with positive-rate and score-bin guardrails before batch acceptance. In chained monthly refresh over six months of production data, this design reduces average FNR@50% from 17.73% under representative random replay to 13.29%. A Reasoning Review Agent audits teacher-generated rationales and supports keep/repair/drop decisions. Production claims are attributed only to the deployed Serving Agent; replay and rationale-review results are offline or sampled-governance evidence. The same serving-agent recipe has been adopted to several additional internal signals, suggesting transferability beyond one task.

</details>


### [90] [XP-JEPA: Cross-Predictive Physics Grounding for Forecastable Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24044)
*Kehan Wen,Ziming Li,Siyuan Luo,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出XP-JEPA（跨预测JEPA）模型，通过将视觉潜在动力学与物理轨迹相结合，解决自预测模型中编码器与预测器联合优化导致潜在状态转移缺乏物理约束的问题。该方法分别编码视觉观察和物理状态，通过共享的动作条件预测器推进两者，并匹配预测与两种未来表征。训练后丢弃物理分支，部署时仅保留视觉模型。实验表明该方法将预测器滚动漂移从0.361降至0.104，平均控制成功率从53.6%提升至78.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自预测式潜在世界模型在规划时通过预测候选动作如何变换学习到的表征来运作，但编码器和预测器联合优化时可能共适应，产生容易预测但缺乏物理场景演化约束的潜在转移，导致潜在动力学与真实物理演化脱节，影响下游控制性能。

Method: 提出跨预测JEPA（XP-JEPA）框架：1) 分别编码视觉观察和物理状态两种模态；2) 两者通过共享的动作条件预测器向前推进；3) 将每个预测同时匹配到两种未来的表征上。这种目标函数促使两种模态间形成统一的潜在动力学，并锚定在底层物理转移之上。训练完成后物理分支被丢弃，部署时仅使用视觉模型。

Result: 在涵盖六个评估子族的多任务套件上，XP-JEPA将新拟合预测器的滚动漂移从0.361降低到0.104，平均控制成功率从53.6%提升到78.2%。对比实验表明，直接进行物理状态回归虽能提升位置可解码性，但预测性和控制性能仍接近视觉基线，说明跨预测式的物理锚定是提升潜在动力学期望性的关键。

Conclusion: 跨预测式物理锚定能够在测试时无需特权输入的情况下，产生更具可预测性的潜在动力学，从而显著提升基于滚动的控制性能。这验证了将物理轨迹作为训练时监督信号、在部署时丢弃的有效性。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by predicting how candidate actions transform learned representations. In self-predictive models, however, the encoder and predictor are optimized jointly and can co-adapt to latent transitions that are easy to predict but only weakly constrained by the physical evolution of the scene. We introduce the cross-predictive JEPA (XP-JEPA), which grounds visual latent dynamics in privileged physical trajectories. XP-JEPA separately encodes visual observations and physical states, advances both through a shared action-conditioned predictor, and matches each prediction to both future representations. This objective encourages unified latent dynamics across the two modalities, grounded in the underlying physical transitions. The physical branch is discarded after training, leaving a visual-only model at deployment. On a multi-task suite spanning six evaluation subfamilies, XP-JEPA reduces rollout drift of a newly fitted predictor from $0.361$ to $0.104$ and increases mean control success from $53.6\%$ to $78.2\%$. Direct physical-state regression raises position decodability but leaves forecastability and control near the visual-only baseline. Cross-predictive physical grounding can therefore produce more forecastable latent dynamics for rollout-based control without privileged inputs at test time.

</details>


### [91] [Physics-Integrated Operator Learning via Gaussian Splatting Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24049)
*Jihao Zhang,Junyi Guo,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前馈高斯溅射（FFGS）表示的物理集成方法，将连续场表示作为离散解场与物理算子之间的接口，无需引入物理残差损失即可将已知PDE算子直接整合到学习到的演化映射中。在2D和3D PDE系统上，该方法相比最强的纯数据驱动基线，在长时程自回归预测中将相对ℓ2误差降低1.5-2.2倍，并持续提升频谱保真度，且在物理方程部分已知时依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的神经算子替代模型在长时程自回归预测中会积累大量误差，且未能利用已知的控制方程结构。现有的物理融入方法主要依赖残差训练目标或PDE特定的架构约束，这两种方式分别带来了优化困难和架构通用性受限的问题。因此需要一种既能利用物理结构又不牺牲通用性和稳定性的集成方案。

Method: 提出FFGS（前馈高斯溅射）表示作为连续接口：将离散化解场重构为连续高斯场，并具备闭式空间导数，从而允许已知物理PDE算子直接嵌入学习到的演化映射中，而非通过物理残差损失来施加约束。该方法在演化映射内部直接整合物理算子，避免了额外的优化目标。

Result: 在2D和3D的多种PDE系统（平流、扩散、非线性自平流、反应动力学）上进行评估。长时程自回归rollout中，相比最强纯数据驱动基线，相对ℓ2误差降低1.5-2.2倍，频谱保真度持续改善。在控制方程部分已知时该方法依然有效，展现出对不完整物理信息的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 连续场表示可以作为将已知物理结构融入通用神经算子替代模型的实际有效接口。该方法避免了物理残差损失带来的优化困难，同时保持架构通用性，并在物理信息不完整时仍具鲁棒性，为物理-数据混合建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Neural operators provide efficient surrogates for spatiotemporal PDE systems, but purely data-driven formulations often accumulate substantial errors during long-horizon autoregressive prediction and may fail to exploit available governing-equation structure. Existing approaches incorporate physics primarily through residual-based training objectives or PDE-specific architectural constraints, which can introduce optimization difficulties or limit architectural generality. In this work, we introduce a representation-level approach to physics integration in which a feed-forward Gaussian splatting (FFGS) representation serves as a continuous interface between discretized solution fields and governing operators. The FFGS representation reconstructs the state as a continuous Gaussian field with closed-form spatial derivatives, allowing available physical PDE operators to be integrated directly within the learned evolution map without introducing a physics-residual loss. We evaluate the framework across two- and three-dimensional PDE systems, including advection, diffusion, nonlinear self-advection, and reaction dynamics. Over long-horizon autoregressive rollouts, the proposed framework reduces relative $\ell_2$ error by $1.5\times$--$2.2\times$ compared with the strongest purely data-driven baseline across the benchmark suite, while consistently improving spectral fidelity. The framework also remains effective when the governing equations are partially known, demonstrating robustness to incomplete physics. These results demonstrate that continuous field representations can provide a practical interface for incorporating known physical structure into generic neural-operator surrogates.

</details>


### [92] [ALPHABET: A Laplace-Pole History Aggregator with Banked Exponential Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24051)
*Daehwa Ko,JaeHyeon Kim,Oh Seong Kwon,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALPHABET，一个紧凑的线性时间序列模型，仅用数千参数即可保持竞争力，同时提供可审计的预测接口。它通过复数极点模式压缩时间历史，在82任务基准上达到平均排名3.97，且推理速度快5.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索序列模型是否能在极少数参数（数千规模）和显式可审计预测界面的约束下仍具竞争力，挑战大规模模型的必要性。

Method: ALPHABET模型架构包含三部分：一个直接合成器将模态状态映射回特征轨迹；一个独立的级联分析器对变换后的轨迹进行分析（无需重合成）；一个仿射头仅读取两个bank的模态能量和滞后矩。论文还从理论上刻画了该描述符保留的时间信息：每个模式能量是二阶频谱的频率局部化测量，连续测量可识别频谱，几乎每个模式能分离任意有限集合的频谱不同类别。

Result: 在匹配低滞后统计的高斯控制任务中，学习到的描述符接近贝叶斯最优（原始自协方差仍处于随机水平）。在固定的82任务注册表上，ALPHABET在十个家族完整比较中达到平均排名3.97。在D=64运行时锚点下，其6437个参数实现平均比九个基线快5.02倍的推理速度和3.93倍的完整训练步骤。

Conclusion: ALPHABET证明了紧凑模型不仅能与大规模序列模型竞争，还能提供可审计的预测接口，在效率和性能上均具显著优势。

Abstract: Can a sequence model remain competitive with only a few thousand parameters and an explicitly auditable prediction interface? We introduce ALPHABET, a compact linear-time model that compresses temporal history into stable complex pole modes: a direct bank synthesizes its modal states back into the feature trajectory, an independent cascaded bank analyzes the transformed trajectory without resynthesis, and an affine head reads only modal energies and lag moments from both banks. We characterize the temporal information this descriptor retains: for a stationary, fully observed feature process, each mode energy is a frequency-localized measurement of the second-order spectrum, the continuum of such measurements identifies the spectrum, and almost every mode separates any fixed finite set of spectrally distinct classes. On a Gaussian control with matched low-lag statistics, the learned descriptor approaches the Bayes oracle where raw autocovariances remain at chance. Across the fixed 82-task registry, ALPHABET attains mean rank 3.97 in the complete ten-family comparison. At the common-width D=64 runtime anchor, its 6,437 parameters deliver 5.02 times faster inference and 3.93 times faster complete training steps than the nine baselines on average.

</details>


### [93] [PhysicsBench: A Unified Leaderboard for Generative and Predictive Models in Engineering Design and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24056)
*Sang Won Lee,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhysicsBench是一个统一基准和排行榜，用于在标准化流程下评估生成式和预测式AI模型，涵盖1D/2D/3D领域的七个任务、九个数据集和66个模型，采用从S到XL的现实有限数据规模，并通过BenchRank（基于PageRank的去偏排名方法）进行排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式和预测式AI模型评估通常是孤立进行的，使用学术数据集、规模不受限制、指标和流程不一致，导致"最先进"的说法缺乏统一标准，难以公平比较和实际模型选择。

Method: 构建PhysicsBench统一基准，涵盖1D/2D/3D的七个生成与预测任务，在九个数据集（包括工业级CAD/CFD/FEA仿真）上扩展为28种配置，用S到XL的有限数据规模模拟现实场景；提出BenchRank方法，通过分布距离、字段/标量精度和工程专用有效性等指标捕捉质量，并利用PageRank在头对头支配图上对相关指标去偏排名，计算成本单独呈现在效率视图。

Result: 跨任务来看，架构的大规模学术排名对其小数据排名预测力很弱；七个任务中有六个的排名榜首随数据规模变化而变化，且没有模型能在超过一个任务中领先。

Conclusion: PhysicsBench将"state-of-the-art"从自我报告的主张转变为公开可用的模型选择基础，为工程设计与仿真领域的AI模型提供了标准化、可复现的评估方法。

Abstract: Generative and predictive artificial intelligence models are increasingly used to generate geometry and to predict physical fields and scalar quantities in engineering design and simulation. Yet these models are typically evaluated in isolation, on academic datasets at unconstrained scales, with inconsistent metrics and procedures. We present PhysicsBench, a unified benchmark and leaderboard that evaluates generative and predictive models under one standardized procedure. PhysicsBench spans seven generation and prediction tasks across 1D, 2D, and 3D domains and ranks 66 models on nine datasets, comprising industrial-scale CAD/CFD/FEA simulations and public references, expanded into 28 configurations. One procedure and ranking apply to both families, each ranked within its own tasks. Evaluation spans realistic, limited data scales from S to XL rather than the unlimited training sets common in academic benchmarks. A common metric suite captures geometric fidelity with distributional distances, physical-field and scalar accuracy, and engineering-specific field- and shape-validity. BenchRank debiases correlated metrics and ranks by PageRank over a head-to-head dominance graph, so every reported quality metric is also ranked, with computational cost in a separate efficiency view. Across tasks, an architecture's large-scale academic standing weakly predicts its small-data ranking. The top model changes with data scale in six of the seven tasks, and no model leads more than one task. PhysicsBench turns "state-of-the-art" from a self-reported claim into an openly published foundation for model selection.

</details>


### [94] [Mechanistic Circuit Identification for Controllable Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24065)
*Nakyung Lee,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电路（circuit）的框架，将训练动态数据估值与机械可解释性（MI）相结合，通过三个互补的效用轴（可学习性、挑战性、对齐性）来概念化数据质量，并利用模型内部电路作为可控接口来主动生成面向特定效用的数据，最终提出SAMS（阶段感知机制调度）方法，根据模型不断演化的优化需求来调度电路引导的数据，在多项选择QA任务上验证了该方法比基于提示的基线能产生更多样化、更精确控制的数据，并持续提升下游性能和校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据合成管线大多依赖基于启发式提示的黑盒控制范式，无法深入了解单个样本与模型底层学习动态之间的交互关系，缺乏对数据生成的精细理解和可控性。

Method: 提出电路接地（circuit-grounded）框架：1）沿可学习性、挑战性、对齐性三个效用轴概念化数据质量；2）发现模型中因果控制这些效用信号的内部专用电路；3）将这些电路作为可控接口，超越启发式提示，主动引导生成面向特定效用的数据；4）提出SAMS（阶段感知机制调度），根据模型演化中的优化需求对电路引导的数据进行调度。

Result: 在多项选择QA任务上的实验表明，该方法能生成比基于提示的基线更多样化且精确控制的数据，持续提升下游性能和校准效果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可解释数据生成的原则性白盒范式，开创了将机械可解释性不仅作为分析工具、更作为实用的可控接口来使用的先例。

Abstract: While recent advances in data synthesis aim to curate high-quality datasets, most generation pipelines still rely on heuristic prompt-based control. This black-box paradigm provides limited insight into how individual samples interact with a model's underlying learning dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose a circuit-grounded framework that connects training-dynamics-based data valuation with mechanistic interpretability (MI). Specifically, we conceptualize data quality along three complementary utility axes, learnability, challenge, and alignment. First, we uncover specialized model-internal circuits that causally govern these utility signals. Then, moving beyond heuristic prompting toward mechanistic control, we leverage these circuits as controllable interfaces, actively steering generation to produce utility-targeted data. Building on this capability, we introduce SAMS (Stage-Aware Mechanistic Scheduling), which schedules circuit-steered data according to the model's evolving optimization needs. Experiments on multiple-choice QA tasks demonstrate that our approach yields precisely controlled data with greater diversity than prompt-based baselines, consistently improving downstream performance and calibration. Ultimately, this work establishes a principled white-box paradigm for interpretable data generation, pioneering the use of MI not just as an analytical tool, but as a practical, controllable interface.

</details>


### [95] [A Feature-Major Codebook for Memory-Efficient Sparse-Binary Self-Organizing Maps: Scaling a MEDLINE Atlas to 1.05 Million Neurons on a Single Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24067)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于特征主序（feature-major）码本布局的SOM训练加速方法，将BMU搜索转化为分块稀疏-稠密乘积，无需牺牲精度即可将BMU搜索加速4.5-8.5倍，并在单GPU上实现了迄今最大的SOM（1024x1024，1,048,576个神经元），可在约72秒内完成2,990万篇MEDLINE文章的收敛训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组织映射（SOM）在MEDLINE等大规模语料上构建二维图谱具有实用价值，但训练的主导瓶颈——最佳匹配单元（BMU）搜索——受限于每轮需读取码本的带宽，使得大规模SOM训练不切实际。作者认为这一瓶颈主要是码本布局造成的伪命题，而非根本性限制。

Method: 将码本以特征主序存储（W[v.M+i]，每个特征的权值连续存放），将BMU搜索重构为分块稀疏-稠密矩阵乘积，使每个加载的权重列在样本块内被复用。仅改变布局，保持实现、精度和更新规则不变，即可显著加速BMU搜索。同时配合与半径无关的box-blur更新和基于收敛的停止准则。

Result: BMU搜索加速4.5-8.5倍；与cuSPARSE基线相比，量化误差相差不超过0.5%；在128x128和256x256时分别为1.5倍和2.6倍快，512x512时是唯一能在24GB显卡上运行的方案；在141GB H200上达到1,048,576个神经元（1024x1024），为迄今最大的SOM；匹配工作量下比MedSOM快约82倍，比最佳多核CPU库快621倍。

Conclusion: SOM训练的大规模瓶颈可通过码本布局优化有效消除，特征主序存储使BMU搜索变为可高度并行的分块乘积操作。在3个数量级的地图规模上，留出误差遵循无拐点的平滑幂律，表明分辨率的上限是计算资源而非数据本身的断点，为更大规模SOM提供了可行性。

Abstract: A self-organising map turns a large corpus into a browsable two-dimensional atlas, but building one at MEDLINE scale has been impractical: the best-matching-unit (BMU) search that dominates training is bound by the bandwidth needed to read the codebook every epoch. I show that this bottleneck is largely an artefact of codebook layout. Storing it feature-major with each feature's weights contiguous, W[v.M+i], recasts the search as a tiled sparse-dense product in which every loaded weight column is reused across a tile of samples. Varying only the layout, with implementation, precision and update rule held fixed, accelerates the BMU search by 4.5-8.5x. Because an exact-argmin BMU is invariant to how the codebook is stored, this gain costs nothing: held-out quantisation error agrees with a cuSPARSE baseline to within 0.5% at every map size. Against that baseline the advantage is a crossover rather than a constant: cuSPARSE.SOM is faster at small maps, SparseBin.SOM is 1.5x faster at 128x128 and 2.6x at 256x256, and at 512x512 it is the only one that runs at all on 24 GB. Paired with a radius-independent box-blur update and a convergence-based stopping rule, it trains a converged map over 29.9 million MEDLINE articles in about 72 s at 64x64 on one 24 GB GPU, and accommodates 262,144 neurons (512x512 edges) where every alternative algorithm I tested exceeds memory constraints. On a 141 GB H200 it reaches 1,048,576 neurons (1024x1024 edges) - to my knowledge the largest self-organising map yet reported. Held-out error follows a smooth power law with no elbow across three decades of map size, so the limit on resolution is compute rather than any breakpoint in the data. At matched work the design is ~82x faster than MedSOM, the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases and, at 128x128, 621x faster than the best available multicore-CPU library.

</details>


### [96] [The Sharp Tail of Uniform Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24098)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了均匀稳定性泛化界中一个长期悬而未决的空白：构建了一个确定性的 γ-均匀稳定学习问题，证明其尾部概率能够同时在所有置信水平 p 上达到 O(γp + L√(p/n)) 的泛化差距下界，从而证明了已有对数无关上界中的线性依赖 log(1/δ) 项是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的对数无关上界表明 γ-均匀稳定算法的泛化差距至多为 O(γlog(1/δ) + L√(log(1/δ)/n))，但此前没有任何有界损失学习算法能证明对 log(1/δ) 的线性依赖是可实现的。已有构造仅适用于辅助弱相关随机变量且其逐点范围随 n 增长；已有学习下界仅在常数概率下成立。因此需要构造一个真正意义上的学习算法来闭合这个差距。

Method: 构造一个普通的有界绝对损失回归问题，标签为常数。核心包括：(1) 多尺度罕见 Rademacher 特征集合；(2) 坐标级 ramp 函数保证 sup 范数意义上的稳定性；(3) 奇对称最大函数将唯一极端特征转换为量级为 γp 的差距而不违反损失界；(4) 几何间隔的 ramp 将所有置信水平融入同一问题。

Result: 对于每个 n、稳定性水平 γ 和损失界 L，构造了一个确定性 γ-均匀稳定学习问题，其尾部满足对 1≤p≤cn 同时成立的泛化差距下界：P(R(A_S)-R_S(A_S) ≥ c'·min{L, γp+L√(p/n)}) ≥ e^(-p)。这确定了均匀稳定性在高概率和矩意义下的最优依赖关系（紧至通用常数）。

Conclusion: 该构造与对数无关上界结合，完全确定了均匀稳定性在学习理论中的最优高概率和矩依赖，说明上界中 log(1/δ) 项是本质不可消除的，填补了理论与实践之间的长期缺口。

Abstract: Uniform stability controls how much one training example can change the loss at any test point. A new logarithmic-free upper bound shows that a $γ$-uniformly stable algorithm with loss in $[0,L]$ has generalization gap at most $O \left(γ\log(1/δ) +L\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/δ)}{n}}\right)$ with probability $1-δ$. Whether an actual bounded-loss learning algorithm can realize the linear dependence on $\log(1/δ)$ has remained open. The known construction realizes it only for auxiliary weakly dependent random variables whose pointwise range grows with $n$. The known learning lower bound holds only at constant probability. We close this gap. For every $n$, stability level $γ$, and loss bound $L$, we construct one deterministic $γ$-uniformly stable learning problem whose tail satisfies, simultaneously for $1\le p\le c n$, $\mathbb P \left( R(A_S)-R_S(A_S) \ge c'\min \left\{L,γp+L\sqrt{p/n}\right\} \right)\ge e^{-p}.$ The construction is ordinary bounded absolute-loss regression with constant labels. Its key is a multiscale collection of rare Rademacher features. A coordinatewise ramp is stable in sup norm, while an odd symmetrized maximum converts a unique extreme feature into a gap of order $γp$ without violating the loss bound. Geometrically spaced ramps put all confidence levels into the same problem. Together with the logarithmic-free upper bound, this determines the optimal high-probability and moment dependence of uniform stability up to universal constants.

</details>


### [97] [Structured Frequency-Domain Evidence for LLM-Based Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24113)
*Jungwook Seo,Sangwon Son,Minjeong Kim,Seungmin Han,Seojin Yoo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种证据增强的零样本时间序列异常检测（TSAD）框架，在保留索引去季节化观测数据的同时，利用FFT补充频率域证据（全局和局部两个分辨率），可增强LLM-based TSAD基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列异常检测方法主要依赖时域证据（如索引值、图表、去季节化表示），忽略了频谱结构信息。然而时间序列异常不仅表现为点异常，还可能体现为周期性偏移、局部振荡波动等频域特征变化，因此需要显式引入频率域证据来弥补这一缺失。

Method: 提出evidence-augmented零样本TSAD框架：1）保留索引去季节化的时域观测；2）使用FFT计算紧凑的频率域证据，分为全局频率域证据（汇总序列级周期上下文）和局部频率域证据（捕获时间局部频谱偏差）两个分辨率；3）将时域与频域证据结合输入给LLM进行零样本异常检测。

Result: 在AnomLLM框架上使用InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct、Gemini-2.5-Flash和GPT-4o等多个模型，并在TSB-AD-U子集上评估，结果表明显式的频率域证据能有效提升LLM-based TSAD基线的性能。

Conclusion: 频率域证据可以与时域输入（索引值和去季节化数据）互补，为零样本LLM-based TSAD提供更全面的异常检测依据。未来可将频域证据作为通用增强手段推广到更多TSAD场景。

Abstract: Time-series anomalies can appear not only as pointwise deviations but also as changes in recurring temporal structure, such as shifted periodicity or localized oscillatory fluctuations. However, existing LLM-based time-series anomaly detection methods mainly expose time-domain evidence through indexed values, plots, or de-seasonalized representations, leaving spectral structure implicit. We propose an evidence-augmented zero-shot TSAD framework that preserves indexed de-seasonalized observations while adding compact frequency-domain evidence computed with the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT). The evidence is constructed at two resolutions: global frequency-domain evidence summarizes sequence-level periodic context, while local frequency-domain evidence captures time-localized spectral departures. Experiments on AnomLLM with InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and GPT-4o, together with evaluation on the TSB-AD-U subset, show that explicit frequency-domain evidence improves LLM-based TSAD baselines. These results suggest that frequency-domain evidence can complement indexed and de-seasonalized time-domain inputs for zero-shot LLM-based TSAD.

</details>


### [98] [A mesh-free multiresolution deep energy method with phase-field modeling of brittle fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24126)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的网格无关离散化方法用于相场脆性断裂模拟。该方法使用单一神经网络同时表示位移场和相场，通过多分辨率C¹二次B样条特征编码控制分辨率，并结合分层蒙特卡洛积分（每次优化迭代重新采样积分点）来最小化增量能量。该方法在全球C¹光滑性下可统一处理二阶和四阶断裂能量密度，在六个算例中载荷-位移曲线与交错有限元参考结果吻合，且在随机多裂纹基准数据集上零样本分类准确率达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场脆性断裂建模需要足够密集的离散化来解析由正则化长度设定的局域化带，且裂纹路径未知。传统有限元方法需要预先知晓或自适应追踪裂纹路径，网格生成成本高。作者希望提出一种无需网格的离散化方案，避免网格对裂纹路径的约束。

Method: 1) 单一神经网络同时表示位移场和相场；2) 坐标通过多分辨率C¹二次B样条网格特征编码输入网络，使可表达的最细尺度由选择决定而非缓慢训练达到；3) 能量通过分层蒙特卡洛积分估计，积分点在每次优化迭代时重新采样；4) 全局C¹光滑性使二阶和四阶断裂能量密度可在相同离散化下运行。

Result: 在六个问题（从单边缺口拉伸/剪切到厚壁圆环）中，计算载荷-位移曲线与交错有限元参考吻合，单边缺口测试峰值载荷误差约1%，拓扑变化裂纹模式误差在8%以内。在公开随机多裂纹基准数据集的20次零样本运行中，90%种子裂纹的活跃/休眠状态被正确分类，而数据集作者的深度Ritz基线方法失败。

Conclusion: 积分的重新采样与多分辨率编码的配合是裂纹能否推进的关键——固定积分点或编码过粗都会导致裂纹无法扩展，而每个成分在另一个到位时都容忍广泛的参数设置。该方法为相场断裂模拟提供了一种无需网格、无需预知裂纹路径的替代方案，且在精度上可与传统交错有限元相媲美。

Abstract: Phase-field modeling of brittle fracture removes the need to track cracks explicitly by recasting their evolution as the minimization of an energy functional. In return it requires a discretization dense enough to resolve a localization band whose width is set by a regularization length and whose path is not known in advance. We propose a mesh-free discretization in which a single neural network represents the displacement and phase fields and is trained by minimizing the incremental energy directly. The coordinates enter the network through a multiresolution feature encoding built from $C^1$ quadratic B-spline grids, so the finest scale the representation can express is set by choice rather than reached through slow training, and the energy is estimated by stratified Monte Carlo integration on points redrawn at every optimizer iteration. This pairing proves critical, since the crack fails to advance both when the integration points are held fixed and when the encoding is too coarse to represent the band, while each ingredient tolerates a wide range of settings once the other is in place. Because the representation is globally $C^1$, the second- and the fourth-order fracture energy densities run on the identical discretization. Across six problems, from single-edge-notched tension and shear to a thick-walled ring on a single spline patch, the computed load-displacement curves follow staggered finite element references at matched regularization length, with peak loads within about 1% on the single-edge-notched tests and within 8% where the crack pattern changes topology. On a public benchmark dataset of random multi-crack configurations the method classifies the active or dormant state of 90% of the seeded cracks in twenty zero-shot runs, where the deep Ritz baseline of the dataset authors fails.

</details>


### [99] [From Gradient-Boosted Trees to Deep Recommenders: Practical Lessons from Migrating a Production Customer Support Recommender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24132)
*Sonia Sharma,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Shreya Rajpal,Swapnil Parekh,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文描述了将生产环境中的对话式推荐系统从梯度提升多分类模型迁移到成对二元深度推荐器的过程。通过将推荐问题重构为成对二元预测、采用负采样和噪声注入增强表示学习、使用注意力池化处理长对话上下文，系统在对话后期阶段超越了原有的CatBoost基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态捆绑、折扣耦合的产品目录正在取代传统的静态SKU，使传统的基于树的分类器面临挑战。这些分类器假设标签空间固定且缓慢变化，难以整合表格数据和对话记录等多模态信号，无法适应快速变化的服务业务需求。

Method: 将推荐问题重构为成对二元预测，联合学习用户和物品特征；通过负采样和噪声注入增强学习表示；采用注意力池化处理长对话上下文的转录块，并与TF-IDF和句子嵌入基线对比；探索多种架构（双塔模型、DeepFM及其变体）和损失函数（如对比损失）。

Result: 与CatBoost基线在所有对话阶段进行比较，结果表明该方案在对话开始阶段达到同等水平，在对话后期阶段表现更优。

Conclusion: 成对二元深度推荐器成功替代了传统的梯度提升树模型，在保持生产系统推荐质量的同时，更好地适应了动态目录和对话语境下的推荐需求。

Abstract: Product catalogs in fast-moving service businesses are shifting from static, independently priced SKUs toward dynamically bundled, discount-coupled offerings--a shift that strains the tree-based classifiers traditionally preferred for sparse and highly imbalanced data. These classifiers assume a fixed, slowly changing label space and struggle to incorporate multimodal signals such as tabular data and transcripts. We present the migration of a live, production conversational recommendation system from a gradient-boosted multiclass model to a pairwise-binary deep recommender. Because this system is critical to ecosystem growth initiatives and downstream features like dynamic pitching--surfacing the most relevant pitch text to a support agent in real time during a live customer conversation--maintaining live recommendation quality was a non-negotiable constraint. We detail the techniques that made this migration successful--reformulating recommendation as pairwise binary prediction to learn jointly from user and item features, and enhancing learned representations via negative sampling and noise injection. To efficiently incorporate long, live conversation context, we apply attention pooling over transcript chunks and benchmark it against TF-IDF and sentence-embedding baselines.
