Extensive experiments on this new dataset and the public MAVE benchmark show that HMGCLIP outperforms strong multimodal encoders, MLLMs, and e-commerce baselines, validating the superiority of HMGCLIP.

</details>


### [233] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Evolutionary Policy Optimization for Preference-Adjustable Heterogeneous Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24470)
*He Wang,Junyu Wu,Hui Li,Yanjie Song,Witold Pedrycz,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化策略优化的框架(RLOSMEA)来解决异构敏捷对地观测卫星(AEOS)调度问题，该框架通过赋值间接编码与解码器等价代价评估统一建模，并利用强化学习在高层选择搜索算子而非直接构造调度方案，实验证明其在加权效用和收敛稳定性上优于代表性元启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AEOS调度中不同卫星在轨道接入、机动能力和载荷资源上存在差异，同一任务在不同平台上具有不同的可行窗口、转移代价和资源消耗模式，导致统一建模和高效优化十分困难。

Method: 提出进化策略优化框架：建模层采用基于赋值（assignment）的间接编码结合解码器等价代价评估，保留卫星依赖约束的同时将任务增益、能量节省和负载均衡整合为可解释的标量效用；优化层将调度解码、种群搜索和在线actor-critic算子控制解耦，强化学习仅负责选择高层搜索算子；在此基础上开发RLOSMEA算法，协调全局探索、可行性恢复和局部精修。

Result: 在不同异构AEOS场景实验中，RLOSMEA相比代表性元启发式基线取得了更高的总体加权效用和更稳定的收敛；敏感性和学习行为分析进一步证实了方法的鲁棒性以及强化学习引导算子选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与元启发式搜索相结合、在高层引导算子选择而非直接构造调度，是一种解决异构AEOS调度问题的高效且稳健的途径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous agile Earth observation satellite (AEOS) scheduling requires task selection, satellite assignment, and observation sequencing under satellite-dependent visibility windows, attitude maneuvering requirements, energy consumption, and onboard storage constraints. Since satellites differ in orbital access, maneuvering capability, and payload resources, the same task may have different feasible windows, transition costs, and resource-consumption patterns on different platforms, which increases the difficulty of unified modeling and efficient optimization. To address this problem, this paper proposes an evolutionary policy optimization framework for heterogeneous AEOS scheduling with preference-adjustable weighted objectives. In the modeling layer, assignment-based indirect encoding is combined with decoder-based equivalent-cost evaluation to retain satellite-dependent constraints while integrating task gain, energy saving, and load balance into an interpretable scalar utility. In the optimization layer, schedule decoding, population-based search, and online actor-critic operator control are decoupled, so that reinforcement learning selects high-level search operators rather than constructing schedules directly. Based on this framework, a reinforcement-learning-assisted operator-selection memetic evolutionary algorithm (RLOSMEA) is developed to coordinate global exploration, feasibility recovery, and local refinement under a limited function-evaluation budget. Experiments on different heterogeneous AEOS scenarios show that RLOSMEA achieves higher overall weighted utility and more stable convergence than representative metaheuristic baselines. Sensitivity and learning-behavior analyses further confirm the robustness of the proposed method and the effectiveness of reinforcement-learning-guided operator selection.

</details>


### [234] [Implicit Q-learning-bootstrapped ant colony optimization for maritime moving-target observation scheduling with agile satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24471)
*He Wang,Junyu Wu,Yeye Liu,Yifan Zhou,Jie Zhang,Hui Li,Yanjie Song,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IQACO方法，将隐式Q学习嵌入蚁群优化算法，用于多卫星海洋移动目标观测调度问题，通过自适应调整信息素因子、启发式因子和蒸发率来控制搜索过程，在14个场景中均取得最优观测收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋移动目标观测调度是一个动态、序列相关的组合优化问题，海面目标持续移动导致可行观测窗口随时间变化，传统方法难以有效解决该复杂约束下的调度优化问题。

Method: IQACO将离线隐式Q学习模块嵌入构造式蚁群优化中，通过紧凑的搜索状态表征（包含信息素分布、当前及历史最优解质量、迭代进度），学习策略自适应地调节信息素因子、启发式因子和蒸发率，从而平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在14个不同规模和卫星配置的场景中，IQACO在每个场景中均取得最高平均观测收益，相比传统蚁群优化提升3.40%-9.40%，收敛速度更快，且在不同目标权重设置下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 离线值学习为约束下的海洋移动目标观测调度提供了一种有效的自适应搜索控制机制，证明了强化学习与元启发式算法结合在该问题上的可行性。

Abstract: Maritime moving-target observation scheduling with agile Earth observation satellites is a dynamic, sequence-dependent combinatorial optimization problem. Sea-surface targets move continuously, causing feasible observation windows to vary with target motion and satellite orbital geometry. The scheduler must jointly determine task selection, satellite assignment, observation-window selection, and observation ordering under time-window, attitude-maneuvering, onboard-resource, and cloud-affected availability constraints. This paper proposes an implicit Q-learning-bootstrapped ant colony optimization method, termed IQACO, for multi-satellite maritime moving-target observation scheduling. Rather than directly learning a task-selection policy, IQACO embeds an offline implicit Q-learning module into constructive ant colony optimization to adaptively adjust the pheromone factor, heuristic factor, and evaporation rate. A compact search-state representation captures pheromone distribution, current and historical-best solution quality, and iteration progress. During online scheduling, ant colony optimization constructs feasible observation sequences, while the learned policy regulates exploration and exploitation according to the current search state. Experiments on 14 scenarios with different scales and satellite configurations show that IQACO obtains the highest mean observation benefit in every scenario, improves the result of conventional ant colony optimization by 3.40\%--9.40\%, accelerates convergence, and remains stable under different objective-weight settings. These results demonstrate that offline value learning provides an effective adaptive search-control mechanism for constrained maritime moving-target observation scheduling.

</details>


### [235] [PeakBench: Benchmarking Resource-Aware Tool Invocation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24509)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Xu-Xiang Zhong,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PeakBench，一个可执行多工具工作流基准测试，包含基于执行验证的依赖标注和资源配置文件，并引入两阶段评估框架，将逻辑规划与物理调度分离，以诊断智能体在资源约束下的调度能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准主要评估工具选择、参数生成和端到端成功率，大多假设串行执行，忽视了并行执行和资源受限调度这一关键维度。这导致实际部署中出现两种失败模式：串行执行安全但缓慢，资源无关的并行执行快速但容易导致资源溢出。

Method: PeakBench包含可执行的多工具工作流，带有执行验证的依赖标注和测量的资源配置文件。评估框架采用两阶段分解：将逻辑规划（依赖规划）与物理调度（资源受限调度）分离，分别设计专用指标进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，强逻辑规划能力并不能可靠地转化为资源约束下的安全或高效执行；暴露资源信息可以减少可避免的资源溢出并改善资源利用率。

Conclusion: PeakBench可作为诊断资源感知智能体行为的有效测试平台，填补了现有基准在并行执行和资源调度评估方面的空白。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly solve tasks by invoking multiple tools, where parallel execution is essential for low latency but difficult to manage safely. Existing agent benchmarks primarily evaluate tool selection, argument generation, and end-to-end success under mostly serial execution, largely overlooking valid parallelization and resource-constrained scheduling. This missing scheduling dimension creates a practical failure mode: serial execution is safe but slow, while resource-agnostic parallel execution is fast but prone to avoidable resource overflows. To address this gap, we introduce PeakBench, a benchmark of executable multi-tool workflows with execution-grounded dependency annotations and measured resource profiles. A central challenge in evaluating such workflows is attribution: failures and inefficiencies may arise from incorrect dependency planning, poor resource-constrained scheduling, or both. PeakBench addresses this challenge with a two-part evaluation framework that disentangles logical planning from physical scheduling, with dedicated metrics for each dimension. Using this framework, we show that strong logical planning does not reliably translate into safe or efficient execution under resource constraints. We further show that exposing resource information can reduce avoidable overflows and improve resource utilization, making PeakBench a useful testbed for diagnosing resource-aware agent behavior. Code is available at https://github.com/Czzzk/Staggering-the-Peaks.

</details>


### [236] [Neurosymbolic Alignment for Physiologically-Safe Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24534)
*Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Erik Cambria,Milena Zivkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号对齐（Neurosymbolic Alignment）框架，将7B规模的临床大语言模型与基于HGNN的生理世界模型耦合，通过知识图谱上的稳态约束、路径合理性和药物相互作用惩罚来驱动偏好优化，使临床LLM的安全对齐指标显著提升（CSS从69.5%提升至90.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床大语言模型能生成看似合理但生理上不安全的推荐。现有的安全对齐主要依赖纯文本监督，缺乏结构化的生理学知识作为约束，导致模型在深层生理推理上存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出Neurosymbolic Alignment框架：1) 将7B临床LLM与基于HGNN的Physiological World Model耦合，后者建立在847K节点的生物医学知识图谱上；2) 使用稳态约束、多跳路径合理性、药物相互作用惩罚对候选响应评分；3) 评分排名驱动迭代on-policy ORPO更新。评估使用Clinical Safety Benchmark（CSB，2500个场景）。

Result: 相较ORPO：CSS从69.5%提升到90.8%（+21.3pp），医生评估HR从14.1%降至5.1%，DID从72.8%提升至91.6%。独立规则引擎验证RSS为86.4%（r=0.97一致性）。超出GPT-4（5-shot）所有安全指标，尽管参数量少10倍。在合成EHR噪声下保持84.2% CSS。消融显示HGNN评分（-16.2pp）和迭代训练（-11.5pp）是主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 训练阶段引入生理学知识的结构化约束能产生可测量且可独立验证的安全改进，但需要在真实临床数据上进行外部验证以确认训练场景中的收益能否迁移到实际部署环境。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs can generate recommendations that are factually plausible yet physiologically unsafe. We investigate whether safety alignment can be improved by grounding preference optimization in structured physiological knowledge rather than text-only supervision. Methods: We propose Neurosymbolic Alignment, a training-time framework that couples a 7B clinical LLM with an HGNN-based Physiological World Model over an 847K-node biomedical knowledge graph. Candidate responses are scored using homeostatic constraints, multi-hop path plausibility, and drug-interaction penalties, and the resulting rankings drive iterative on-policy ORPO updates. Evaluation is performed on the Clinical Safety Benchmark (CSB), a 2,500-scenario benchmark for physiological constraint violations in generative clinical reasoning. Results: Relative to ORPO, the proposed method improves CSS from 69.5% to 90.8% (+21.3 pp), reduces physician-evaluated HR from 14.1% to 5.1% on the blinded subset, and improves DID from 72.8% to 91.6%. These gains are corroborated by an HGNN-independent Rule-Engine Safety Score (RSS: 86.4%, +21.2 pp over ORPO; r=0.97 concordance with CSS). The method also exceeds GPT-4 (5-shot) on all safety metrics despite a 10x parameter disadvantage, and outperforms an inference-time self-correction pipeline (SFT+SelfCorrect) by 11.4 pp CSS. Under synthetic EHR-style noise, 84.2% CSS is retained. Ablation analysis shows that HGNN scoring (-16.2 pp) and iterative training (-11.5 pp) are the dominant contributors. PhysioScore calibration against 200 clinician labels yielded ECE = 0.038 and kappa = 0.91. Conclusion: Training-time physiological grounding produces measurable and independently verifiable safety improvements in open-weight clinical LLMs under controlled evaluation. External validation on real clinical data is required to determine whether these gains transfer to deployment settings

</details>


### [237] [Discovering Adaptive Transmission Programs for Collective Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24545)
*Cédric Colas,Jérémy Perez,Eleni Nisioti,Akhilesh Mocherla,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer,Clément Moulin-Frier,Maxime Derex*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用LLM引导的进化搜索来设计"状态感知"的传播协议（state-aware transmission protocols），这些协议能根据智能体和集体的状态条件化信息路由，在集体发现任务中将性能提升高达37%，且具备跨领域和跨群体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于群体智能中信息传播的研究主要聚焦于网络结构（谁与谁分享、何时分享），但网络是状态无关的（state-agnostic），无法根据智能体已知内容或集体状态来条件化传播。作者旨在弥合这一空白，将传播协议形式化为状态感知的程序。

Method: 1) 将传播协议形式化为"状态感知程序"，基于智能体状态和集体状态路由信息与资源；2) 使用LLM引导的进化搜索（LLM-guided evolutionary search）在集体发现任务中自动设计有效协议；3) 通过消融实验（去除内容依赖性但保留网络拓扑和时序）验证状态感知的作用。

Result: 进化出的协议相比文献中的标准基线将集体性能提升高达37%；消融实验证实状态感知（内容依赖性）是性能优势的关键驱动因素；进化出的协议能够跨领域变化和跨智能体群体转移。

Conclusion: 有效且可泛化的传播协议可以在硅基环境中被发现，这为AI辅助设计协调基础设施、增强人类集体智能开辟了道路。

Abstract: Human collective intelligence depends on transmission processes: who shares what with whom, how, and when. While these processes emerge from individual cognition, they can also be directed by deliberate top-down protocols. Prior work has studied how transmission shapes collective outcomes primarily through the lens of network structure, varying who shares with whom and when. But networks are state-agnostic: they cannot condition transmission on what agents know or on the state of the collective. Here, we formalize transmission protocols as state-aware programs that route information and resources based on agent and collective states, and we use LLM-guided evolutionary search to design effective protocols in a collective discovery task. Evolved protocols increase collective performance over standard baselines from the literature by up to 37%. Ablations confirm that state-awareness drives this advantage: removing content-dependence while preserving network topology and timing eliminates performance gains. We find that evolved protocols also transfer across domain variations and agent populations. These results demonstrate that effective and generalizable transmission protocols can be discovered in silico, suggesting a path toward AI-assisted design of coordination infrastructure that enhances human collective intelligence.

</details>


### [238] [When "Must" Becomes "Maybe": Constraint Weakening in LLM Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24569)
*Yiheng Sun,Huifei Wang,Yancheng Zhu,Zhenyu Li,Zebin Zhao,Yifan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型智能体在多方协作流程中，中间语言产物（如摘要、备忘录等）如何保留"操作性状态"（operational state）而非仅仅是主题内容。通过1296个受控合成实验，发现压缩等交接转换会导致安全阻断器从约束性要求退化为非约束性信息，造成状态传递失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多角色多阶段的流程中，上游状态通过中间语言产物（摘要、计划、备忘录等）传递给下游组件。现有研究只关注主题性保留（topical retention），但忽略了"操作性状态保留"（operational state preservation）——即状态不仅需要被提及，还必须继续约束下游的行动。安全阻断器（safety blockers）提供了一个理想的受控场景来研究这一问题。

Method: 研究设计了一个受控实验框架：以安全阻断器为实例，其每个源状态包含四个关键字段（先决条件、权限、回退、执行后果）。实验条件包括：(1)确定上游识别正确；(2)变换不同的交接转换方式（如压缩、计划同化、收敛、所有权延迟、先例替换等）；(3)评估仅依赖所生成产物的执行器是否按状态约束行动。共进行1296个受控合成实验。

Result: 直接交接对照组保留了全部阻断器；正常的交接压缩导致100.0%的阻断器失活和54.2%的违禁动作；恢复全部四个状态字段可将保留率提升至100.0%，违禁动作降至0.0%；固定产物干预可进一步区分保留与遏制——下游验证消除了违禁动作，但产物失活率仍为95.3%。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一种存在于信息提取与行动之间的状态传递失败模式：交接转换可以在保留状态内容的同时削弱其下游行动的约束力。语义可用性（semantic availability）不能保证操作性保留（operational preservation），这对LLM智能体的安全关键应用具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents coordinate complex tasks through multi-role and multi-stage workflows. Upstream state is repeatedly transformed into intermediate language artifacts, such as summaries, plans, tickets, memories, and handoff notes, from which downstream components act. For action-constraining state, topical retention is insufficient: an artifact may mention an unresolved condition while changing it from a requirement that must be resolved before execution into information that may merely inform the next action. We study this action-binding role as operational state preservation. Safety blockers provide a controlled instance because each source state has an explicit prerequisite, authority, fallback, and execution consequence. We condition on correct upstream identification, vary the handoff transformation, and evaluate an executor restricted to the resulting artifact. Across 1,296 controlled synthetic episodes, direct-handoff controls preserve every blocker, whereas compression, plan assimilation, convergence, ownership deferral, and precedent substitution repeatedly turn binding state into caveats or non-binding considerations. Normal handoff compression produces 100.0% deactivation and 54.2% forbidden action. Restoring all four state fields raises preservation to 100.0% and reduces forbidden action to 0.0%. Fixed-artifact interventions further separate preservation from containment: downstream verification eliminates forbidden action while artifact deactivation remains 95.3%. These results identify a state-transmission failure between information extraction and action. Handoff transformations can retain state content while weakening its constraints on downstream action. Semantic availability does not guarantee operational preservation.

</details>


### [239] [EviDx: Evidence-Aware Active Diagnosis with Scaffolded LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24570)
*Lihang Zeng,Shaoting Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviDx是一个证据感知的主动诊断框架，通过构建患者特定的诊断环境、临床诊断脚手架和观察者引导的运行时机制，将LLM诊断从静态的病例到答案预测转变为动态的证据获取过程，实验表明其提升了诊断性能和流程稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗诊断系统大多将诊断视为静态的"病例到答案"预测任务，缺乏对证据获取过程的支持；而智能体LLM虽然提供了动态替代方案，但现有系统对患者证据如何暴露、搭建和控制缺乏明确规范。

Method: EviDx框架包含三个核心组件：1) E-Synthesis从原始临床病例构建交互式诊断环境；2) 临床诊断脚手架组织角色专业化智能体、证据工具和不断演化的证据状态；3) 观察者引导的运行时机制通过追踪不确定性和证据覆盖率来调控诊断终止。评估采用3级金字塔评估体系涵盖执行鲁棒性、推理动态和诊断结果。

Result: 实验表明EviDx改善了诊断性能和流程稳定性，同时揭示了模型依赖的能力边界。

Conclusion: EviDx证明了将主动证据获取纳入LLM诊断系统的可行性，但不同模型的能力边界仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is an active evidence-seeking process in which clinicians acquire evidence, update competing hypotheses, and decide when the available evidence is sufficient for diagnosis. Yet many medical diagnosis systems built around large language models (LLMs) still formulate diagnosis as static case-to-answer prediction, with limited support for evidence acquisition. Agentic LLMs offer a dynamic alternative through tool use and intermediate diagnostic trajectories, but existing systems often under-specify how patient evidence should be exposed, scaffolded, and controlled at runtime. We introduce EviDx, an evidence-aware active diagnosis framework that pairs patient-specific diagnostic environments with a clinical diagnostic scaffold and an observer-guided runtime harness. In EviDx, $\mathcal{E}$-Synthesis constructs interactive environments from raw clinical cases; the scaffold organizes role-specialized agents, evidence tools, and evolving evidence states; and the harness regulates diagnostic termination by tracking uncertainty and evidence coverage. A 3-level evaluation pyramid assesses execution robustness, reasoning dynamics, and diagnostic outcomes. Experiments show that EviDx improves diagnostic performance and process stability while revealing model-dependent capability boundaries.

</details>


### [240] [Joint Optimization of Tool Creation and Use for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24571)
*Zhi Rui Tam,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Shao-Hua Sun,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SMITH框架，通过强化学习在单一策略内联合训练工具创建与工具使用，使4B模型在13个程序推理任务中达到79.8%宏平均准确率，超越30B模型，并跨域泛化至视觉和表格任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强语言模型受限于人类编写的API，工具创建系统将创建工具和使用工具的模型解耦，导致写工具的模型不知道产生的schema是否可用。缺乏信号让模型生成的工具schema能被自己调用。

Method: 提出SMITH（Schema-grounded Multi-task Iterative Tool Honing）强化学习框架，在单一策略内联合训练工具创建和工具使用。每次rollout为构建任务（从少量示例写工具）或使用任务（在held-out问题中调用工具池工具）。三个独立奖励轴分别捕获schema、代码和结果失败，使每种失败模式贡献独立梯度。

Result: 4B Qwen3在13个程序推理任务（含精确验证器）上达到79.8%宏平均准确率，超越所有评估方法，领先未训练的30B-A3B工具编写器。TabMWP-Hard达40.4，跨域GQA达42.6（比同骨干推理时基线高7.6），无需视觉或表格训练数据。该4B模型写的工具还能提升LFM-2.5-350M和Qwen3-30B-A3B在相同推理任务上的表现。

Conclusion: SMITH证明联合训练工具创建与工具使用能显著提升小模型能力，使其在工具生成上超越大模型，且生成的工具具有跨模型与跨域的泛化价值。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language models are bounded by the APIs humans bothered to write; existing tool-creation systems patch this by prompting a frozen LLM at inference time, leaving the model that writes a tool decoupled from the one that uses it, with no signal that the schemas it produces are schemas it can invoke. We propose SMITH (Schema-grounded Multi-task Iterative Tool Honing), a reinforcement learning framework that jointly trains tool creation and tool use inside a single policy. Each rollout is either a build task (write a tool from a few examples) or a use task (invoke a pooled tool on a held-out question). Three separate reward axes catch schema, code, and outcome failures independently, so each failure mode contributes its own gradient. A 4B Qwen3 trained with SMITH on 13 procedural reasoning tasks with exact verifiers reaches 79.8 macro-average accuracy on held-out tasks, the best across all evaluated methods and ahead of an untrained 30B-A3B tool-writer. It also reaches 40.4 on TabMWP-Hard and 42.6 on out-of-domain GQA (+7.6 over the best same-backbone inference-time baseline), without any visual or tabular training data. Tools written by our 4B models also lifted the performance of LFM-2.5-350M and Qwen3-30B-A3B under same reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [241] [PhysMLLMs: Spatial Priors for Unified Referring Segmentation and Grounded Reasoning of Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24574)
*Siyao Yan,Bo Han,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Shude Wang,Hong Peng,Yulan Guo,Jianhuang Lai,Bin Hu,Tat-SengChua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhysMLLMs提出了一种训练阶段的先验注入架构，通过将视频多模态大语言模型的学生全局视觉表征与冻结的DINOv2教师模型对齐（REPA-Global机制），注入物理启发的空间连续性先验，从而改善视频分割的时空一致性问题（如抖动、漂移和身份切换），且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大语言模型在语言引导的视频分割中常出现时空不一致问题（抖动、漂移、身份切换），尤其在目标部分遮挡或附近有相似物体时更为严重。作者认为根本原因是当前训练缺乏显式空间先验，难以在时间上维持稳定的空间身份和形状。

Method: 提出PhysMLLMs架构，核心机制为全局表征先验对齐（REPA-Global）：在训练阶段，通过离线嵌入缓存和调度蒸馏计划，将学生的全局视觉表征与冻结的DINOv2教师模型进行知识蒸馏对齐，注入物理启发的空间连续性先验。推理阶段架构不变，无额外推理时间开销。

Result: 在多个视频基准上，PhysMLLMs提升了视频分割掩码质量和跨帧一致性，在小目标、快速运动、遮挡、干扰物和推理查询等困难场景下提升最大。在单帧指代图像分割和通用VLM基准上保持相当性能，说明注入空间先验不损害图像级接地能力和通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: 物理启发的空间先验注入能够在不牺牲通用能力的前提下提升视频时间稳定性，为Video MLLMs提供了一种有效的训练阶段改进方案。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models support language guided video segmentation, but they often show spatio temporal inconsistencies, e.g., jitter, drift, and identity switches. These failures are more common when targets are partly hidden or when similar objects appear nearby.One likely reason is that current training lacks explicit spatial priors, which makes it difficult to maintain stable spatial identity and shape over time. We present PhysMLLMs, a training-stage prior injection architecture that injects physics-inspired spatial continuity priors into Video MLLMs. PhysMLLMs is designed to encourage more stable object-centered representations by aligning the student global visual representation with a frozen teacher model during training. Our core mechanism, Global Representation Prior Alignment (REPA-Global), distills global visual representations from a frozen DINOv2 teacher using an offline embedding cache and a scheduled distillation plan. This design keeps inference unchanged and does not add inference time cost. Across multiple video benchmarks, PhysMLLMs improves video segmentation mask quality and cross-frame consistency, with larger gains on challenging cases involving small targets, fast motion, occlusion, distractors, and reasoning queries. On single-frame referring image segmentation and representative general VLM benchmarks, PhysMLLMs maintains comparable performance, demonstrating that the injected spatial prior improves video consistency without compromising image-level grounding or general multimodal capability. These results suggest that physics-inspired spatial prior injection can improve temporal stability while preserving general capability. The code is available at https://github.com/tusu-code/20260121-icml2026-2.git.

</details>


### [242] [Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding: Solvability, Complexity, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24585)
*Andrea Di Nezza,Mihir Patel,Fabio Fagnani,Sara Bernardini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PS-MAPF（Pivot-and-Station多智能体路径规划），一种新的MAPF变体：部分有任务的智能体必须先访问一组可互换的枢轴点（如工作台），然后整个车队停靠在匿名站点。作者完整刻画了可解性条件，证明了最小化站makespan或流时间的NP难性，并提出三种算法（完整基线、SAT最优求解器、Pivot-Prioritized Planning），最后一种能以数量级更优的性能解决74-89%的基准实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化高密度存储系统（仓库、机器人停车、工厂物流等）需要智能体车队在稀缺的关键资源间移动，然后停车而不阻碍后续操作。现有MAPF框架无法同时处理"中间访问枢轴点"和"最终匿名停靠"这两种混合约束，因此需要新的变体来建模和求解这类实际问题。

Method: 1) 对PS-MAPF进行形式化定义；2) 从图论角度完整刻画可解性：2-边连通图上所有实例可解，任意连通图上给出基于有效距离与空闲顶点数量的充要条件；3) 证明单枢轴情况下最小化站makespan或流时间即为NP难；4) 提出三种算法：完整基线算法、基于SAT的最优求解器、以及启发式的Pivot-Prioritized Planning (PPP)算法。

Result: PPP算法在基准测试中解决了74-89%的实例，其makespan和流时间比基线算法低数个数量级；同时给出了完整的可解性判定条件，并证明了优化目标的NP难性。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上完整刻画了PS-MAPF的可解性，确立了优化问题的计算复杂度下界，并在实践中验证了所提算法的高效性，为自动化存储系统的调度与路径规划提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Automated high-density storage systems (warehouses, robotic parking, plant logistics, etc.) require fleets of agents to move through scarce task-critical resources and then park without obstructing future operations. We introduce Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding (PS-MAPF), a MAPF variant in which a subset of tasked agents must each visit one of a set of interchangeable pivots (e.g., workstations) before the entire fleet terminates at anonymous stations, one agent per station. We characterize solvability completely: every instance on a 2-edge-connected graph is solvable, and, on arbitrary connected graphs, a structural effective-distance measure relative to the number of unoccupied vertices gives a necessary and sufficient condition. We prove that minimizing station-makespan or station-flowtime is NP-hard already with a single pivot. We present three algorithms, a complete baseline, a SAT-based optimal solver, and Pivot-Prioritized Planning (PPP), the last solving 74-89% of benchmark instances with makespan and flowtime orders of magnitude below the baseline.

</details>


### [243] [Causal Modelling of Support Interventions for Student Competency Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24632)
*Francesca Mangili,Alessandro Antonucci,Rafael Cabañas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文倡导将教育评估从传统的概率心理测量模型（如项目反应理论）转向结构因果建模框架，提出相应的构建协议，支持干预与反事实推理，并用真实评估数据（测量学生算法技能）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教育评估基于项目反应理论等概率模型，仅支持关联性推理和信念更新，无法回答干预性（如提供提示）和反事实（如"如果…会怎样"）问题，限制了教育者识别需求和设计干预的能力。

Method: 提出结构因果建模（SCM）的教育评估协议，要求专家提供结构方程的结构性信息（纯逻辑，无需概率假设），显式建模干预（如提示）并支持反事实场景分析，超越了标准关联模型的推理能力。

Result: 以测量义务教育阶段学生算法技能的复杂任务评估数据为例，展示了该协议的实际可行性，证明其能够提供传统概率模型无法实现的干预与反事实推理。

Conclusion: 结构因果建模为教育评估提供了更丰富的推理框架，尽管需专家提供结构方程，但所需信息纯逻辑化、假设更弱，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of student competencies is essential for enabling educators to identify individual needs, design targeted interventions, and evaluate the effectiveness of educational strategies. Empirical assessment procedures are typically grounded in psychometric models, such as item response theory, which relate student competence levels to performance on assessment tasks. In this paper, we advocate adopting a structural causal modelling approach to educational assessment, moving beyond probabilistic belief updating toward a framework that explicitly supports interventional and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a corresponding protocol for its construction and analyse the practical relevance of forms of reasoning that remain inaccessible to standard associative models, including the explicit modelling of interventions such as hints and the related counterfactual scenario analysis. Although our protocol requires the structural equations to be elicited from experts, the necessary information is purely logical and does not rely on probabilistic, less tenable assumptions. We illustrate the approach using data from an assessment that employs complex tasks designed to measure compulsory school student algorithmic skills.

</details>


### [244] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Parason，一种同时利用Subtask Parallelism和Trial Parallelism两种形式并行性的LLM推理加速框架。通过将串行推理轨迹转换为结构化并行轨迹，并采用PA-GRPO训练，在保持精度的同时实现约1.7倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码在推理时串行执行长推理链，导致困难任务时延严重（可达数天甚至数周）。虽然并行推理是自然解决方案，但现有系统只关注Subtask Parallelism（子任务分解），忽略了Trial Parallelism（多次推测性尝试并行探索、验证和聚合竞争假设）这一更普遍的并行形式。

Method: 1) 分析发现Trial Parallelism占可并行化推理计算的大部分（在DeepSeek-V4的HLE推理步骤中占65.5%），且在困难问题上占比更高；2) 基于此分类，用上下文无关文法将串行推理轨迹转换为结构化并行轨迹；3) 提出并行感知的组相对策略优化（PA-GRPO），其奖励函数联合平衡准确率、时延和两种并行比率；4) 推理时通过工具调用执行学习到的并行结构，将理论节省转化为实际墙钟加速。

Result: 在AIME24和AIME25等数学推理基准上，Parason在保持竞争性准确率的同时实现约1.7倍平均加速。

Conclusion: Parason证明了同时利用Subtask和Trial两种并行性可以显著提升LLM推理速度而几乎不损失精度，揭示了Trial Parallelism是推理并行化的主导形式，为未来的推理加速研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [245] [Lifted Model Construction under Approximate Commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24713)
*Malte Luttermann,Jan Speller,Tanya Braun,Marcel Gehrke,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-可交换性概念，放宽了提升推理中可交换因子的严格条件，允许近似可交换的因子参与提升模型构建和推理，并证明了由此引入的近似误差的严格界限，在保证查询精度的同时显著降低运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升推理算法依赖于识别可交换因子（其输出值在输入排列下不变），但真实数据中学到的参数即使关联对象不可区分也必然存在偏差，导致因子只能近似可交换而非严格可交换，阻碍了提升表示的构建。

Method: 引入ε-可交换性概念，作为可交换性的松弛形式（输出值在输入排列下近似不变）。展示了如何利用ε-可交换性进行提升模型构建和下游概率推理，并证明了由此产生的近似误差的严格上界。

Result: 理论保证得到实证验证，在运行时更低的情况下实现了可比的查询精度。

Conclusion: 通过ε-可交换性，即使在参数不完全对称的现实场景中也能进行提升模型构建，在保持高查询精度的同时确保实际可用性，兼顾了效率与准确性。

Abstract: Lifted inference algorithms enable scalable probabilistic inference even for large object domains by leveraging the indistinguishability of objects in a probability distribution. An essential prerequisite for constructing a lifted representation is to identify commutative factors, i.e., functions whose output values are invariant under permutations of a subset of their input values, in a potential-based factorisation. In practice, however, parameters learned from data inevitably deviate even if associated objects are indistinguishable, causing their corresponding factors to be only approximately commutative instead of being exactly commutative. We address this problem by introducing the concept of ε-commutativity, a relaxation of commutativity where output values are only approximately invariant under permutations of input values. Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [246] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RACE（残差对齐一致性估计）框架，一种前向传播统计方法，用于评估Transformer神经元在跨整个领域的功能性一致性。相比梯度方法，RACE在领域特异性上更优，计算开销低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机制可解释性中，发现跨整个领域的稳定神经元行为仍具挑战。现有方法依赖实例级点估计或高计算成本流程，前者掩盖群体级变异性，后者限制大规模领域分析的可扩展性。

Method: RACE（Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation）是一种前向传播统计框架，通过评估Transformer神经元的领域级功能一致性来工作。它利用残差对齐机制，无需反向传播梯度即可完成一致性估计。

Result: 扰动实验表明RACE相比基于梯度的方法具有更优的领域特异性；token分布级结果验证了所选神经元与目标领域之间的关联性；计算开销比梯度方法低两个数量级。

Conclusion: RACE提供了一种高效且可扩展的领域级神经元分析方案，克服了现有方法在群体变异性捕捉与计算成本之间的权衡，为大规模机制可解释性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [247] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AtlasNav，一个持久多视图语料库导航框架，通过一次性组织语料库为"语料库地图集"（Corpus Atlas），让每个查询自适应导航而非重构共享结构，在BrowseComp-Plus上达到92.05%的严格准确率，并将在线推理成本降低30.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCI（直接语料库交互）范式下，大规模智能体搜索存在"证据盲区"（Evidence Blindness）问题：有限交互预算下，所需证据可能无法被表面化、支持文档未被打开、或已打开文档未暴露关键片段。传统动态工作区方法从每个查询中在线重构交互空间，效率低下且缺乏可复用结构。

Method: 提出AtlasNav框架：保持直接语料库交互，但将语料库一次性组织成持久的多视图"语料库地图集"（Corpus Atlas），使每个查询能自适应导航而非重复重构共享结构。引入阶段式证据实现（stage-wise evidence realization）来量化证据盲区。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上达到92.05%的严格准确率，在线推理成本相对先前动态工作区SOTA降低30.21%；在匹配预算下更早实现完整所需证据，并更快接近同模型的证据供给经验参考值。在PhantomWiki的不同语料库组织及10K-1M受控扩展下依然有效，并成功迁移至异构企业知识库。

Conclusion: 智能体搜索不仅取决于证据的可达性，更取决于语料库的表示方式——有限的交互应成为有效的导航，而非重构。持久化语料结构比在线动态重构更高效、更有效。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [248] [StepGuard: Learning Step-Level Guardrails with Scalable Supervision and Safety-Utility Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24777)
*Zhijie Zheng,Yu Li,Chen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Lu Sheng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StepGuard，一个步骤级防护模型，可在工具执行前审计智能体轨迹并检查动作。通过StepGen自动生成安全/不安全轨迹，配合Balance-GRPO平衡训练，平均准确率高于开源防护模型，与GPT-5.4相当，降低攻击成功率77.3%而效用仅损失2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体通过工具调用与环境交互，带来文件修改、信息泄露和未授权操作等安全风险。现有防护措施大多在完整轨迹执行后评估，缺乏对步骤级动作的预执行监控。

Method: 1) 提出StepGuard步骤级防护模型，既能审计已完成轨迹，也能在动作执行前检查。2) 设计StepGen自动数据引擎，生成具有相同上下文但风险步骤动作不同的安全/不安全轨迹。3) 提出Balance-GRPO训练方法，根据观测准确率动态平衡安全与不安全动作的学习。

Result: StepGuard在开放权重防护模型中平均准确率最高，性能与GPT-5.4相当。在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上作为智能体防护时，平均攻击成功率相对无防护设置降低77.3%，平均效用仅下降2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 步骤级预执行防护是可行的，StepGuard在显著降低安全风险的同时保持极小的效用损失，为LLM智能体安全防护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can interact with external environments through tool invocation, but this capability also introduces security risks such as file modification, information leakage, and unauthorized actions. Existing guardrails often evaluate completed trajectories, leaving pre-execution monitoring of step-level actions underexplored. We propose StepGuard, a step-level guard model that can audit completed agent trajectories and check tool actions before they are executed. To train StepGuard, we introduce StepGen, an automatic data engine that generates safe and unsafe trajectories with the same context but different actions at the risky step. To further reduce over-defense and under-defense, we propose Balance-GRPO, which dynamically balances learning between safe and unsafe actions based on their observed accuracy. Experiments show that StepGuard achieves the highest average accuracy among open-weight guard models, with performance comparable to GPT-5.4. When used to guard agents on AgentDojo and AgentDyn, StepGuard reduces mean attack success rate by 77.3% relative to the no-guard setting, while mean utility drops by only 2.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [249] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套医学思维链（CoT）忠实性审计框架，通过30种临床驱动的扰动算子（如严重性反转、否定翻转、人口统计学交换、证据删减）同时对链条和问题进行编辑，并结合链条更新与答案翻转的联合分析，对14个LLM在4个医学QA基准上进行测试。结果显示Chain-Decoupling Rate（CDR）高达72.9%，链条损坏不影响准确率，移除CoT提示也不降低准确率，表明医学CoT在很大程度上与最终答案"解耦"，更像文档记录而非真正的推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生将CoT推理链视为医学推理的证据，但现有通用领域的CoT忠实性探测方法忽略了临床成本，医学LLM评估则把推理链当作黑箱。作者旨在弥合这一差距，提供可量化的医学CoT忠实性审计工具。

Method: 1）构建30个临床动机扰动算子，同时对问题和CoT进行编辑；2）提出Chain-Decoupling Rate（CDR）指标，衡量链条未注册编辑且答案未翻转的比例；3）采用链条更新-答案翻转联合分析对模型失败模式分类；4）对14个LLM在4个医学QA基准上测试；5）由两名认证临床医生对197个扰动问题进行重新标注验证。

Result: 面板整体CDR为72.9%（针对临床上有意义的破坏性编辑）；链条损坏后准确率无变化；移除CoT提示不降低准确率；98.5%的扰动问题金标准仍可辩护；该模式在医学/推理微调及模型规模上均成立；闭源模型的答案侧信号与解耦现象一致。

Conclusion: 医学CoT推理链在很大程度上与模型预测解耦——模型并未真正依赖可见推理链做出决策，链条更多扮演"文档记录"角色而非推理证据。CDR可作为审计医学CoT忠实性的可复用标准，对临床应用中依赖LLM推理链条的可信度提出警示。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [250] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAFE框架，通过共享参数的模型在搜索代理与评论家角色间交替，实现反馈与策略的协同进化，在七个智能体搜索基准上优于现有RL方法并降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果监督的搜索代理只能获得终端奖励，无法定位中间错误或在错误累积前纠正轨迹。将纠正性反馈视为学习到的轨迹内干预需要解决两个耦合问题：代理何时请求/使用反馈，以及评论家如何在结果混淆且失败模式随代理改进而变化的轨迹中推断有效纠正。

Method: CAFE框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 共享参数模型交替担任搜索代理和评论家角色；(2) 在线RL阶段，通过提示级调用-跳过成功差距形成比较性反馈估计来塑造请求回报，并用反馈感知的优势塑形在反馈前后重加权token优势；(3) 离线阶段，通过滚动派生的偏好优化从匹配的成功/失败轨迹对中学习反馈。反馈条件的恢复初始化自基础代理自身失败构建的轨迹。

Result: 在七个智能体搜索基准上，CAFE平均优于评估的基于RL的搜索代理；在六个域外基准上保持增益；降低了答案级幻觉。消融实验显示只改进代理或只改进评论家最终会平台化，而交替更新两者能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 自我改进的搜索代理需要与其引导的策略共同进化的反馈，反馈和策略的协同进化是实现持续性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [251] [StarHarness: Evolving Harnesses with Stratified Search for Enterprise Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24804)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Denis Akhiyarov,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar,Patrice Bechard,Sridhar Nemala,Sagar Davasam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StarHarness是一个框架，用于在保持模型权重不变的情况下演化环境特定的agent harness（包括提示词、工具接口、技能、MCP提供者、子代理结构和agent循环配置）。在ITBench SRE、EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM和AutomationBench Finance三个基准上，harness演化使完整基准性能提升了20-35个百分点，且收益可泛化到未参与演化的任务，并跨模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具丰富的企业任务中，模型与环境的持续不匹配是实际部署的关键障碍。现有方法通常需要调整模型权重或为每个环境手工设计agent配置，缺乏系统化、自动化的方式来解决模型-环境失配问题。

Method: StarHarness通过分层采样构建紧凑演化池（按基线失败行为分层）；将提议者可观的搜索任务与提议者隐藏的选择任务分离；保留held-out任务评估泛化性。演化的harness组件包括提示和任务框架、工具接口、技能、MCP支持提供者、子代理结构和agent循环配置。

Result: 在三个基准环境中，每次环境仅需4-12次接受的变更即实现全基准性能提升20-35个百分点。收益在未参与演化的held-out任务上持续存在，且无需重新演化即可跨GPT和Qwen模型家族迁移。轨迹分析显示改进源自接口修复、环境约定和压缩搜索的操作知识，减少了误报诊断并缩短了轨迹。

Conclusion: StarHarness提供了一种实用且有效的方式，通过演化环境特定的harness而非修改模型权重，来显著降低工具丰富型企业任务中持续的模型-环境失配问题，同时保证泛化性和跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: We present StarHarness, a framework for evolving environment-specific agent harnesses while keeping model weights fixed. The evolved harness can include prompt and task framing, tool interfaces, skills, MCP-backed providers, subagent structure, and agent-loop configuration. StarHarness constructs a compact evolution pool by stratifying tasks according to baseline failure behavior, separates proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, and reserves held-out tasks for evaluating generalization. Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, harness evolution improves full-benchmark performance by 20-35 percentage points over the default harness after 4-12 accepted changes per environment. These gains persist on tasks excluded from evolution and transfer without re-evolution across GPT and Qwen model families. Trace analysis links the improvements to interface repairs, environment conventions, and operational knowledge that compresses search, with fewer false-positive diagnoses and shorter trajectories in several settings. StarHarness therefore offers a practical way to reduce persistent model-environment mismatch in tool-rich enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [252] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格的因果流式视频异常检测器，其固定大小状态以O(1)时间/内存逐帧更新，无需缓冲或前瞻。核心是带输入与状态相关衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，通过因果下一嵌入预测自监督训练。作者推导了衰减谱与检测延迟/最短可捕获异常之间的闭式关系，并在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上验证。实际测量Apple M3 Pro上每帧0.74/0.77ms延迟（超1300 FPS），但精度落后于非因果SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba式SSM的视频异常检测仍依赖内部剪辑或窗口缓冲，缺乏时序记忆与检测延迟的理论解释，且仅用GPU吞吐量而非目标边缘硬件进行效率基准测试。作者旨在解决这三个缺陷。

Method: 提出严格因果的流式异常检测器：固定大小状态O(1)更新、无前瞻无缓冲；时序核心为带输入与状态相关衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归；在冻结视觉骨干上通过因果下一嵌入预测进行自监督训练；推导衰减谱与检测延迟/最短可捕获异常的理论闭式关系；在真实边缘硬件（Apple M3 Pro）上测量端到端延迟与吞吐量。

Result: 理论预测的稳定延迟界（57-59帧）远高于实测检测延迟（1.6和18.4帧），表明事件边界门而非基础衰减主导响应速度。M3 Pro上每帧0.74/0.77ms（超1300 FPS）。未调参配置在Ped2和Avenue上分别达到67.9%和70.2%帧级AUC，落后于非因果SSM基线。消融显示门控贡献依赖数据集规模：在较小Ped2上损害精度，在较大Avenue上有帮助。

Conclusion: 该流式检测器在边缘硬件效率上具备显著优势，但精度存在差距。作者计划通过缩小精度差距并扩展到第三个更大基准来推进后续工作。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [253] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES-PK框架，针对LLM-based KGQA中生成的候选答案，使用轻量级符号约束（类型、关系、排除）进行验证，并采用三值约束语义（满足/违反/未知）来应对不完整知识图谱的开放世界假设，从而过滤无效答案并保留有效答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-based KGQA方法要么依赖完整的语义解析（如SPARQL），在面对复杂schema和不完整KG时脆弱；要么完全依赖LLM推理生成答案，虽然鲁棒但缺乏形式化保证。作者希望研究一种互补的设置：由LLM生成候选答案，再用轻量级符号约束进行验证。

Method: 提出CES-PK（受限实体选择在部分知识下）问题框架。其核心包括：(1) 从问题中提取类型、关系和排除等轻量级符号约束；(2) 使用三值约束语义（satisfied, violated, unknown）处理不完整KG，避免在开放世界假设下错误拒绝；(3) 过滤明确违反约束的候选答案，保留满足或状态未知的候选。

Result: 在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上实例化该框架，实验表明：过滤无效候选可提升精确率，同时保留约束未被明确违反的候选以维持召回率；满足的约束还能为剩余候选排序提供额外的正面符号证据。

Conclusion: 通过符号约束验证而非完整的可执行逻辑形式，可以在保持开放世界假设下鲁棒性的同时，为LLM生成的候选答案提供形式化保证，实现精确率与召回率的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [254] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的双维度自动试题相似性分析框架（AISA），通过结构化分解和语义关联性两个维度来检测测验中与构念无关的冗余内容，并在心理测量验证和计算机化自适应测验（CAT）模拟中证明其优于传统文本相似度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测评的扩张和自动试题生成的普及导致题目间出现意外内容冗余（如措辞或情境框架的重复），传统相似度指标（如BLEU、余弦相似度）无法同时捕捉驱动感知冗余的结构层面和语义层面的细微差异。

Method: 提出双维度AISA框架：1）结构化分解（Structured Decomposition）用于分析题目的结构层次；2）语义关联性（Semantic Relatedness）用于衡量语义层面的关联；两者由LLM驱动实现操作化。通过心理测量验证比较LLM指标与传统文本指标与构念无关局部依赖的契合度；并在CAT模拟中引入基于LLM相似度约束的选题策略进行评估。

Result: 心理测量验证显示LLM导出的相似度指标与传统文本测量相比，与构念无关局部依赖指标关联更紧密，产生的项目参数分组更一致。CAT模拟表明，基于LLM相似度约束的选题策略在最小效率损失下改善了估计稳定性并降低了偏差，优于基于传统指标的约束。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AISA框架有潜力支持可扩展的题库管理、内容感知的测验组装以及体验敏感的自适应测验，适用于多样化的测评情境。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [255] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedV-KGQA框架，用于在知识图谱问答中处理跨组织垂直分区（各组织共享实体但拥有不相交的关系集合）的多跳推理问题，通过局部图增强和知识图谱嵌入实现无需集中式图访问的推理，并引入话题实体锚定机制避免运行时跨组织通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的知识图谱问答数据因治理和数据主权约束分布在不同组织间，集中式系统无法在事实被分割在垂直分区孤岛中时回答多跳问题。现有的集中式方法无法应对这种联邦场景下的多跳推理需求。

Method: 1) 融合局部图增强和知识图谱嵌入，确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各孤岛，建立结构化数据边界；2) 引入话题实体锚定机制，将问题锚定在正确的图邻域中，无需运行时跨组织通信。

Result: 在三个基准上评估了12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现强劲，接近集中式性能，可泛化到3跳推理，且对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA有效解决了垂直分区知识图谱下的多跳问答问题，在保护数据主权和隐私的同时保持了与集中式方法相当的性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [256] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPO++，一种改进的单流策略优化方法。SPO原本使用每个轨迹的whitening来归一化优势值，但作者发现轨迹级centering无法正确中心化actor实际消费的token加权量。SPO++通过在动作token度量下标准化终端结果优势，并按策略事件而非接收顺序组织提示证据，在ALFWorld和Math-TIR基准上显著提升了在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习（Group-relative RL）需要等待同一提示的兄弟轨迹（sibling rollouts），这对于长且可变长度的工具使用轨迹代价高昂。SPO虽然通过持久提示级价值估计消除了这一依赖，但其轨迹级whitening方案存在数学缺陷——轨迹centering无法正确中心化actor在token层面实际优化的加权量，导致学习效率受损。

Method: 1) 识别SPO中轨迹centering与token加权actor损失之间的不匹配问题；2) 提出在动作token度量下标准化终端结果优势（action-token-measure standardization）以修正此偏差；3) 重新组织提示证据的排序方式，按产生证据的策略事件而非学习者接收顺序排列，以更好地利用提示信息。

Result: 在ALFWorld的两个模型规模及Math-TIR上，SPO++相比SPO在在线学习效率上均有所提升。配对消融实验表明，动作token度量下的标准化（action-token-measure normalization）是最有效的组件，其余组件也有贡献。

Conclusion: SPO++通过修正SPO中轨迹级归一化与token加权actor损失之间的数学不匹配，并优化提示证据的组织方式，为长轨迹工具使用场景下的在线强化学习提供了更高效的解决方案。动作token度量下的标准化被证实是性能提升的关键因素。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Replicable Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23638)
*Marios Papamichalis,Regina Ruane,Theofanis Papamichalis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究预测模型校准的可复现性问题：不同分析者在独立样本上校准同一模型会得到不同预测集。通过共享随机种子并将校准阈值向上取整到粗粒度共享网格，可以实现具有任意期望概率的完全相同分类器，同时保持覆盖率保证，代价是集合大小和校准数据量的可量化增加。匹配下界证明该方法最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不同站点对预测模型进行校准会因数据随机性产生不一致的预测集，导致审计、缓存和跨站点批准成本高昂，且无法验证两次校准产生相同对象。需要解决独立校准何时能产生相同分类器以及这种一致性的代价问题。

Method: 提出共享单个随机种子并将校准阈值向上取整到粗粒度共享网格的方法，实现跨分析者的相同分类器部署。通过理论分析给出匹配下界，证明该方法的样本-成本前沿最优性，并通过真实数据实验验证。

Result: 共享种子+粗网格方法使部署分类器以任意期望概率跨分析者一致，覆盖率保证不受影响。实验在ImageNet输出、四医院站点划分和四种语言模型家族上验证了理论，包括测得的样本成本前沿。无共享种子时，固定网格仍将所有分析者限制在两个相邻分类器内。

Conclusion: 完美一致不可能实现，但共享种子结合粗网格取整可以以可量化代价换取高概率的一致性。该方法成本最优（匹配下界），且具有防操纵性，能阻止对标准共形预测的潜在攻击。

Abstract: Two analysts who calibrate the same predictive model on independent samples will deploy different prediction sets every time, because the calibration threshold inherits the randomness of the data. Wherever deployments must be audited, cached, or approved across sites, this instability is costly: no one can verify that two calibrations produced the same object. We ask two questions: when can independent calibrations yield the identical classifier, and what must that agreement cost? Perfect agreement is impossible, since a procedure that almost always returns one fixed answer cannot remain valid for every distribution, and exact agreement through shared randomness forces the procedure to ignore its data. Sharing a single random seed and rounding the calibrated threshold up to a coarse shared grid resolves the tension: the deployed classifier becomes identical across analysts with any desired probability, coverage guarantees survive, and the price is a quantified increase in set size and calibration data. Matching lower bounds show that no threshold method can pay less, and the method's one tuning constant vanishes asymptotically. Without any shared seed, a fixed grid still confines all analysts to two adjacent classifiers, and no method does better. Replicability also blocks gaming: selecting the most favorable of many recalibrations barely moves a replicable classifier, while the same selection silently undercovers standard conformal prediction. Experiments on real ImageNet outputs, a four-hospital site split, and four language-model families match the theory, including the measured sample-cost frontier.

</details>


### [258] [$\texttt{findr}$: Transparent and Fair Credit Risk Decisions through Semi-Structured Regressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24582)
*Victor Medina-Olivares,Stefan Lessmann,Jonathan Crook*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出findr框架，一种用于二元信用风险建模的半结构化深度回归方法，将逻辑斯蒂回归的可解释线性分量与正交神经残差相结合，并引入Wasserstein公平约束，实现预测精度、公平性和可解释性之间的显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用风险模型需要同时满足预测准确性、可解释性和公平性约束。逻辑回归系数易解释但无法捕捉非线性结构；灵活模型预测更好但其解释往往是事后性的，不能真实反映决策规则本身。因此需要在可解释性和预测能力之间架起桥梁。

Method: 将logit分解为可解释的结构化分量和正交神经残差两部分。正交化将系数效应与残差非线性变化分离；训练过程中引入in-processing Wasserstein惩罚以缓解组间差异；框架还提供诊断工具，衡量结构化分量对logit变异的贡献、决策一致性和局部方向一致性。

Result: 在模拟研究和八个公开信用数据集上的评分级准确性-公平性前沿评估中，当信号近似线性时，findr表现接近逻辑回归；当存在相关非线性结构时，能恢复神经模型大部分预测增益。诊断工具能识别系数解释何时接近完整拟合模型，以及何时需要检查残差变化。

Conclusion: 半结构化建模是使性能、公平性和可解释性权衡在信用风险决策中显式化的实用方法。

Abstract: Credit risk models increasingly need to combine predictive accuracy with transparent explanations and auditable fairness constraints. Logistic regression remains attractive because its coefficients are easy to interpret, but it can miss nonlinear structure. Flexible models can improve prediction, but their explanations are often post-hoc and may not describe the decision rule itself. We introduce $\texttt{findr}$, short for flexible, interpretable deep regression, a semi-structured framework for binary credit risk modelling that decomposes the logit into an interpretable structured component and an orthogonal neural residual. The orthogonalisation separates coefficient-based effects from residual nonlinear variation, while an in-processing Wasserstein penalty mitigates group disparities by comparing score distributions during training. The framework also includes diagnostics that measure the structured component's contribution to logit variation, decision agreement, and local directional consistency. We evaluate $\texttt{findr}$ in a simulation study and on eight public credit datasets using score-level accuracy-fairness frontiers. The results show that $\texttt{findr}$ behaves close to logistic regression when the signal is approximately linear, while recovering much of the predictive gain of neural models when nonlinear structure is relevant. The diagnostics identify when coefficient-based explanations remain close to the full fitted model and when residual variation must also be examined. These findings support semi-structured modelling as a practical way to make performance, fairness, and interpretability trade-offs explicit in credit risk decisions.

</details>


### [259] [(Mis)Understanding Benign Overfitting in Equity Return Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23761)
*Hui Guo,Jiawei Huang,Runze Li,Yan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了良性过拟合现象是否适用于股票收益预测。研究发现：过量参数化模型在预测风险上呈现双下降模式；最优岭回归模型虽然始终优于无岭模型，但在参数与观测比极高时差距可忽略；但最终两种模型都无法跑赢简单历史均值基准，表明标准股票预测因子缺乏真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典偏差-方差权衡理论认为过拟合会损害泛化性能，但最近研究表明高度过量参数化模型即使在插值训练数据时也能良好预测。作者质疑这一"良性过拟合"现象是否适用于权益收益预测这一复杂领域，并希望调和现代与经典机器学习在资产定价中的矛盾。

Method: 作者通过实证分析研究岭回归与无岭（ridgeless）回归模型在股票收益预测中的表现，记录预测风险的双下降模式，并比较两种模型在不同参数-观测比下的性能差距。同时推导零斜率系数原假设下的渐近结果，将实证发现与统计理论进行对照。

Result: 实证发现：1) 无岭模型的预测风险呈现双下降模式；2) 最优岭模型一致优于无岭模型，但参数-观测比极高时差距趋近为零；3) 两种模型都无法超越简单历史平均的预测表现。这些实证结果与零斜率系数假设下的渐近理论一致。

Conclusion: 在不存在真实预测信号的条件下，即使高度灵活的非线性机器学习模型也无法提供超越历史均值的股票收益预测能力。现代与经典机器学习在资产定价中趋向一致：缺乏真实信号时，它们渐近地坍缩到历史平均基准。这表明标准股票预测因子缺乏真正的预测能力。

Abstract: Highly overparameterized models often predict well despite interpolating training data in complex domains, challenging the classical bias--variance tradeoff. We investigate whether this ``benign overfitting'' phenomenon extends to equity return prediction. Consistent with recent statistical theory, we document two key phenomena: first, a double descent pattern in the ridgeless model's prediction risk; and second, that while the optimal ridge model consistently outperforms its ridgeless counterpart, this performance gap becomes negligible at large parameter-to-observation ratios. Ultimately, however, both models fail to outperform a simple historical average. This empirical evidence aligns with our asymptotic results under the null hypothesis of zero slope coefficients, suggesting that standard equity predictors lack true forecasting power---even within highly flexible, nonlinear machine learning architectures. These findings reconcile modern and classical machine learning in asset pricing: in the absence of a true signal, they asymptotically collapse to the historical average benchmark.

</details>


### [260] [Generalization, memorization, and overfitting for diffusion models trained in the lazy high-dimensional regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23938)
*Hugo Latourelle-Vigeant,Sinho Chewi,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian,John Sous,Theodor Misiakiewicz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于分数的生成模型在过参数化条件下的泛化机制，在高维比例极限下推导了去噪分数匹配的精确风险轨迹，揭示了训练过程中三个阶段的不同估计器行为（谱估计器、纯噪声分数、经验贝叶斯估计器），并分析了它们在反向SDE中的组合方式及最终样本分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的生成模型在图像、音频、视频等高维任务中取得了显著成功，但其泛化机制尚不清晰——如果回归问题在有限数据上精确求解，模型只会重现训练样本。因此理解隐式或显式正则化如何带来泛化能力是核心问题。作者希望将监督学习中良性过拟合和算法正则化的理论拓展到生成模型领域。

Method: 在向量值再生核希尔伯特空间中使用内积核研究去噪分数匹配。在n≍d的比例高维体制下，推导梯度流训练下的精确风险轨迹。分析三种不同估计器（谱估计器、纯噪声分数、经验贝叶斯估计器）的特征，并研究它们沿反向时间SDE的组合方式以及由此产生的样本分布。

Result: 风险轨迹呈现三个阶段，每个阶段由性质不同的估计器主导：泛化性能好的谱估计器、局部峰值插值训练目标的纯噪声分数、以及记忆数据的经验贝叶斯估计器。分析揭示了监督学习中熟悉的机制（核线性化、核非线性部分的自诱导正则化），同时也发现了生成建模特有的新现象。

Conclusion: 这项工作为基于分数的生成模型建立了与监督学习中良性过拟合理论平行的生成对应理论，揭示了过参数化生成模型在训练不同阶段的泛化与记忆行为，并展示了生成建模特有的现象学特征。

Abstract: Modern score-based generative models have achieved remarkable empirical success in high-dimensional tasks such as image, audio, and video synthesis. These models reduce distribution learning to a sequence of regression problems that, if solved exactly on finite data, would ultimately reproduce the training samples. Their ability to generalize must therefore arise from the implicit or explicit regularization during training. In this work, we develop a generative counterpart to the theory of benign overfitting and algorithmic regularization for overparameterized neural networks in the supervised lazy-training regime. We study denoising score matching in a vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space with an inner-product kernel. In the proportional high-dimensional regime $n\asymp d$, we derive exact risk trajectories under gradient flow training. These trajectories exhibit three phases governed by qualitatively distinct estimators: a spectral estimator that generalizes, a pure-noise score with localized peaks that interpolate the training objective, and an empirical Bayes estimator that memorizes the data. We then analyze how these estimators combine along the reverse-time SDE and characterize the distribution of the resulting samples. The analysis reveals familiar mechanisms from supervised learning, including kernel linearization and self-induced regularization from the nonlinear part of the kernel, but also reveals a distinct phenomenology specific to generative modeling.

</details>


### [261] [qshap: Fast Shapley Decomposition of $R^2$ for Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24104)
*Zhongli Jiang,Min Zhang,Dabao Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了qshap，一个同时提供R和Python版本的工具包，用于对梯度提升决策树（GBDT）的R²值进行Shapley分解，从而量化特征对模型性能的具体贡献。它通过分解单个观测的二次损失，支持xgboost、lightgbm和catboost等主流GBDT实现，并针对oblivious树设计了专门的加速后端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征归因方法大多聚焦于单个预测的局部归因，虽然可以通过聚合得到特征重要性，但无法直接分解R²等模型性能度量指标。因此需要一种能够全局量化特征对模型性能贡献的方法。

Method: qshap通过对单个观测的二次损失进行Shapley分解来量化特征对模型性能的贡献。它支持xgboost、lightgbm和catboost等主流GBDT实现，采用统一的树表示和高效的C++后端。此外，针对oblivious树设计了专门的后端，利用其对称结构大幅加速计算。整个工具包采用模块化设计，可扩展支持其他基于二叉决策树的GBDT实现。

Result: qshap提供了R和Python两个版本的实现，能够对GBDT模型的R²值进行Shapley分解，量化特征和观测的重要性，并支持主流GBDT框架。针对oblivious树的专用后端显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: qshap为GBDT模型提供了全局层面的特征贡献分析工具，弥补了现有局部归因方法无法直接分解模型性能指标（如R²）的不足，并通过高效的后端实现和模块化设计支持多种主流GBDT框架。

Abstract: Numerous methods have been developed to quantify feature attributions in individual predictions for tree ensembles. However, many applications require global measures of feature contributions to overall model performance. Although local attribution scores can be aggregated to characterize feature importance, such summaries do not directly decompose measures of predictive performance, such as $R^2$. This article introduces qshap, available in both R and Python, which provides Shapley decomposition of $R^2$ values for gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDTs) to quantify feature-specific contributions to model performance. By decomposing the quadratic loss of individual observations, qshap provides flexible tools to explore the importance of individual features and observations. qshap currently supports widely used GBDT implementations, including xgboost, lightgbm, and catboost, through a unified tree representation and efficient C++ backends. Its modular design can accommodate other GBDT implementations built from binary decision trees. In addition, we introduce a specialized backend for oblivious trees that exploits their symmetric structure to substantially accelerate computation.

</details>


### [262] [A Heterogeneous Mixture of Experts Framework for Interpretable Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24195)
*Soham Chatterjee,Rwitobroto Dey,Smarajit Bose*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了可解释的混合专家框架（MoDT），引入包含决策树、线性SVM和二次判别分析的异质专家族，在统一概率门控机制下实现自适应归纳偏置选择，并保证似然推断的单调上升性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释混合专家方法（如MoDT）采用同质决策树专家，虽然透明但限制了模型在整个特征空间中只能使用单一的归纳偏置，无法根据不同的局部数据几何特性选择最合适的模型。

Method: 1) 引入异质专家族：将决策树、线性支持向量机和二次判别分析三种专家整合到统一的概率门控机制中；2) 对非概率专家进行校准，使其输出条件类别概率，从而支持基于似然的推断；3) 在广义期望最大化框架下进行参数估计，并证明异质门控更新的单调上升保证。

Result: 在合成和真实基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架能根据局部数据几何特征自适应地选择专家，产生可解释的专家分配结果，预测性能与同质MoDT和随机森林相当。

Conclusion: 该方法在统一的混合专家框架中实现了可解释性、自适应归纳偏置选择和概率一致性三者的结合。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models provide a flexible framework for partitioning complex prediction problems into simpler local learning tasks through an input-dependent gating mechanism. Existing interpretable MoE approaches, such as Mixture of Decision Trees (MoDT), achieve transparency by employing homogeneous decision-tree experts, but this restricts the model to a single inductive bias across all regions of the feature space. We extend the MoDT framework by introducing heterogeneous expert families comprising decision trees, linear support vector machines, and quadratic discriminant analysis under a common probabilistic gating mechanism. To ensure coherent likelihood-based inference, non-probabilistic experts are calibrated to produce conditional class probabilities, allowing parameter estimation within the generalized Expectation-Maximization framework of MoDT. We further establish theoretical monotone ascent guarantees for the proposed heterogeneous gating updates, providing a justification for the optimization procedure. Experiments on a diverse collection of synthetic and real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework adaptively specializes experts according to local data geometry, yielding interpretable expert assignments while achieving predictive performance competitive with homogeneous MoDT and Random Forests. The proposed approach combines interpretability, adaptive inductive bias selection, and probabilistic coherence within a unified mixture-of-experts framework.

</details>


### [263] [Sequential operator learning under dependent data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24426)
*Rafael Oliveira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对顺序采集数据（如自适应实验设计、贝叶斯优化、动力系统建模）中的算子学习问题，推导了Hilbert空间中带向量值噪声的随机过程的时间一致自归一化浓度界，并以此获得线性及非线性参数化算子的回归误差保证，无需独立或混合假设，支持无限维输入输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习理论多假设数据独立或满足混合条件，但在自适应实验设计、贝叶斯优化和动力系统建模中，观测数据是依序采集且相互依赖的，未来输入或感知算子可能依赖前面数据。这种依赖性和自适应选择使得传统浓度不等式失效，缺乏对自适应算子学习收敛性的理论保证。

Method: 推导了Hilbert空间中带向量值噪声的随机过程的time-uniform self-normalized concentration bound；基于这些界，分别分析了两类算子：一是线性算子的回归误差保证（允许目标在Hilbert估计空间之外）；二是带强凸损失和正则化器的非线性参数化算子的回归误差保证。方法支持无限维输入输出且不依赖独立性或混合性假设。

Result: 获得了线性算子（包括目标超出Hilbert估计空间的情况）和非线性参数化算子（使用强凸损失与正则化器）的回归误差上界；这些结果适用于无限维输入输出，且无需独立性原混合假设，适用于自适应学习和随机动力学数据场景。

Conclusion: 该工作为自适应算子学习和从随机动力学数据学习提供了重要的收敛性理论保证，是向此类场景中算子学习方法建立系统理论迈出的关键一步。

Abstract: Learning operators from sequentially collected data arises in adaptive experimental design, Bayesian optimization, and dynamical-system modelling, where observations may be dependent, and future inputs or sensing operators may depend on preceding data. We derive time-uniform self-normalized concentration bounds for stochastic processes in Hilbert spaces with vector-valued noise. We use these bounds to obtain regression-error guarantees for linear operators, including targets outside the Hilbert estimation space, and for nonlinear parametric operators trained with strongly convex losses and regularizers. Our results allow possibly infinite-dimensional inputs and outputs without independence or mixing assumptions, providing a major step towards convergence guarantees for adaptive operator learning and learning from stochastic dynamical data.

</details>


### [264] [Scalable and Versatile Identification for Hierarchical Structural Causal Models: A New Look at Project STAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24500)
*Janis Aiad,Aghiles Drali,Aymen El Ouadrhiri,Anass Ettahiri,Yasser Oufqir,Simon Patry,David Cortes,Marianne Clausel,Emilie Devijver*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完整、可扩展、开源的层级结构因果模型（HSCM）流水线，将符号识别与数值估计相结合，以STAR实验（基于班级规模的随机实验）为应用场景。该流水线集成图变换、pyAgrum的do-calculus自动识别、符号表达式到闭式HSCM公式的适配，以及基于局部概率模型的数值估计，并通过改进的抽象语法树（AST）实现并行化计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级数据（如学生嵌套于班级）在因果推断中普遍存在，但现有方法要么忽略层级结构（平坦基线）导致无法编码班级层面的干预，要么仅有符号识别而缺乏实用的数值估计。作者希望弥合符号识别与实际估计之间的鸿沟，提供可落地的HSCM推断工具。

Method: 1) 图变换构建HSCM；2) 利用pyAgrum的do-calculus自动识别因果效应；3) 将符号表达式适配为闭式HSCM公式；4) 通过拟合的局部概率模型进行数值估计；5) 提出改进的抽象语法树（AST），将识别出的公式分解为独立的密度、期望和边缘化任务，实现并行与可扩展计算。

Result: 在规范HSCM结构及已知真值的基准场景上验证了流水线的正确性；在STAR实验幼儿园数学成绩上的应用表明，平坦基线虽然能恢复关联，但无法编码班级层面的干预；仅靠符号识别不足以完成实用的层级因果推断，可扩展估计和数值稳定性检查是关键组成部分。

Conclusion: 层级结构信息的保留对于正确编码班级层面干预至关重要；一个完整的HSCM推断流水线必须同时包含符号识别、数值估计与稳定性验证；本工作提供了开源、并行化的解决方案，为该领域后续研究奠定了工程基础。

Abstract: The STAR (Student-Teacher Achievement Ratio) experiment (1985, Tennessee, USA) is a landmark hierarchical dataset designed to assess the impact of class size on student outcomes, with observations nested within classes. To encode class-level interventions in such hierarchical settings, we develop a complete, scalable, open-source pipeline for Hierarchical Structural Causal Models (HSCM) that bridges symbolic identification and practical estimation. Our approach integrates graph transformations, pyAgrum's do-calculus for automatic identification of causal effects, adaptation of symbolic expression into closed-form HSCM formulas, and numerical estimation from fitted local probability models. A key innovation is our adapted Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), which decomposes pyAgrum's identified formulas into independent density, expectation, and marginalization tasks, enabling parallel and scalable computation. We validate the pipeline on canonical HSCM motifs and benchmark scenarios with known ground truth, then apply it to STAR kindergarten mathematics outcomes. The results show that flat baselines (ignoring hierarchy) recover associations but fail to encode class-level interventions, and that symbolic identification alone is not enough for practical Hierarchical Structural Causal inference; scalable estimation and numerical stability checks are central parts of the scientific object.

</details>


### [265] [What FID Hides: Detecting, Ranking, and Diagnosing Deviations in Generative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24881)
*Hao Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ZID（Z-resolved Integrated Diagnostic），一个综合评估生成模型分布质量的诊断框架，由三个关联输出组成：排序离群程度的指数、置换检验p值（检验分布相等性）以及有符号的离散度读出（用于诊断）。ZID结合了六个标准化的位置和离散度敏感指标（来自RISE秩图和两个带宽的高斯核），克服了FID和KID仅用标量值且无法区分欠离散/过离散方向的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID只使用前两阶矩（均值与协方差），可能遗漏分布差异；在ImageNet上，仅优化匹配Inception均值/协方差的不可识别图像得到的FID（24.7）甚至优于真实图像（58.6），说明FID的矩限制有实际后果。此外，FID/KID作为标量差异不随样本交换而变化，无法编码离散度变化的方向（模式崩塌导致的欠离散 vs 过离散），因此需要一个能够诊断方向且经过校准的检验方法。

Method: ZID融合三类互补信号：(1) RISE秩图提供位置和离散度敏感指标；(2) 两个不同带宽的高斯核（GPK）捕捉不同尺度下的分布差异。六个标准化指标被组合成三个关联输出：一个用于排序离群程度的指数，一个置换p值用于统计检验分布相等性，以及一个有符号的离散度读出用于方向诊断（区分欠离散和过离散）。

Result: 在受控实验中，ZID能够检测广泛的分布偏离，其得分在对应扫描中随严重程度递增，包括FID保持平坦甚至反转的情况。在DiT-XL/2和SiT-XL/2引导参数扫描实验中，ZID检测到与真实数据的偏离，其有符号读出将高引导下的多样性崩塌标记为欠离散。

Conclusion: ZID作为FID/KID的补充或替代诊断工具，不仅能对生成模型进行排序和显著性检验，还能诊断偏离方向（欠离散vs过离散），从而为生成模型评估提供更全面、更可信的视角。

Abstract: Generative models are commonly ranked by Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Kernel Inception Distance (KID), yet FID's first-two-moment summary can miss distributional differences, and a reported scalar gap alone is not a calibrated test against sampling variation. FID's moment restriction has concrete consequences: on ImageNet, visually unrecognizable images optimized only to match the reference Inception mean and covariance obtain FID $24.7$ versus $58.6$ for held-out real images (lower is better). Moreover, FID and KID are scalar discrepancies that are unchanged when the two samples are exchanged and therefore do not encode the direction of a dispersion change: under-dispersion, as can occur in mode collapse, versus over-dispersion. We introduce \textbf{ZID} (\emph{Z-resolved Integrated Diagnostic}), which combines six standardized location- and dispersion-sensitive arms from a rank graph (RISE) and Gaussian kernels (GPK at two bandwidths). Rather than asking one scalar to serve incompatible roles, ZID reports three linked outputs: an index for ranking departure magnitude, a permutation $p$-value for testing distributional equality, and a signed dispersion readout for diagnosis. In controlled experiments, ZID detects a broad range of departures, and its score tracks increasing severity along the corresponding sweeps, including cases in which FID is flat or reversed. On DiT-XL/2 and SiT-XL/2 guidance sweeps, ZID detects departure from real data, and its signed readout labels the high-guidance diversity collapse as under-dispersion.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Energy-Aware Performance Evaluation of Nonlinear Mechatronic Systems Under Matched-Tracking Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23578)
*Bhanuka Dayawansa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个能量感知评估框架，将轨迹跟踪误差与执行器累计能量相结合，引入单一指标EAPI，用于评估机电系统控制性能。仿真表明，在相似的跟踪精度下，非线性系统比线性系统消耗更多执行器能量，说明仅靠轨迹指标不足以全面评价系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的轨迹跟踪指标（如RMSE、超调量、稳定时间）只描述输出跟踪效果，不考虑产生该运动所需的执行器努力。在非线性系统（刚度、耗散依赖系统状态）中，这一局限更为突出，因此需要一种考虑能量消耗的性能评估方法。

Method: 提出一个能量感知评估框架，将跟踪误差与累计执行器能量结合，实现近似匹配性能系统间的比较。使用一个简单指标（EAPI）将两个方面统一到单一度量中。通过PD控制下的线性与非线性系统仿真进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，可比的跟踪精度可能对应显著不同的执行器能量消耗。非线性系统在匹配工作点下始终需要更多能量，反映了非线性刚度和摩擦的影响。

Conclusion: 单独的轨迹指标不足以全面反映系统努力的差异，将能量纳入评估可提供更全面的性能评价基础。

Abstract: Trajectory-tracking metrics such as root-mean-square error (RMSE), overshoot, and settling time are widely used to evaluate control performance in mechatronic systems. However, these measures describe output tracking alone and do not account for the actuator effort required to produce the observed motion. This limitation becomes more pronounced in nonlinear systems, where stiffness and dissipation depend on the system state. This paper examines how these effects influence actuator energy under similar tracking conditions. An energy-aware evaluation framework is introduced that combines tracking error with cumulative actuator energy and enables comparison between systems with approximately matched performance. A simple index (EAPI) is used to capture both aspects in a single measure. Simulation results for linear and nonlinear systems under proportional-derivative control show that comparable tracking accuracy can correspond to significantly different actuator energy. The nonlinear system consistently requires more energy across matched operating points, reflecting the influence of nonlinear stiffness and friction. These results suggest that trajectory-based metrics alone may not fully capture differences in system effort, and that including energy provides a more informative basis for performance evaluation.

</details>


### [267] [A Hybrid Two-Stage Machine Learning Pipeline for Fault Detection and Classification in Power Transmission Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23726)
*Sahil Manikshete,Atharva Gujarathi,Thanh Long Vu,Akhtar Hussain,Van-Hai Bui*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段混合机器学习管线用于高压输电网络故障检测，将异常检测（Isolation Forest + 可选监督分支的OR融合）与随机森林多分类解耦，结合零序对称分量特征工程，在TLFaultDataset上将线路故障端到端准确率从31.3%提升至95.8%，并在独立单点数据集上达到97.25%的端到端准确率，超越联邦学习基准且无需GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电故障数据集常存在类别不平衡问题，且某些故障类型的电气特征落在正常运行包络内，导致单一模型分类器在安全关键场景下失效，无法兼顾检测与分类的准确性。

Method: 提出两阶段管线：(1) 第一阶段将Isolation Forest异常检测器与可选监督二分类器通过OR融合规则结合，监督分支仅在异常检测器无法解决的故障类别存在时自动分配；(2) 第二阶段仅对第一阶段标记的样本应用随机森林多分类器。特征工程采用每测量点的操作映射，将6个原始通道扩展为18个特征（含基于Fortescue定理的零序对称分量），共产生18L个特征。
