Result: 在TLFaultDataset上线路故障端到端准确率从31.3%提升至95.8%；在独立单点数据集上实现97.25%全类别端到端准确率，超过TLFed联邦基准94.84%，CPU上每样本仅0.05ms。消融实验显示零序特征解决了三相与三相接地故障的混淆，将该类别对F1从0.39提升至0.997。

Conclusion: 两阶段解耦架构有效解决不平衡数据下检测与分类的耦合问题，零序对称分量特征对故障辨识至关重要，且零序方向具有系统依赖性，需学习决策边界而非固定继电器阈值。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate fault detection in high-voltage transmission networks is essential for grid reliability and equipment protection. Transmission fault datasets are frequently imbalanced, and certain fault types produce electrical signatures that fall within the normal operating envelope, causing single-model classifiers to fail on safety-critical cases. This paper proposes a hybrid two-stage machine learning pipeline that decouples detection from classification. Stage 1 combines an Isolation Forest anomaly detector with an optional supervised binary detector through an OR-fusion rule; the supervised branch is allocated automatically during training for any fault class the anomaly detector cannot resolve, and is omitted when no such class exists. Stage 2 applies a Random Forest multiclass classifier only to samples flagged by Stage 1. Feature engineering is expressed as a per-measurement-point operator mapping six raw channels to eighteen features, including zero-sequence symmetrical components derived from Fortescue's theorem, yielding 18L features for L measurement points. On the TLFaultDataset, the pipeline raises Line-fault end-to-end accuracy from 31.3% to 95.8%. On an independent single-point dataset, the same framework attains 97.25% end-to-end accuracy across all classes including normal operation, exceeding the TLFed federated benchmark of 94.84% without GPU or federated infrastructure, at 0.05 ms per sample on CPU. Ablation on both datasets shows zero-sequence features resolving the three-phase versus three-phase-to-ground ambiguity, raising the F1-score of that class pair from 0.39 to 0.997. The direction of the zero-sequence signature is found to be system-dependent, motivating a learned decision boundary in place of a fixed relay threshold.

</details>


### [268] [Critical Weather Scenario Screening Using Weather-to-Voltage (W2V) Predictive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23872)
*Sol Lim,Min-Seung Ko,Hao Zhu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关键天气场景筛选框架，用于识别可能触发电网高压（HV）事件的天气条件。该方法通过可微分的天气到电压（W2V）预测模型，在紧凑潜在空间中进行梯度更新，寻找使电压临界分数最大化的天气扰动，并通过非负性和L1范数约束保证物理一致性和稀疏可解释性。在6717节点合成德州系统上的实验验证了天气不确定性触发高压事件的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天气感知的电力系统脆弱性分析仅局限于组件级停电风险，无法识别天气场景作为电网级电压违规的潜在驱动因素。因此需要一种框架，能够从高维天气空间中筛选出可能触发高压事件的关键天气场景。

Method: 1) 构建可微分的天气到电压（W2V）预测模型，映射高维天气空间到电压状态。2) 在紧凑潜在空间中进行梯度更新，最大化预定义的电压临界性分数。3) 采用非负性约束实现物理一致的天气扰动。4) 采用L1范数约束实现有界扰动，促进稀疏可解释的扰动模式，并用于生成敏感性感知的脆弱性排名。

Result: 在6717节点合成德州电力系统上的数值实验有效证明了天气不确定性触发高压事件的潜力，这种潜力无法通过单个天气场景的电压分析来体现。最脆弱的场景呈现出以风为主导、集中在高风电容量区域的扰动模式，与实际潮流数据和真实系统运行经验一致。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了天气不确定性可能系统性触发电网高压事件，且脆弱性最高的天气场景具有可解释的物理特征（风主导、高风电容量区域集中），为电力系统天气感知的脆弱性评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: This paper proposes a critical weather scenario screening framework for identifying weather conditions that can trigger high-voltage (HV) events in the power grid. Unlike conventional weather-aware contingency analysis limited to component-level outage risk, our framework screens weather scenarios as potential drivers of grid-level voltage violations. Given a non-critical weather scenario, we seek the perturbation over the high-dimensional weather space to maximize a pre-defined voltage criticality score, by using a differentiable weather-to-voltage (W2V) predictive model to facilitate the gradient update over a compact latent space. Specifically, a non-negativity constraint is used for achieving physically-consistent perturbations, with another L1-norm based constraint for bounded perturbation. The latter could promote sparse and interpretable perturbations, and this uniform budget also yields a sensitivity-aware vulnerability ranking across different weather scenarios. Numerical experiments on a 6717-bus synthetic Texas system have effectively demonstrated the potential of weather uncertainty in triggering HV events, and this potential cannot be represented by the voltage analysis of individual weather scenarios. Interestingly, the most vulnerable scenarios are characterized by wind-dominated perturbation patterns concentrated in high wind-capacity regions, coinciding with observations from actual power flow data and experiences in real system operations.

</details>


### [269] [A tale of perfect fit and phantom optima: how data-driven models can fail in real-time optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23885)
*Prithvi Dake,Rahul Bindlish,James B. Rawlings*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过乙酸乙烯酯单体基准过程研究数据驱动模型在实时优化（RTO）中的可靠性。研究发现，无论是结构化混合模型还是全数据驱动的神经ODE模型，虽然都能准确拟合工厂数据，但其经济最优解与工厂真实最优解存在显著差异，并产生大量虚假最优解。更关键的是，即使使用无噪声数据并从能恢复工厂最优解的权重初始化，随机梯度训练仍可能漂移导致更差的RTO方案，表明训练优化器本身也是误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时优化依赖过程模型寻找经济最优操作条件，但第一性原理模型需要大量过程知识。数据驱动的机器学习模型虽然能准确拟合历史数据并通过验证测试，但能否被信任用于经济优化尚不明确，本文旨在系统地调查这一问题。

Method: 使用具有唯一、条件良好经济最优解的乙酸乙烯酯单体基准过程。训练两种模型：(1)结构化混合模型，结合已知质量平衡和热力学与神经网络闭合的未知动力学；(2)全数据驱动的神经ODE模型。通过多起点搜索比较模型经济最优解与工厂真实最优解，并检验训练优化器对RTO解的影响。

Result: 两种模型均能准确再现工厂测量结果且不同随机初始化下预测差异很小，但经济最优解与工厂差异显著。工厂在多次起点搜索中返回单一最优解，而训练模型返回许多虚假最优解。此外，即使在无噪声数据下从能恢复工厂最优解的权重初始化，随机梯度训练仍可能漂移至产生更差RTO解的权重。

Conclusion: 对所有可用测量数据具有良好的预测拟合并不能保证可靠的经济性能。数据驱动RTO模型至少应能在此类决策导向的基准上恢复最优解，才能考虑用于工厂测试和应用。所辨识的模型既是数据的产物，也是训练优化器的产物。

Abstract: Real-time optimization (RTO) relies on process models to locate economically optimal operating conditions. Because developing first-principles models requires significant process knowledge, data-driven alternatives are increasingly attractive. Modern machine-learning models can fit historical plant data accurately and often pass standard validation tests. Whether such models can be trusted for economic optimization, however, remains unclear. We investigate this question using a vinyl acetate monomer benchmark process with a unique, well-conditioned economic optimum. We train a structured hybrid model that combines known mass balances and thermodynamics with a neural-network closure for unknown kinetics, and a fully data-driven neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) model. Both models reproduce plant measurements accurately and exhibit little variation in predictions across random initializations. Yet their economic optima differ substantially from that of the plant. Where the plant returns a single optimum on multistart search, the trained models return many phantom optima. We further show that the training optimizer alone can be yet another source of error. Even with noise-free data and initialization at weights that recover the plant optimum, stochastic gradient training can drift to weights that yield substantially worse RTO solutions. The identified model is thus an artifact of the training optimizer as well as the data. These results demonstrate that a good predictive fit of all available measurements does not guarantee reliable economic performance. A data-driven model for RTO should at least be required to recover the optimum on a decision-oriented benchmark like the one developed here before being considered for plant testing and application.

</details>


### [270] [Dynamical System-Based Imitation Learning and Neuroadaptive Control for Trajectory Recovery in Autonomous Ships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23924)
*Yeyson A. Becerra-Mora,José Ángel Acosta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合学习-控制架构，将基于动态系统(DS)的模仿学习(IL)参考生成器与神经自适应控制器相结合，用于无人水面艇(USV)的重复性海上作业，解决了DS-based IL在外部扰动下全局目标收敛与局部轨迹复现保真度之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DS-based IL方法在真实海事环境中面临关键权衡：优先保证全局目标收敛，却牺牲了局部轨迹复现的保真度，难以应对持续的海洋扰动。

Method: 提出一个混合学习-控制架构，包含两部分：基于DS的IL参考生成器用于学习人类示范；神经自适应控制器用于将USV在外部扰动后驱回示范路径，实现动态类人的反应性对齐（称为行为跟踪）。

Result: 使用Marine Systems Simulator (MSS)工具箱进行仿真验证，结果表明该框架能泛化复杂操纵任务，并在扰动下相比其他控制策略显著提升轨迹跟踪保真度。

Conclusion: 混合学习-控制架构有效解决了DS-based IL在真实环境扰动下的轨迹保真度问题，为USV在复杂海事条件下的自主操纵提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Repetitive maritime operations can be effectively learned using the Imitation Learning (IL) paradigm, which transfers human expertise directly to Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) control systems. Dynamical Systems (DS) are widely used to model non-linear human demonstrations while offering inherent stability guarantees. However, real-world execution under persistent marine perturbations reveals a critical trade-off: standard DS-based IL approaches prioritize global target convergence at the expense of localized trajectory reproduction fidelity. To address this limitation, we present a hybrid learning-control architecture that integrates a DS-based IL reference generator with a neuroadaptive controller. Our approach introduces a control action that drives the USV back to the demonstrated path following exogenous disturbances, enabling dynamic human-like reactive alignment-termed behavioral tracking. The proposed methodology is validated using the Marine Systems Simulator (MSS) toolbox. Simulation results confirm that the framework generalizes complex maneuvering tasks while substantially improving trajectory tracking fidelity under disturbances compared to alternative control strategies.

</details>


### [271] [Trusted Polytopic Action Sets for Fast Planning in Underactuated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24019)
*Akshay Jaitly,Siavash Farzan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多面体动作集（PAS）的在线凸运动规划方法，通过构造局部动作坐标和信任凸内近似，实现在欠驱动非线性系统中的快速规划，比基线方法快14-78倍，并显著降低终端误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动系统的动态可行运动位于函数空间的流形上，这给凸运动规划带来了挑战。现有方法难以在欠驱动且可能非线性的系统中快速生成可信的凸动作集合。

Method: 在名义轨迹周围构造局部有限维动作坐标，每个参数向量通过仿射轨迹映射编码完整邻近运动；引入动力学违反度量并在动作空间中用IRIS启发式膨胀过程提取信任凸内近似；PAS引导的树扩展将节点视为可组合的可行家族而非单条轨迹。

Result: 在杂乱的平面场景中规划时间仅为几十毫秒（比运动动力学RRT基线快14-78倍）；在非线性欠驱动基准测试中，相比采样和NLP基线，终端误差降低了26-86%。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将局部非线性保真度与凸重用相结合，实现了欠驱动非线性系统上快速、可信的在线凸运动规划，为长时域规划提供了高效的框架。

Abstract: Underactuated systems pose a challenge for convex motion planning because their dynamically feasible motions lie on a manifold of trajectories in function space. Building on our earlier formulation of polytopic action sets (PAS), this paper presents a method for rapidly generating, online, trusted convex sets of short-horizon actions for underactuated and potentially nonlinear systems. Around a nominal trajectory, we construct local finite-dimensional action coordinates in which each parameter vector encodes a complete nearby motion through an affine trajectory map, rendering collision-avoidance and control bounds linear. To remain consistent with the nonlinear dynamics, we introduce a dynamics-violation metric and extract a trusted convex inner approximation using an IRIS-inspired inflation procedure directly in action space. The resulting PAS are reusable convex families of actions that can be queried and composed with linear programs, and a PAS-guided tree expansion treats nodes as composed reachable families rather than single trajectories, coupling local nonlinear fidelity with convex reuse for longer-horizon planning. The planner solves cluttered planar scenes in tens of milliseconds (14-78x faster than a kinodynamic RRT baseline) and reduces terminal error on a nonlinear underactuated benchmark by 26-86% over sampling and NLP baselines.

</details>


### [272] [On the Reduced Rank Hypersurface Germ Condition for Near-Controllability of Bilinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24029)
*Wenyu Zhao,Lin Tie*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文指出现有关于双线性系统近可控性验证中"降秩超曲面芽条件（RRHGC）"算法存在遗漏特殊情况的缺陷，并提出基于Darboux多项式刻画的新算法，用于全面检测RRHGC，并给出示例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统的可控性研究通常依赖李代数方法，但局部可控性容易证明，全局可控性（即使对双线性系统）通常难以获得。近期发展的新方法通过检查双线性项对应的降秩点是否形成超平面或超曲面来代数验证全局近可控性。然而，现有检查RRHGC的算法存在缺陷，遗漏了一些特殊情况，需要修正。

Method: 作者首先揭露原算法在处理降秩超曲面芽条件时的漏洞（某些特殊情况被忽略），随后提出一种基于Darboux多项式刻画的新算法来检测RRHGC，该算法能够处理所有情形，并通过一个具体示例展示新算法的有效性。

Result: 论文提出了一个全新的、基于Darboux多项式特征的RRHGC检测算法，弥补了原有算法的缺陷，能够覆盖所有特殊情况，并通过示例验证了新算法的正确性和适用性。

Conclusion: 现有的RRHGC检测算法存在缺陷，不能覆盖所有特殊情况；本文提出的基于Darboux多项式的新算法能够全面、正确地检测RRHGC，为双线性系统全局近可控性的代数验证提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Controllability of nonlinear systems has been extensively studied by using the Lie algebra methods. Although local controllability can be proved, global controllability is in general hard to obtain even for bilinear systems. Recently, a new approach is developed to study controllability of bilinear systems by checking whether the reduced rank points corresponding to the bilinear terms form hyperplanes or hypersurfaces, so that near-controllability, in the global sense, can be algebraically verified once no hypersurface exists. In this paper, we show that there is a flaw in the algorithm for checking the reduced rank hypersurface germ condition (RRHGC) that some special cases are overlooked. We thus propose a new algorithm for testing the RRHGC based on a Darboux-polynomial characterization, which can be used to deal with all cases. An example is provided to demonstrate the proposed new algorithm.

</details>


### [273] [Event-Triggered Pinning Impulsive Control of Complex Networks with Actuation Delays: Stability Analysis and Zeno-Free Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24074)
*Ethan Astri,Hamza Saleem,Kexue Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在执行延迟的事件触发脉冲固定控制下复杂网络的镇定问题。通过构造Lyapunov函数分析延迟区间内的网络动态，推导出显式延迟相关的充分条件以保证渐近稳定性，并建立了事件间隔的正下界以排除Zeno行为。同时提出基于网络拉普拉斯谱条件的选择固定节点的拓扑判据，并通过耦合Chua电路网络仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事件触发脉冲控制方案通常假设脉冲在事件检测后瞬时执行，但在实际工程中，事件检测到脉冲执行之间必然存在执行延迟。忽略这一延迟可能导致控制性能下降甚至不稳定，因此需要显式地考虑执行延迟对网络镇定问题的影响。

Method: 1) 构建合适的Lyapunov函数，分析延迟区间内的网络动态行为；2) 推导延迟依赖的充分条件以保证渐近稳定性，刻画网络拓扑、执行延迟、脉冲控制增益与触发参数之间的相互作用；3) 建立事件间隔严格正下界以排除Zeno行为；4) 通过网络拉普拉斯矩阵的谱条件提出选择固定节点的拓扑判据；5) 在耦合Chua电路网络上进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 得到了延迟依赖的渐近稳定性充分条件，建立了排除Zeno行为的事件间隔正下界，提出了基于谱条件的固定节点选择准则，并通过耦合Chua电路网络的数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性和设计流程的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过显式建模执行延迟，弥补了现有事件触发脉冲控制方案的不足，为实际系统中存在执行延迟的网络镇定问题提供了理论保证和实用设计指导，同时保证了控制方案的工程可实现性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the stabilization of complex networks via event-triggered pinning impulsive control in the presence of actuation delays. Unlike existing event-triggered impulsive control schemes that assume instantaneous implementation, the proposed framework explicitly accounts for the delay between event detection and impulse execution. By constructing suitable Lyapunov functions and analyzing the network dynamics during the delay intervals, explicit delay-dependent sufficient conditions are derived to guarantee asymptotic stability. The obtained conditions characterize the interplay among network topology, actuation delays, impulsive control gains, and triggering parameters. In addition, a strictly positive lower bound on inter-event times is established, which excludes Zeno behavior and ensures practical implementability. A topology-based criterion for selecting pinned nodes is also developed through a spectral condition on the network Laplacian. Numerical simulations on a network of coupled Chua circuits illustrate the design procedure and verify the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [274] [LLM-Guided Contextual Action Evaluation for Operational Decisions in Industrial Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24156)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Dakuo He*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出LCAE方法，利用大语言模型在训练前将固定工业文档规范化为"动作-观测-方向-延迟"关系基，将文档语义融入最大熵策略学习中，用于工业过程中的操作决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业actor-critic方法通常将连续动作表示为匿名的数值坐标，需要从有限交互中学习每个动作影响哪些过程变量、影响方向以及延迟时间。而固定的工业文档已描述了部分这些关系，但其开放式文本陈述既无法表示当前运行条件，也不能直接拟合数值策略，造成知识浪费。

Method: LCAE在训练前使用LLM将固定文档规范化为冻结的"动作-观测-方向-延迟"关系基。近期数值动作-响应历史用于调节每个关系的当前强度，被评估的动作形成状态条件化的非线性动作-效果场。Critic通过该场评估动作，actor使用相同关系增益生成动作，使文档语义成为最大熵策略学习的一部分。训练和部署期间LLM与嵌入模型均不运行。

Result: 该方法提出一个可证伪的假设：当文档化关系正确且近期历史反映其上下文强度时，该动作表示应比原始动作坐标提供更有用的决策偏差。

Conclusion: LCAE将固定文档的知识离线转化为语义工件并融入策略学习，部署时仅需冻结的语义工件和可见数值历史，无需在线运行LLM，有望提升工业强化学习的样本效率与决策质量。

Abstract: Industrial actor--critic methods usually represent continuous actions as anonymous numerical coordinates. They must therefore learn from limited interactions which process variables each action affects, in which direction, and after what delay. Fixed industrial documents already describe part of these relations, but their open-text statements neither represent the current operating condition nor directly fit a numerical policy. This article presents LLM-Guided Contextual Action Evaluation for Operational Decisions in Industrial Processes (LCAE), which uses a large language model before training to normalize fixed documents into a frozen action--observation--direction--delay relation basis. Recent numerical action--response history then modulates the current strength of each relation, while the evaluated action forms a state-conditioned nonlinear action-effect field in the same basis. The critic evaluates actions through this field, and the actor uses the same relation gains to generate actions, making document semantics part of maximum-entropy policy learning. Neither the LLM nor the embedding model runs online during training or deployment; the deployed policy uses only frozen semantic artifacts and visible numerical history. The method states a falsifiable hypothesis: when documented relations are correct and recent history reflects their contextual strength, this action representation should provide a more useful decision bias than raw action coordinates.

</details>


### [275] [The Flat Earth Error: Differential Geometry in Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24280)
*David J. N. Limebeer,Charl van de Merwe*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文阐述了将道路表面理想化为水平平面所引入的"平面地球误差"，并提出使用经典微分几何来建模曲面上的车辆运动。通过度量张量、第二基本形式、形状算子和Christoffel符号表征道路曲率，并以椭圆锥为基准示例，用MATLAB脚本构建测地线和曲面粒子动力学仿真。该方法被整合到单轨车辆-轨道模型的伪谱最优控制框架中，并在Darlington赛道的LiDAR数据上进行最小圈时轨迹优化，揭示了真实赛道几何诱导的显著三维动力学效应，尤其对高倾角NASCAR椭圆形赛道至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统车辆动力学仿真通常将路面理想化为水平平面，这在真实赛道（尤其是高倾角赛道）上会引入显著误差（"Flat Earth Error"）。作者旨在提供一种基于经典微分几何的严格框架来建模曲面上的车辆运动，从而提升车辆动力学仿真的保真度。

Method: 论文采用经典微分几何工具（度量张量、第二基本形式、形状算子、Christoffel符号）刻画曲面曲率；使用MATLAB脚本以椭圆锥为例构建测地线和曲面粒子动力学仿真；将几何结构整合到单轨车辆-轨道模型的伪谱最优控制框架中，并通过hp自适应配点法在Darlington赛道的LiDAR高密度点云所建立的高保真路面模型上求解最小圈时最优控制问题。

Result: 优化得到的车速和轮胎滑移率曲线表明，真实赛道的几何形状会诱发动力学上显著的三维效应，包括轮胎非光滑牵引饱和极限以及随位置变化的重力作用力。这些效应在高度倾斜的赛道表面（如NASCAR椭圆形赛道）上尤其重要。

Conclusion: 微分几何、多体力学与最优控制的整合是实现高保真有人驾驶与自动驾驶车辆动力学仿真的必要条件。忽略路面曲率的传统平面假设会导致性能受限场景下显著的仿真误差。

Abstract: The widespread idealization of road surfaces as horizontal planes can introduce significant inaccuracies into vehicle dynamics simulations, a phenomenon termed the ``Flat Earth Error.'' This article provides an expository guide to the use of classical differential geometry to model vehicular motion on curved surfaces. The influence of road curvature is characterized using the metric tensor, the second fundamental form, the shape operator, and the Christoffel symbols. Reproducible MATLAB scripts using an elliptic cone as a benchmark example illustrate the construction of geodesic curves and the simulation of particle dynamics on curved surfaces. The resulting geometric structures are integrated into a single-track vehicle-and-track model within a pseudospectral optimal control framework. Trajectory optimization results over a high-density mobile LiDAR profile of Darlington Raceway are used to generate a high-fidelity road-surface model. This model is used within an hp-adaptive collocation framework to investigate minimum lap time optimal control vehicular trajectories. These computations capture the non-smooth traction saturation limits of the tyres alongside position-dependent variations in gravitational forcing. Integrating differential geometry, multibody mechanics, and optimal control is essential for high-fidelity driven and autonomous vehicle-dynamics simulations. The optimized velocity and tyre slip profiles show that real-world racing track geometries induce dynamically significant three-dimensional effects. These results are particularly relevant to performance-limited simulations on highly banked track surfaces such as NASCAR ovals.

</details>


### [276] [Cognitive Energy Management: Concept,Framework, and Demonstration in Smart Port Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24284)
*Hafiz Majid Hussain,Wajiha Samer,Juhaa Haakana*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出认知能源管理（CEM）概念框架，在能源互联网（EI）的赛博物理架构基础上，将目标导向推理和持续适应能力嵌入能源管理环路，使能源系统从被动优化走向主动认知治理，并通过智慧港口船舶周转预测场景的玩具问题进行实践验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源管理系统即使在先进的能源互联网基础设施中仍是反应式、优化受限的，缺乏对情境、意图和不确定性的推理能力。EI范式虽建立了强大的赛博物理架构，但如何让能源系统"思考、适应和智能决策"仍是未解难题，因此需要一种新的认知层框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 首先形式化定义CEM框架，将其与基于规则和基于优化的传统范式进行结构化对比，阐述其核心架构层次。随后以智慧港口（最具操作挑战性的EI节点环境之一）为背景构建玩具问题：模拟船舶周转预测场景，让CEM系统在六小时操作时间窗内规划能源采购、储能预充电和负荷调度。

Result: 演示表明CEM系统能够将EI从仅仅寻求可行性的范式提升至智能的、具有前瞻性的能源治理层面，能够在复杂操作环境中实现感知、推理、学习与行动的闭环，有效处理船舶周转预测等动态场景下的能源规划问题。

Conclusion: CEM将认知智能引入能源管理领域，作为EI基础设施的认知治理层，克服了传统能源管理在情境理解和不确定性处理上的根本局限，为未来能源系统向主动、预判式治理演进提供了理论框架和实践路径。

Abstract: Modern energy management systems, even within advanced energy internet (EI) infrastructures, remain fundamentally reactive, optimization-bound, and incapable of reasoning about context, intent, or uncertainty. While the EI paradigm has established a powerful cyber-physical architecture for interconnecting distributed energy resources via software-defined packetized networks, the question of how such systems should think, adapt, and govern energy decisions intelligently remains an open challenge. This paper introduces cognitive energy management (CEM); a new conceptual framework that addresses this gap by redefining how energy systems perceive, reason, learn, and act within complex operational environments. Grounded in the EI cyber-physical foundation, CEM extends beyond conventional optimization by embedding goal-directed reasoning and continuous adaptation into the energy management loop, positioning itself as the cognitive governance layer of EI-based infrastructures. We formally define CEM, distinguish it from rule-based and optimization-based paradigms through structured comparison, and articulate its core architectural layers. To demonstrate the framework's practical value, we develop a toy problem grounded in smart port energy management; one of the most operationally demanding EI node environments in modern infrastructure. Specifically, we model a predictive vessel turnaround scenario in which a CEM-enabled system plans energy procurement, storage pre-charging, and load scheduling across a six-hour operational horizon. The demonstration illustrates how CEM moves the EI beyond feasibility-seeking toward intelligent, anticipatory energy governance.

</details>


### [277] [Electric Vehicle Charging Right Trading: Concept, Mechanism, and Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24296)
*Ruike Lyu,Yuxuan Gu,Qixin Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电动汽车充电权（CR）交易机制（CRM），通过预先购买充电权来减少充电等待时间，并利用排队论估计等待时间，从而缓解充电站拥堵问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车普及率提高，无协调的充电行为导致充电站出现混乱、无序和长时间等待的问题，亟需一种机制来引导电动汽车优化充电行为并减少等待时间。

Method: 定义充电权（CR）为预约特定充电服务的权利，提出CR交易机制（CRM），允许电动汽车提前购买CR；利用排队论进行等待时间估计，并首次考虑充电站排队状态公开对估计的影响；CR价格用于引导充电行为并更新充电价格。

Result: 仿真结果验证了等待时间估计的准确性以及所提机制在减少等待时间和缓解充电拥堵方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CR交易机制能够有效减少电动汽车充电等待时间并缓解充电站拥堵，排队状态公开的考虑为等待时间估计提供了更准确的依据。

Abstract: With the increasing penetration of electric vehicles (EVs), uncoordinated EV charging and the resulting chaos, disorder, and long waiting times at EV charging stations (EVCSs) will no longer be tolerable. An EV charging right (CR) is the right to reserve a predefined charging service. By purchasing CRs, EVs can reduce their charging waiting time, and the price of CRs can guide EVs toward optimized charging behaviors. In this article, we define CR, propose the CR trading mechanism (CRM), and analyze the effect of CRM on reducing waiting times and mitigating congestion in EV charging. In the proposed CRM, EVs can purchase CRs in advance, and the CRs are used to estimate the waiting time and update the price of charging. Queue theory is utilized in the waiting time estimation, in which the impact of disclosing queue states at EVCSs is considered for the first time. The simulation results verify the accuracy of the waiting time estimation and the effect of the proposed mechanism.

</details>


### [278] [Data-Driven Dimension Reduction for Industrial Load Modeling Using Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24390)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Goran Strbac,Chongqing Kang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的降维方法，用于工业负荷建模，通过使用工业负荷的最优能耗数据训练降维模型，使其最佳拟合原始约束条件，并以可调负荷车队模型实现，在三个工业负荷数据集上优于解析降维方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业负荷模型中的混合整数约束使其难以直接集成到经济调度或市场出清过程中，且现有的解析降维方法在处理这类约束时效果不佳，因此需要一种新的降维方法来解决这一难题。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的降维方法，利用工业负荷的最优能耗数据来训练一个降维模型，使其能够最好地拟合原始约束条件，并通过可调负荷车队模型进行实现。

Result: 该方法在三个工业负荷数据集上均优于解析降维方法，验证了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的降维方法能够有效处理工业负荷模型中复杂的混合整数约束，为工业负荷参与经济调度和市场清空提供了一种可行且优于传统解析方法的解决方案。

Abstract: The intricate mixed-integer constraints in industrial load models not only pose challenges for their direct integration into economic dispatch or market clearing processes but also render current analytical dimension-reduction methods ineffective. We propose a novel data-driven dimension-reduction approach for industrial load modeling, which uses the optimal energy usage data from industrial loads to train a dimension-reduced model that best fits the original constraints. Our approach, implemented by the adjustable load fleet model, outperformed analytical methods across three industrial load datasets.

</details>


### [279] [Co-optimizing Bidding and Power Allocation of an EV Aggregator Providing Real-time Frequency Regulation Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24397)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Kedi Zheng,Mingyang Sun,Qixin Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个协同优化电动汽车聚合商（EVA）在调节市场中的投标决策与功率分配的框架。投标模型被建模为随机规划问题，其中嵌入了离散化调节信号场景下的功率分配；为满足在线求解时间要求，进一步提出利用投标问题中的拉格朗日乘子保证分配结果对应于投标问题最优解的在线功率分配模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车规模的快速扩张和电池成本下降使车网互动（V2G）服务成为现实。然而，EVA在调节市场中的投标和功率分配两个问题虽然相互耦合，但常被解耦独立优化，导致EVA利润下降。因此需要协同优化这两个问题以提升收益。

Method: 提出一个协同优化框架：(1) 投标模型采用随机规划建模，嵌入离散化调节信号场景下的功率分配；(2) 针对调节部署的实时求解需求，提出可在线求解的功率分配模型，利用投标问题中的拉格朗日乘子保证分配结果与投标问题最优解一致。

Result: 案例研究表明，所提出的框架能有效提升EVA利润并降低电池退化成本。

Conclusion: 协同优化EVA投标与功率分配能够显著提升聚合商经济效益，同时兼顾了在线求解的时间要求，为V2G调节市场服务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapidly expanding scale of electric vehicle (EV) fleets and continuously decreasing battery costs are making vehicle-to-grid services a reality. In this paper, we study the interaction between the problems of an EV aggregator's bidding in the regulation market and power allocation (i.e., determining the (dis)charging powers of the EVs in regulation deployment). Although the two problems are coupled, they are often regarded as decoupled and optimized separately for complexity issues. However, failing to consider the coupling of bidding and power allocation can lead to a decline in the profit of the EV aggregator (EVA). In this paper, we propose a framework for co-optimizing EVA bidding and power allocation in the regulation market. The bidding model is formulated as a stochastic programming problem with embedded power allocation in discretized regulation signal scenarios. To meet the solution time requirement for regulation deployment, we further propose a power allocation model that can be solved online. It utilizes the Lagrange multipliers from the bidding problem to ensure that the allocation results correspond to the optimal solution of the bidding problem. The effect of the proposed framework on improving EVA profits and reducing degradation costs is verified in the case study.

</details>


### [280] [A kernel proof of the De Cock-De Moor Lyapunov identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24405)
*Jonas Gillberg,Johan Löfberg*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文证明了2004年控制系统与控制理论未解问题集中记录为Problem 9.1的秩一Lyapunov谱恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数学系统与控制理论中一个长期未解的公开问题（Problem 9.1），即证明与矩阵A及其秩一更新A2=A+vw^⊤相关联的离散Lyapunov和Sylvester方程解之间的谱恒等关系。

Method: 通过秩一行列式因子分解得到标量双线性核，在A的特征值及其倒数处求值推导出矩阵恒等式，再利用多项式延拓（polynomial continuation）将恒等式从非空开集推广到整个可容许域。

Result: 证明了P^{-1}RQ^{-1}R^⊤与(I+PQ)^{-1}具有相同的特征多项式；当A和A2的谱不相交时，Z=b(A)^{-1}P给出显式相似变换；在Schur稳定实现设定下，恢复主角度和过去/未来典型相关谱。

Conclusion: 完整解决了Problem 9.1，无需稳定性假设即可获得行列式推论，并将结果应用到Schur稳定系统的谱分析中。

Abstract: We prove the rank-one Lyapunov spectral identity recorded as Problem 9.1 in the 2004 collection of unsolved problems in mathematical systems and control theory. Let $P,Q,R$ solve the coupled discrete Lyapunov and Sylvester equations associated with $A$ and its rank-one update $A_2=A+vw^\top$. When the displayed inverses exist, we show that $P^{-1}RQ^{-1}R^\top$ and $(I+PQ)^{-1}$ have the same characteristic polynomial. A rank-one determinant factorization of the equation for $Q$ produces a scalar bilinear kernel. Evaluating it at the eigenvalues of $A$ and at their reciprocals gives $RQ^{-1}R^\top=BQ^{-1}B=P-BPB$, after which the two target matrices are the same two factors in opposite order. Polynomial continuation extends the identity from a nonempty open set of admissible systems to the full admissible domain and yields a determinant corollary without stability assumptions; when the spectra of $A$ and $A_2$ are disjoint, $Z=b(A)^{-1}P$ gives an explicit similarity. In the Schur-stable realization setting, the result recovers the associated principal-angle and past/future canonical-correlation spectra.

</details>


### [281] [Autonomous path following using data-driven predictive control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24540)
*Josip Kir Hromatko,Marko Švec,Šandor Ileš*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于数据驱动预测控制（而非物理模型）在车辆动力学控制和自主路径跟踪中的应用。通过将路径跟踪问题转化到误差空间，使系统近似线性化，从而可以应用现有的数据驱动预测控制方法，并可根据纵向速度进行调度。该算法在两种不同换道工况的高保真仿真环境中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的预测控制依赖于物理模型，而基于信息性系统轨迹的数据驱动预测控制近年受到关注。本文的动机是探索这种无需物理模型的数据驱动控制方法在车辆动力学控制与自主路径跟踪中的潜力。

Method: 将路径跟踪问题转化到误差空间中，使底层系统近似为线性系统，从而可直接应用已有的数据驱动预测控制结果。此外，基于纵向速度进行调度（scheduling）以处理速度变化带来的系统参数变化。

Result: 所提出的控制算法在两种不同的换道工况下，于高保真仿真环境中进行了测试，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动预测控制作为一种无需物理模型的替代方案，在车辆路径跟踪控制中具有可行性，通过误差空间转化和速度调度机制可有效应用于自主驾驶场景。

Abstract: Predictive control based on an informative system trajectory, instead of a physics-based model, has received significant attention in recent years. This paper investigates the potential of using such data-driven control for vehicle dynamics control and autonomous path following. By considering the path following problem in the error space, the underlying system is approximately linear and existing results for data-driven predictive control can be applied. Also, scheduling based on longitudinal speed can be readily included. The proposed control algorithm was tested on two different lane change maneuvers in a high-fidelity simulation environment.

</details>


### [282] [Adaptive robust tracking control with active learning for linear systems with ellipsoidal bounded uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.03727)
*Xuehui Ma,Shiliang Zhang,Yushuai Li,Fucai Qian,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对线性不确定系统的鲁棒跟踪控制问题，提出了一种能够主动学习椭球不确定集的自适应鲁棒控制方法。该方法利用递归集员状态估计来学习椭球集，并基于学习到的集合构建带一步预测的鲁棒跟踪控制律，通过二阶锥规划求解优化问题，同时通过最大化椭球体积来促进主动学习、加速不确定性缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性不确定系统中未知参数和扰动通常被界定在椭球集内，但传统方法通常假设这些椭球集是固定且已知的。本文的动机在于如何让系统在控制过程中主动学习并更新这些不确定集合，从而减轻不确定性对控制性能的影响，提升系统跟踪控制的鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 1) 利用递归集员状态估计（recursive set-membership state estimation）来学习椭球不确定集；2) 基于学习到的集合构建带一步预测的鲁棒输出跟踪控制律；3) 将优化目标重构为二阶锥规划（SOCP）问题，使其可高效求解；4) 通过最大化椭球集体积来丰富学习信息，促进主动学习，提高学习精度并加速不确定性缩减。

Result: 通过数值仿真，将固定椭球集鲁棒控制与所提出的主动学习控制方法进行对比，验证了所设计主动学习机制在不确定系统控制过程中的积极作用，表明该方法能有效改善跟踪控制性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的主动学习椭球集的鲁棒控制方法，将学习过程与控制过程有机结合，通过最大化椭球体积激励主动探索，在计算友好的SOCP框架下实现了对线性不确定系统更优的鲁棒跟踪控制，证明了主动学习在不确定系统控制中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the robust tracking control of linear uncertain systems, whose unknown system parameters and disturbances are bounded within ellipsoidal sets. We propose an adaptive robust control that can actively learn the ellipsoid sets. Particularly, the proposed approach utilizes the recursive set-membership state estimation in learning the ellipsoidal sets, aiming at mitigating uncertainties in the system control. Upon the learned sets representing the recognized uncertainties, we construct a robust control with one-step prediction for system output tracking. In deriving an optimized control law, we reformulate the optimization objective into a second-order cone programming problem that can be solved in a computationally friendly way. To further stimulate the active learning of uncertainties over the control procedures, we enrich the information used for the learning by maximizing the volume of the ellipsoid set, supposed to lead to increased learning accuracy and accelerated uncertainty reduction. To verify our approach, we conduct numerical simulations to compare the fixed-ellipsoidal-set robust control with ours, and investigate the positive effect of the designed active learning in the uncertain system control process.

</details>


### [283] [Partial Observation Amplifies Model Mismatch in MAP Estimation via Information-Curvature Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24550)
*Junsei Ito,Yasuaki Wasa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了系统模型失配如何影响部分观测受控动态系统中有限时域最大后验（MAP）初值估计的位移，提出将MAP位移分解为模型侧失配注入与估计侧曲率抵抗，并识别出一个依赖于传感器的信息曲率裕度作为放大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受控动态系统的初值估计中，系统模型失配会显著影响MAP估计的准确性，但现有研究缺乏对这种位移机理的理论分解与分析，尤其是对传感器配置与失配鲁棒性之间关系的理解尚不清晰。

Method: 采用路径敏感性分析，将初值标称-预言机位移（MAP shift）分解为模型侧失配注入与估计侧曲率抵抗两部分；通过识别传感器相关的信息曲率裕度作为放大瓶颈，并将其与标称Gauss-Newton曲率及贝叶斯Fisher信息矩阵建立联系；提出了非线性系统中的可计算标称代理，并给出线性时不变情形的显式表达式。

Result: 发现信息曲率裕度由最弱的后验曲率方向决定，因此最大化聚合信息的传感器配置仍可能对失配脆弱；在非线性系统中提供了可计算的标称代理，在LTI情形下得到显式形式，并通过两个数值算例验证。

Conclusion: 该工作区分了实例级失配鲁棒性与设计时可推断性，揭示了信息量最大化并不等同于失配鲁棒性，为传感器设计与模型失配缓解提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper theoretically analyzes how system model mismatch displaces finite-horizon maximum a posteriori (MAP) initial-state estimates in controlled dynamical systems under partial observation. From pathwise sensitivity analysis, the initial-state nominal-oracle displacement called MAP shift is decomposed into a model-side mismatch injection and an estimator-side curvature resistance to identify a sensor-dependent information-curvature margin as the amplification bottleneck. The margin is governed by the weakest posterior-curvature direction, so that sensor configurations that maximize aggregate information can still be fragile to mismatches. We connect the margin to nominal Gauss-Newton curvature and to the Bayesian Fisher information matrix, distinguishing instance-wise mismatch robustness from design-time inferability. The margin admits a computable nominal proxy in nonlinear systems, becomes explicit in the linear time-invariant case, and is validated through two numerical examples.

</details>


### [284] [Dual-Based Weight Selection for Approximate Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24629)
*Su Li,Andre A. Cire,Adam Diamant,Vahid Sarhangian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对偶的近似线性规划（ALP）方法，利用对偶解中的投影占用信息构建平滑随机策略并自适应更新状态相关权重，避免了传统主流方法中重复的贪心策略构造，同时证明了权重与诱导策略折扣占用量匹配的条件、唯一性及全局收敛性，并在排队和调度问题上验证了其高效性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALP在大规模MDP中广泛使用，但其性能对状态相关权重的选取敏感，而权重通常依赖启发式选择。现有性能界暗示权重应与诱导策略的折扣占用量对齐，现有主流方法虽通过重复贪心策略构造来实现这一目标，但缺乏收敛保证且计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出一种基于对偶的方法：利用ALP对偶解中的投影占用量信息来构造平滑随机策略并更新状态相关权重，从而避免单独的贪心动作计算。作者建立了权重与诱导策略折扣占用量匹配的条件，并在适当平滑下证明唯一性和全局收敛性，同时推导了后验策略损失界。

Result: 在经典排队和多优先级调度问题上，所提方法降低了策略对固定权重的敏感性，在策略质量上与主流更新方法相当或更好，且计算成本更低。此外，实验表明当基函数足够有表达力时，自适应加权的价值最为显著。

Conclusion: 基于对偶的自适应权重更新方法为ALP提供了一种更高效、有收敛保证的替代方案，其有效性依赖于基函数的表达能力，为大规模MDP中的应用提供了新的实用工具。

Abstract: Approximate Linear Programming (ALP) is widely used for large-scale Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), but its performance can be sensitive to the choice of state-relevance weights, which are typically selected heuristically. Performance bounds suggest aligning these weights with the discounted occupancy measure of the induced policy, and existing primal approaches address this through repeated greedy-policy construction. Nonetheless, they lack convergence guarantees and are computationally expensive. We propose a dual-based method that uses projected occupancy information from the ALP dual solution to construct a smooth stochastic policy and update the state-relevance weights, which avoids separate greedy-action calculations. We establish conditions under which the weights match the discounted occupancy of the induced policy and prove uniqueness and global convergence under appropriate smoothing. We also derive an a posteriori policy-loss bound that separates error from the weighted Bellman residual, occupancy mismatch, and stochastic-versus-greedy disagreement. Experiments on classical queueing and multi-priority scheduling problems show that the proposed approach reduces sensitivity to fixed weights and achieves comparable or better policy quality than primal updates at lower computational cost. Finally, we show that adaptive weighting is most valuable when the basis functions are sufficiently expressive for occupancy information to influence the resulting policy.

</details>


### [285] [DeepONet-LSTM Neural Operator for Output Feedback Control of Reaction Diffusion PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24699)
*Jing Zhang,Jie Qi,Linglong Jiang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经算子的输出反馈边界镇定方法，用于反应扩散PDE系统。通过将经典的输出反馈反推设计重新表述为因果边界算子，并利用混合DeepONet-LSTM神经算子学习该算子，避免了求解控制核函数和观测核函数的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典输出反馈反推设计需要对每个反应系数求解控制和观测核方程，计算成本高昂且需要重新计算。作者希望避免这些核函数的在线求解，从而提出一种数据驱动/学习的方法来近似边界镇定控制律。

Method: 将输出反馈控制律转化为一个因果边界算子，该算子将反应系数和边界测量映射到边界控制输入。提出混合DeepONet-LSTM神经算子：DeepONet负责编码空间系数，LSTM负责捕捉测量历史的时间依赖性。分析了边界算子的Lipschitz连续性，证明了闭环系统的实际稳定性，并引入修改的损失函数以改善学习边界输入的时间正则性。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的神经算子控制器能够有效镇定反应扩散PDE系统。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了神经算子方法在PDE边界控制中的可行性，通过将核函数求解问题转化为学习问题，显著降低了计算成本，同时保证了闭环稳定性的理论保证。

Abstract: This paper presents a neural operator-based approach for the output feedback boundary stabilization of reaction diffusion PDEs. The classical output feedback backstepping design requires solving control and observer kernel equations for each reaction coefficient. To avoid computing these kernel functions, the output feedback control law is reformulated as a causal boundary operator that maps the reaction coefficient and the boundary measurement to the boundary control input. A hybrid DeepONet-LSTM neural operator is proposed to approximate this causal operator, where DeepONet encodes the spatial coefficient and LSTM captures the temporal dependence of the measurement history. We analyze the Lipschitz continuity of the boundary operator and prove the closed-loop practical stability with the learned controller. A modified loss is also introduced to improve the temporal regularity of the learned boundary input. Numerical results illustrate that the proposed neural operator controller effectively stabilizes the system.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Equilibrium in closed constant-function market maker economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23915)
*Muqiao Huang,Ruodu Wang,Yiyun Wang*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文研究了封闭、无费用的双资产、双交易者常数函数做市商（CFMM）经济中的均衡。内部状态是单边无交易均衡当且仅当CFMM边际价格等于两位交易者的边际替代率。固定均衡状态下可通过加权上卷积获得弱代表性主体，而状态无关的强代表性主体仅在交易者共享同质偏好时存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解CFMM（如Uniswap等去中心化交易所的核心机制）中的均衡性质至关重要，但已有文献对CFMM经济中双边交易者博弈均衡的理论性质研究不足，尤其是关于帕累托最优性、代表性主体存在性以及交易顺序优势等问题。

Method: 采用微观经济均衡分析框架，定义单边无贸易均衡条件，利用加权上卷积构造弱代表性主体，通过偏好同质性刻画强代表性主体的存在条件，并分析有限步可行性及交替效用最大化交易的收敛性，以及首轮交易中的先发优势条件。

Result: 1) 内部状态为单边无贸易均衡当且仅当CFMM边际价格与两交易者边际替代率一致；2) 内部初始状态下，个人理性单边均衡相对于固定CFMM不变量是帕累托最优的；3) 弱代表性主体通过加权上卷积在每个固定均衡下存在，强代表性主体仅当偏好同质时存在；4) 每个内部可行状态可通过有限次有效交易到达，交替效用最大化交易收敛到帕累托最优单边均衡；5) 推导了首轮交易中先发优势/劣势的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为CFMM经济提供了系统的均衡理论基础，明确了边际价格与边际替代率的均衡对齐条件，建立了帕累托最优性、代表性主体存在性与偏好结构之间的联系，并为交易收敛性与交易顺序效应提供了理论保证，对理解DEX机制设计和流动性提供有重要启示。

Abstract: We study equilibria in a closed, fee-free constant-function market maker (CFMM) economy with two assets and two traders. An interior state is a unilateral no-trade equilibrium exactly when the CFMM marginal price equals both traders' marginal rates of substitution. For an interior initial state, individually rational unilateral equilibria are Pareto optimal relative to the fixed CFMM invariant. A weak representative agent is obtained at each fixed equilibrium by weighted sup-convolution, whereas a state-independent strong representative agent exists exactly when traders share a common homothetic preference. Every interior feasible state is reachable through finitely many valid trades, and alternating utility-maximizing trades converge to a Pareto optimal unilateral equilibrium. We also derive conditions under which trading order produces a first-mover advantage or disadvantage in the first round.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Where Does Ethereum Validators' Money Go? A Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23577)
*Irene Aldridge*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于谱方法的去中心化测度新方法，从“目的地侧”（资金流向）而非传统的“起源侧”（挖矿/验证）来衡量区块链网络的集中度。通过将钱包间转账建模为马尔可夫链，并利用Perron-Frobenius定理计算稳态概率，识别长期终极接收账户。对76,855个以太坊钱包（四年挖矿数据）的分析发现，资金流最终汇聚到仅四个终端账户，且这些账户均不属于网络三大主导矿工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化度量几乎全部集中在起源侧，即衡量谁在挖矿或验证区块的集中度。本文认为这种测度忽略了价值的最终流向，无法全面反映真正的去中心化程度，因此需要一种从目的地侧衡量资金集中度的新方法。

Method: 将钱包到钱包的转账建模为马尔可夫链，利用Perron-Frobenius定理计算近实时的稳态概率，从而识别长期终端接收者（资金最终流向的账户）。该方法应用于76,855个以太坊钱包的四年挖矿数据。

Result: 研究发现资金流最终塌缩到仅四个终端账户。关键是，这四个资金目的地账户都不在网络三大可识别的高收入矿工之中，表明起源侧与目的地侧的集中度存在显著脱节。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了区块链去中心化的多维度特性：即使起源侧（挖矿）看似分散或集中于某些矿工，目的地侧的资金流集中度可能呈现完全不同的格局。作者提出的谱方法为衡量区块链去中心化提供了一种新颖的、更全面的补充视角。

Abstract: Existing decentralization measures are almost entirely origination-side, quantifying concentration in who mines or validates blocks. We introduce a spectral methodology measuring concentration on the destination side instead: where value ultimately flows once it leaves a validator wallet. Modeling wallet-to-wallet transfers as a Markov chain, we compute near-real-time steady-state probabilities via the Perron--Frobenius theorem to identify long-run terminal recipients. Applied to 76,855 Ethereum wallets from four years of mining data, fund flows collapse to just four terminal accounts. None of these fund destination accounts are among the network's three dominant identifiable revenue-earning miners.

</details>


### [288] [Principal Component Analysis for a Mix of Stationary and Nonstationary Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23732)
*James D. Hamilton,Xinwei Ma,Jin Xi*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，用于从混合平稳和非平稳变量的数据中提取共同的周期性因子，无需事先知道哪些变量是非平稳的或其非平稳性质。在FRED-MD宏观经济数据集上的应用表明，该方法在保持传统主成分分析优势的同时，还提供了额外的好处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因子提取方法（如主成分分析）通常假设变量具有相同的平稳性，但现实中宏观经济数据往往混合了平稳和非平稳变量。现有方法需要预先知道变量的非平稳性状态，这在实际应用中是不现实的，因此需要一种能自动适应混合平稳性数据的因子提取方法。

Method: 开发了一种新的程序，能够从平稳和非平稳变量的混合数据中发现共同的周期性因子。该方法的关键优势在于不需要预先知道哪些变量是非平稳的，也不需要了解非平稳性的具体形式（如趋势、单位根等）。

Result: 在FRED-MD宏观经济数据集上的应用表明，该方法能够提供与传统主成分分析相似的好处，同时还具备一些额外的优势，表明该方法在实际宏观经济数据分析中是有效且可用的。

Conclusion: 该方法为处理混合平稳性宏观经济数据提供了一种灵活且实用的因子提取方案，克服了传统方法对平稳性先验知识的依赖，具有广泛的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: This paper develops a procedure for uncovering the common cyclical factors that drive a mix of stationary and nonstationary variables. The method does not require knowing which variables are nonstationary or the nature of the nonstationarity. An application to the FRED-MD macroeconomic dataset demonstrates that the approach offers similar benefits to those of traditional principal component analysis with some added advantages.

</details>


### [289] [Accelerating the Adoption of Residential Solar Power Systems: Policy Analysis using a Dynamic Structural Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23796)
*Sebastián Souyris,Jason A. Duan,Anantaram Balakrishnan,Varun Rai*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个动态结构模型来研究住宅光伏（PV）系统的扩散，模型考虑前瞻性家庭的安装决策、投资回报率、邻居影响以及未观察到的异质性。基于奥斯汀市的家户数据用贝叶斯方法估计模型，并通过反事实政策模拟评估不同的回扣补贴设计，发现限时回扣比长期昂贵的补贴更有效，两阶段递减优于多次小幅递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府通过前期回扣和税收抵免鼓励家庭光伏采用，但预算有限，需要基于数据的、原则性的政策来理解采用的驱动因素以及激励措施对采用率的影响。

Method: 开发动态结构模型，基于前瞻性家庭在现在安装与推迟安装之间的经济权衡决策。模型考虑投资回报和邻居采用者的影响，按房屋价值和城市化水平细分家庭，纳入未观察到的异质性，并捕捉时空安装动态。使用贝叶斯方法和奥斯汀市的详细家庭层面数据进行估计，在动态均衡中模拟反事实政策来评估回扣设计。

Result: 限时回扣比长期更昂贵的计划产生更多的采用量和减排量；两阶段递减优于多次小幅递减；地理差异化改善政策绩效，而按房屋价值区分相对于统一回扣几乎没有优势。模型在样本外测试中比现有替代方法的预测更准确。

Conclusion: 回扣政策的设计应考虑前瞻性行为、邻居影响和限时效应。管理者应优先采用限时回扣和两步递减方案，并考虑地理差异化而非房屋价值差异化。该框架可适用于其他耐用技术的采用研究。

Abstract: Problem definition: Solar electricity generation is a strategic component of energy portfolios designed to meet growing demand and reduce carbon emissions. Governments and municipalities encourage household photovoltaic (PV) adoption through upfront rebates and tax credits. Limited budgets require principled, data-driven policies that account for the drivers of adoption and the effects of incentives on adoption rates.
  Methodology/results: We develop a dynamic structural model of residential PV diffusion based on adoption decisions by forward-looking households that weigh the economic trade-offs between installing now and later. Adoption depends on return on investment and influence from neighboring adopters. The model segments households by home value and urbanization level, incorporates unobserved heterogeneity, and captures spatiotemporal installation dynamics. We estimate the model using Bayesian methods and detailed household-level data from Austin, Texas. In out-of-sample tests, it predicts installations more accurately than contemporary alternatives. We simulate counterfactual policies within the dynamic equilibrium of PV diffusion to evaluate rebate designs. The framework can also be adapted to study the adoption of other durable technologies.
  Managerial implications: A rebate offered for a limited period generates more adoption and emissions reductions than a prolonged, costlier program. This counterintuitive result arises from forward-looking behavior, neighbor influence, and accelerated adoption before the rebate expires. We also evaluate phased reductions and rebates differentiated by household segment. A two-step reduction outperforms multiple small reductions. Geographic differentiation improves policy performance, whereas differentiation by home value offers little advantage over a uniform rebate.

</details>


### [290] [Repairing Locally Misspecified GMM: An Empirical Bayes Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23925)
*Patrick Kline*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究当矩条件被阶为n^{-1/2}的可交换设定误差污染时，广义矩方法（GMM）的估计与推断问题。作者发展了设定误差均值和方差的估计量，提出可行的偏差校正估计器和经验贝叶斯估计器，并建立渐近正态性以实现对误设定的频率推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计量经济模型是对数据生成过程的一种简洁但非精确的近似。在实际应用中，矩条件几乎不可能完全满足，存在设定误差。现有GMM框架通常假设矩条件精确成立，当存在可交换的设定误差时，标准估计会产生偏差。作者希望开发一套能够估计设定误差特征（均值和方差）并利用这些信息进行偏差校正和推断的方法。

Method: 1) 在过度识别约束数量随样本量增长的渐近框架下，发展设定误差均值和方差的一致估计量；2) 利用超参数估计构建可行的偏差校正估计器；3) 提出经验贝叶斯估计器，通过从偏差校正估计器中减去一阶估计误差的最佳线性预测来温和提升精度；4) 使用组合中心极限定理建立两个估计器的渐近正态性，并提供方差估计量以支持对误设定有意识的频率推断。

Result: 模拟实验表明，在存在排除性约束违背时，所提方法能显著优于标准两阶段最小二乘估计。在Angrist和Krueger (1991)的经典研究中，当使用可交换排除性违背合理的工具集时，对教育回报的2SLS估计进行修复后，估计值向OLS方向移动，且对控制变量设定的敏感性降低。

Conclusion: 本文为存在设定误差的GMM估计提供了一个完整的方法论框架，包括偏差校正和推断工具。实证结果表明该方法在实践中具有价值，能够有效修正工具变量估计中的排除性约束违背问题，使估计结果更加稳健。

Abstract: Econometric models offer parsimonious but inexact approximations to data-generating processes. This paper studies the generalized method of moments (GMM) when exchangeable specification errors of order $n^{-1/2}$ contaminate the moment conditions. I develop estimators for the mean and variance of these specification errors, establishing their consistency in an asymptotic framework where the number of overidentifying restrictions grows with the sample size. These hyperparameter estimates are used to develop a feasible bias-corrected estimator of target parameters. I also propose an empirical Bayes estimator that weakly improves precision by subtracting a best linear predictor of the first-order estimation error from the bias-corrected estimator. Using a combinatorial central limit theorem, I establish asymptotic normality of both estimators and provide variance estimators that enable misspecification-aware frequentist inference. Simulation exercises indicate the procedures can meaningfully improve on standard two-stage least squares estimation when exclusion violations are present. Revisiting the influential study of Angrist and Krueger (1991), I consider an instrument set where exchangeable excludability violations are plausible. Repairing the two-stage least squares estimates of the returns to schooling moves them in the direction of ordinary least squares and reduces sensitivity to the specification of controls.

</details>


### [291] [Estimation of Random-Coefficient Dynamic Panel Data Models with a Fixed T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23988)
*Xun Tang,Pei Yu*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究动态线性面板数据模型，其中滞后结果和严格外生协变量带有个体特定系数，时变误差具有灵活协方差结构。在固定时间期数下，基于严格外生性的分布形式，作者点识别了随机系数与结构误差的联合分布，并提出基于逆Radon变换的闭式多步估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态线性面板数据模型中，传统方法通常假设系数同质，但实际中个体异质性广泛存在。本文旨在放松这一假设，允许滞后结果和外生协变量携带个体特定（随机）系数，同时处理误差的灵活协方差结构，并解决固定T时段的识别与估计问题。

Method: 作者利用严格外生性的分布形式假设来点识别随机系数与结构误差的联合分布；提出基于逆Radon变换的闭式多步估计器；为随机系数密度估计器建立一致收敛速率，并为条件密度估计器建立一致一致性。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明估计器具有良好的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 本文在固定时间期数下成功实现了对随机系数动态面板模型的识别与估计，为处理个体异质性和复杂误差结构提供了理论基础和可操作的估计方法。

Abstract: We study dynamic linear panel data models in which the lagged outcomes and strictly exogenous covariates carry individual-specific coefficients and the time-varying errors have a flexible covariance structure. With a fixed number of time periods, we point-identify the joint distribution of the random coefficients and the structural errors under a distributional form of strict exogeneity, and propose a closed-form, multi-step estimator based on the inverse Radon transform. We establish a uniform convergence rate for the estimator of the random coefficient density, as well as uniform consistency of the estimator for the conditional density of the time-varying structural errors. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate good finite-sample performance of the estimators.

</details>


### [292] [Dynamic Discrete Choice and Inverse Reinforcement Learning: Inferring Preferences and Beliefs From Human Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24362)
*Pranjal Rawat,John Rust*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文综述了两个从不同学科传统出发但解决同一根本问题的文献：结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）和机器学习中的逆强化学习（IRL）。两者都在马尔可夫决策过程框架下，从序列行为中推断决策者的偏好。尽管独立起源，两个领域已趋同于相似的数学表述，本文比较了各自的方法，并指出深度融合的广阔前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDC（结构计量经济学）和IRL（机器学习）各自独立发展，但都旨在从观测到的序列行为中推断决策者的偏好。由于领域壁垒，两个领域的学者对彼此的工作了解不足，导致方法重复发展和错失交叉借鉴的机会。本文的动机是系统比较这两个领域，揭示其数学等价性，并促进跨领域融合。

Method: 本文采用综述与比较分析方法：（1）展示soft Q-learning（IRL主流框架）与含加性极值偏好冲击的DDC模型在数学上的等价性，包括相同的softmax（多项logit）选择概率和光滑贝尔曼方程；（2）对比两个领域的估计与计算方法——DDC侧重极大似然估计、条件选择概率估计器和策略迭代方法，而IRL发展出最大熵方法、对抗方法、基于深度神经网络的模型无关时序差分估计器等可扩展方案；（3）识别共同的基础性挑战：识别问题和维度灾难。

Result: 论文表明IRL与DDC在数学公式上已趋同，特别是指出结合时序差分学习与计量经济学经典两步法的模型无关IRL估计器，是连接两个领域的有前景方向。同时识别了两个领域共同面对的识别问题和维度灾难两大挑战。

Conclusion: DDC与IRL的跨领域融合（交叉融合）为两个领域的方法论进步提供了重大机遇，特别是在利用机器学习扩展性解决维度灾难、利用计量经济学识别理论解决结构性问题等方面存在广阔的合作空间。

Abstract: This article surveys two deeply connected literatures that approach the same fundamental problem from different disciplinary traditions: dynamic discrete choice (DDC) in structural econometrics and inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) in machine learning. Both seek to infer the preferences of decision makers from observed sequential behavior, assuming that individuals act to maximize an expected reward function within a dynamic, uncertain environment formalized as a Markov decision process (MDP).
  Despite independent origins, the two fields have converged on similar mathematical formulations. We show that the (soft Q-learning) framework now prevalent in IRL is closely related to DDC models under additive extreme value preference shocks, yielding the same softmax (multinomial logit) choice probabilities and smooth Bellman equations that underpin structural estimation in economics. We compare the estimation and computational methods developed in each field. DDC has emphasized maximum likelihood estimation, conditional choice probability estimators, and policy iteration methods. IRL has developed scalable alternatives, including maximum entropy methods, adversarial approaches, and model-free temporal difference estimators that extend to high-dimensional state spaces using deep neural networks. Model-free IRL estimators that combine temporal difference learning with classical two-step methods from econometrics represent a promising direction for bridging the two literatures. Both fields confront shared foundational challenges: the identification problem, whereby multiple reward functions can rationalize the same observed behavior, and the curse of dimensionality in solving the underlying MDP. We believe that cross-fertilization offers substantial opportunities for methodological progress in both fields.

</details>


### [293] [How does hazard exposure influence job choice? Evaluating time-dependent tradeoffs between salary and hazard risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24811)
*Richard Bernknopf,Leila Gonzales,Christpher Keane*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文通过离散选择模拟和Cox生存分析模型，研究薪资、犯罪率和自然灾害风险如何影响求职者的工作选择，发现薪资和犯罪率影响稳定，而自然灾害风险的作用随时间变化（早期增加淘汰概率，后期趋于中性或有利）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害作为一种非市场性不利因素，会影响个体的求职过程，产生负面的环境外部性和经济效率损失。理解这些因素如何影响工作选择对于劳动力市场研究和政策制定具有重要意义。

Method: 开发了一种"搜索-筛选"求职搜索方法，使用离散选择模拟，将工作决策过程建模为一系列淘汰事件，采用基于随机效用模型的Cox比例风险模型进行分析。还扩展了标准模型，允许自然灾害风险的影响随决策轮次变化。

Result: 薪资和犯罪率对工作淘汰具有稳定且经济直觉一致的影响（高薪降低淘汰可能性，高犯罪率增加淘汰可能性）；自然灾害风险具有时变效应，在早期决策轮次增加淘汰概率，但在后期阶段变为中性或有利。

Conclusion: 环境风险在求职者从初步筛选到最终选择的过渡过程中被不同地评估，这强调了将工作选择建模为多阶段过程的重要性。

Abstract: Natural hazards are a nonmarket disamenity that affects an individual's search for employment resulting in a negative environmental impact that produces an economic inefficiency. We develop a seek-and-screen job search approach that uses a discrete choice simulation to examine how salary, crime, and natural hazard risk influence job choice. We model the job decision process as a series of elimination events using a Cox hazard model grounded in a Random Utility Model. We use data from the discrete choice simulation to estimate both a standard proportional hazards model and an extended specification that allows the effect of natural hazard risk to vary across decision rounds. Individuals are exposed to the dynamics of a simulated job search as they make decisions between pairs of job offers in an adaptive learning process based on income, geography, crime level, and natural hazard attributes. The results of the job choice decisions provide the input to a statistical survival analysis. The results indicate that salary and crime exert stable and economically intuitive effects on job elimination, with higher salary reducing and higher crime increasing the likelihood of removal. In contrast, natural hazard risk exhibits a time-varying effect that increases the probability of elimination in early rounds but becomes neutral or favorable in later stages of the decision process. These findings suggest that environmental risk is evaluated differently as individuals transition from initial screening to final job selection, highlighting the importance of modeling job choice as a multi-stage process.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [On the Convergence Rate of the Duality Gap in the Generalized Frank-Wolfe Method Under the Weak Growth Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23767)
*Renbo Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了广义Frank-Wolfe方法中对偶间隙序列的收敛速率，在Peña (2023)提出的弱增长条件下，利用一个可能独立有趣的递归引理进行推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Frank-Wolfe方法是求解约束优化问题的重要算法，但其对偶间隙的收敛速率分析在较弱的条件下尚不完善。本文旨在填补在弱增长条件下对偶间隙序列收敛速率的理论空白。

Method: 作者通过提出并利用一个递归引理（recursion lemma）来分析广义Frank-Wolfe方法中对偶间隙序列的收敛行为，该分析在Peña (2023)提出的弱增长条件框架下进行。

Result: 在弱增长条件的特定设定下，得到了广义Frank-Wolfe方法对偶间隙序列的收敛速率结果。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了对广义Frank-Wolfe方法收敛性质的理解，所提出的递归引理可能对相关优化问题的分析具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: We analyze the convergence rate of certain sequence of the duality gaps in the generalized Frank-Wolfe method, under certain regime of the weak growth condition proposed in Peña (2023). Our analysis leverages a recursion lemma that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [295] [Let AEDs Move: Urban Mobility Enhanced Defibrillator Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23810)
*Bahar Dehqani Viniche,Sheng Liu,Nooshin Salari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用现有网约车车队携带移动AED（自动体外除颤器）来改善院外心脏骤停（OHCA）急救可及性的问题。基于纽约市和多伦多的真实数据，在总AED数量固定的前提下，比较了纯固定部署、纯移动部署及混合部署策略的效果。结果显示移动AED能显著提升响应速度和可靠性，但最优的固定/移动配比因城市而异：纽约市移动占比55-70%时最优（平均响应时间节省超2.5分钟），多伦多则趋向全移动配置（节省近4分钟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共除颤系统中固定AED网络无法适应OHCA需求在时间和空间上的动态变化，导致急救响应不及时，亟需更具适应性的AED部署方案。

Method: 构建平台驱动的移动AED模型，利用网约车车队携带AED；在总AED供给量固定的约束下，比较纯固定、纯移动及混合部署策略；整合纽约和多伦多的地理参考AED清单、EMS报告的OHCA事件数据及网约车出行数据进行分析。

Result: 移动AED显著改善响应时间和可靠性，但收益取决于固定与移动部署的配比。纽约市平均响应时间节省超2.5分钟，移动占比55-70%时最优，超出该区间收益下降；多伦多平均节省近4分钟，并随系统趋近全移动配置而趋于平台期。

Conclusion: 移动AED能实质性地改善急救可及性，但最优的固定与移动AED配比需根据各城市当地的覆盖条件和移动性特点进行定制，不存在放之四海而皆准的统一方案。

Abstract: Timely access to automated external defibrillators (AEDs) remains a major limitation of public-access defibrillation systems because stationary AED networks cannot adapt to spatial and temporal variation in out-of-hospital cardiac arrest (OHCA) demand. We study a platform-enabled mobile AED model in which AEDs are carried by existing urban ride-hailing fleets that can be dispatched to OHCA locations. Rather than assuming additional AED capacity, we hold the total AED supply fixed and compare alternative allocations between stationary and mobile deployment, including hybrid policies that retain both forms of coverage. Our analysis leverages real-world urban data from New York City and Toronto, integrating georeferenced public AED inventories, EMS-reported OHCA incident data, and ride-hailing mobility data. The results show that mobile AEDs can substantially improve both response times and reliability, but the benefits depend on how AED capacity is divided between stationary and mobile deployment. In New York City, average response-time gains exceed 2.5 minutes and peak when approximately 55 to 70 percent of AED capacity is mobile; beyond this range, both gains and reliability decline as stationary coverage is reduced. In Toronto, average gains approach 4 minutes and then plateau as the system moves toward a fully mobile configuration. These contrasting patterns show that mobile AEDs can materially improve access, but the best mix of stationary and mobile AEDs must be tailored to local coverage and mobility conditions.

</details>


### [296] [Dimensionless Controls of Plasticity Under Alternating Tasks: From Evolutionary Biology to Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23889)
*Owen Skriloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究持续学习中环境变化下的可塑性，通过将生物学中的可塑性控制因素翻译到梯度下降的深度学习框架中，发现系统简化为两个无量纲控制参数（任务分歧r和reach ηT），并推导出可塑性的两个界，通过9,720条轨迹的ANOVA验证，提出仅从任务分歧即可设定最优reach的经验法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可塑性在进化生物学和持续学习中都是核心问题，但生物学中的可塑性控制机制是否适用于深度学习尚不清楚。作者希望通过基因型-表型映射的启发，在最小深度学习模型上研究生物学控制因素在梯度下降下的存活性。

Method: 以交替训练两种布尔标签集的深度网络为最小系统，将四种生物学可塑性因素重新解释为训练动力学量，将其归约为两个无量纲控制参数（任务分歧r和学习率与切换周期的乘积ηT）。推导可塑性的两个界：r单独决定乌托邦距离的几何下限，r和ηT共同约束遗忘。通过9,720条轨迹进行ANOVA验证各因素的主导性。

Result: ANOVA证实r、η、T主导可塑性，而中性集大小（生物学情境中强调的因素）影响可忽略。最优reach遵循近似逆幂律ηT*∝r^-1.18，可仅凭任务分歧设定最优reach。

Conclusion: 生物学到深度学习的可塑性类比中，存活的映射是动力学层面的而非几何层面的。该框架使可塑性研究能够借助物理学和工程学中驱动系统的视角进行理解。

Abstract: Plasticity under changing environments is central to both evolutionary biology and continual learning. Motivated by recent work on genotype--phenotype maps, we study a minimal deep-learning analogue where a network is trained alternately on two Boolean label sets, and ask which biological controls of plasticity survive the translation to gradient descent. Reinterpreting four proposed biological factors as quantities of training dynamics, we find the system reduces to two dimensionless controls: the task disagreement $r$, the fraction of disagreeing labels, and the reach $ηT$, the product of learning rate and switching period. We derive two bounds on plasticity: $r$ alone fixes an extremal geometric floor on the utopia distance, while $r$ and $ηT$ jointly bound forgetting. Across 9,720 trajectories, an ANOVA confirms that $r$, $η$, and $T$ dominate, while the effect of neutral-set size (emphasized in the biological setting) is negligible. The optimal reach itself follows an approximate inverse power law $ηT^{*}\propto r^{-1.18}$, yielding a heuristic that sets the optimal reach $ηT^*$ from the task disagreement alone. The analogy that survives is therefore dynamical rather than geometric, and our setting enables a view of plasticity through the lens of other driven systems in physics and engineering.

</details>


### [297] [The Shadow Price of Intelligence: Quality Degradation in LLM Inference as a Supply Chain Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23986)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文挑战了大模型提供商在算力受限时通过降级服务（路由到小模型、削减推理、截断上下文）来"省钱"的传统会计逻辑，指出该逻辑忽略了降级导致的失败重试与客户流失代价。作者用报童模型、几何重试乘子、两状态暂态队列三个经典原语建模推理分配，证明存在一个非空可测区域，其中更便宜的模型虽节省单位满意答案的能耗，却消耗更多单位满意答案的容量，折扣恰在容量受限时反转；动态上，激进的限流策略可能越过点火阈值反而制造更多流量，释放规则设置不当会把瞬时激增变成永久降级态；异构客户下，限流被转化为带重试膨胀负载的运输问题，最优策略按关键比率逐类配给智能，其对偶价格即智能的影子价格，可毫秒级计算。随机分析强化而非削弱论点。结论：拥塞下限流不是成本杠杆，而是需求杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型提供商面临计算资源约束，普遍采用降级服务（小模型、削减推理、截断上下文）来应对拥塞，行业会计认为这能省钱。作者指出这种会计是错误的，因为它把查询定价为客户购买答案的时刻，而一个降级答案可能失败，失败要么转为重试（在系统最拥塞时加剧到达），要么变成客户流失（摧毁终身价值，而该损失不在成本仪表盘上显示）。

Method: 作者用三个经典原语建模推理分配：(1) 报童模型，缺货成本为流失的终身价值；(2) 几何重试乘子，回收的产品是不满意度；(3) 两状态暂态队列，到达率由重试内生决定。结合静态分析、动态分析与随机分析，并在异构客户场景下将限流转化为运输问题，用关键比率逐类配给智能，通过影子价格定价边际查询。

Result: 静态上存在非空可测区域：更便宜的模型每满意答案节省能耗，却每满意答案消耗严格更多容量，折扣恰在容量受限时反转。动态上，激增期间触发的反应式限流可能越过点火阈值，制造的流量比削减的还多；设在降级均衡之下的释放规则会把瞬时激增转化为永久降级。异构客户下，最优限流是运输问题，其双偶影子价格可按类别和小时定价边际查询，闭式轨迹可毫秒级计算。随机分析下点火边界获得预期宽度，噪声惩罚把系统停在边界旁的反应式策略。

Conclusion: 在拥塞条件下，限流（throttling）不是成本杠杆而是需求杠杆。降级服务隐含着失败的代价（重试与流失），传统成本会计忽视了这一点，真正的优化应把智能配给作为需求管理而非单纯的成本削减手段。

Abstract: Large language model providers are compute constrained, and their universal response to congestion is to degrade service: route queries to smaller models, cut reasoning effort, truncate context. The industry's accounting says this saves money. We show the accounting is wrong, because it prices a query when the customer buys an answer. A degraded answer fails with some probability, and a failed answer either returns as a retry, inflating arrivals when the system is most loaded, or departs as churn, destroying lifetime value on a ledger no cost dashboard displays. We model inference allocation with three classical primitives: a newsvendor whose stockout cost is churned lifetime value, a geometric retry multiplier in which the recycled product is dissatisfaction, and a two-regime transient queue whose arrival rate is made endogenous by retries. Statically, there is a nonempty, measurable regime in which a cheaper model saves energy per satisfied answer while consuming strictly more capacity per satisfied answer, so the discount inverts exactly when capacity binds. Dynamically, a reactive throttle fired during a surge can cross an ignition threshold beyond which it manufactures more traffic than it sheds, and a release rule set below the degraded equilibrium converts a transient surge into a permanent degraded regime. With heterogeneous customers, throttling is a transportation problem in retry-inflated load whose optimal policy rations intelligence by critical ratio, class by class, and whose dual, the shadow price of intelligence, prices a marginal query by class and by hour; closed-form trajectories make it computable in milliseconds. Stochastic analysis sharpens rather than erodes the thesis: the ignition boundary acquires a predicted width, and noise punishes the reactive policy that parks the system against it. Under congestion, throttling is not a cost lever but a demand lever.

</details>


### [298] [Existence of Relaxed Equilibrium for Time-Inconsistent Mean Field Games: A Set-Valued Fixed Point Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24016)
*Zhenhua Wang,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域连续时间时间不一致平均场博弈中松弛均衡的存在性。作者在松弛反馈策略与种群流的乘积空间上直接工作，利用Young测度的稳定拓扑和Wasserstein连续流的紧凸集，通过建立辅助值函数的Sobolev和Hölder估计及其稳定性，结合Fokker-Planck方程的对偶论证，构造非空紧凸值的集值最优响应/一致性映射，最终应用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理证明了松弛均衡的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致平均场博弈中均衡的存在性是一个重要但困难的问题。由于时间不一致性导致经典动态规划方法失效，且策略空间缺乏紧性，直接证明均衡存在性面临挑战。作者通过引入松弛均衡（relaxed equilibrium）的概念，利用Young测度紧化策略空间，为这一难题提供了解决方案。

Method: 作者在松弛反馈策略（Young测度）与种群流（Wasserstein连续流，满足矩和时间正则性界）的乘积空间上工作。关键技术包括：(1) 对每个策略-流对建立辅助值函数的均匀Sobolev和Hölder估计；(2) 证明值函数在Young测度收敛和Wasserstein收敛下的稳定性；(3) 通过Fokker-Planck方程的对偶论证建立种群流映射的连续性（因Young测度收敛仅给出受控漂移的弱-*收敛）；(4) 构造非空紧凸值的集值最优响应/一致性映射；(5) 应用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理。

Result: 成功构造了一个满足Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理条件的集值映射，其不动点同时满足人内博弈的均衡响应条件和平均场一致性条件，从而建立了松弛均衡的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文为时间不一致平均场博弈提供了松弛均衡存在性的完整证明框架。通过将策略空间松弛为Young测度并借助不动点定理，克服了时间不一致性和策略空间非紧性带来的困难，为后续研究此类博弈的数值方法和应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of relaxed equilibria for finite-horizon continuous-time time-inconsistent mean field games. We work directly on the product space of relaxed feedback policies and population flows. The policy component is endowed with the stable topology of Young measures, while the population component is restricted to a compact convex set of Wasserstein-continuous flows with uniform moment and time-regularity bounds. For every policy-flow pair, we establish uniform Sobolev and Hölder estimates for the associated auxiliary value function and prove its stability under Young-measure convergence of policies and uniform Wasserstein convergence of population flows. We also establish continuity of the induced population-flow map. The latter requires a duality argument for the Fokker-Planck equations because Young-measure convergence yields only weak-$*$ convergence of the controlled drifts. We then construct a set-valued best-response/consistency map with nonempty compact convex values and apply the Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg fixed-point theorem. The resulting fixed point satisfies both the equilibrium response condition of the intra-personal game and the mean field consistency condition, , thereby establishing the existence of a relaxed equilibrium

</details>


### [299] [Safe Distributed Generalized Nash Equilibrium Seeking via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24077)
*Yihan Meng,Weijian Li,Lacra Pavel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带耦合约束集的非合作博弈中的广义纳什均衡（GNE）求解问题，通过在GNE求解动力学中引入控制障碍函数（CBF）来保证安全性，设计了全信息和部分信息两种设置下的动力学，并证明了约束集前向不变性、动力学平衡点与精确GNE等价以及渐近稳定性，同时扩展到多积分器智能体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作博弈中，当存在耦合约束集时求解广义纳什均衡需要确保安全性，即约束集在动力学演化过程中保持前向不变。现有的GNE求解方法缺乏对安全性保证的系统性设计，因此需要引入控制障碍函数来显式编码并保障安全约束。

Method: 将控制障碍函数(CBF)集成到分布式GNE求解动力学设计中，分别针对全信息设置（每个玩家知道所有其他玩家的决策信息）和部分信息设置（每个玩家只知道邻居玩家的决策信息）设计动力学。通过CBF约束确保耦合约束集前向不变，并使动力学的平衡点与精确GNE一致。进一步将方法推广到多积分器动力学模型。

Result: 理论结果证明：所提出的动力学保证耦合约束集前向不变、动力学平衡点与精确GNE一致、系统渐近稳定。数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性，并成功扩展到多积分器智能体场景。

Conclusion: 本文通过CBF框架为分布式GNE求解提供了安全性保障，涵盖全信息和部分信息两种通信场景，并推广至高阶多积分器动力学，为安全约束下的非合作博弈均衡计算提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) seeking in non-cooperative games with coupled constraint sets. Specifically, we aim to enforce safety for distributed GNE seeking, whereby the safety specifications are encoded in the coupled constraint set. To achieve this, we introduce the control barrier function (CBF) in the design of the GNE seeking dynamics. We design the dynamics for both full- and partial-information setting, where each player has knowledge of the decision information of all other players or only neighboring players, respectively. We justify the proposed dynamics by showing that the coupled constraint set is forward invariant, the equilibrium of the dynamics coincides with the exact GNE of the game, and the dynamics is asymptotically stable. Furthermore, we extend the approach to games where the agents are multi-integrators. Numerical simulations are provided to verify our results.

</details>


### [300] [Quadratic Optimization over Probability Measures with Coupling Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24184)
*Hoang Anh Tran,Yong Sheng Soh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二次目标函数、决策变量为概率测度的优化问题的凸松弛层级方法，将矩SOS（平方和）层级框架推广到概率测度上，并在最优传输（如Gromov-Wasserstein问题）和能量景观最小化等应用中建立了全局最优解的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的广义矩问题（Generalized Moment Problems）框架仅适用于目标函数为线性的情况，而许多重要应用（如Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输问题和能量景观最小化）涉及目标函数对决策变量（概率测度）的二次依赖，超出了现有建模框架的适用范围。

Method: 1) 提出基于基空间乘积上概率测度搜索的凸松弛层级；2) 将矩SOS（sum-of-squares）层级框架从多项式优化适配到概率测度优化；3) 引入一种扩展的矩阵正半定概念；4) 在决策变量满足边际约束（如Kantorovich最优传输公式）的假设下建立收敛性；5) 目标为多项式时，提出有限维半定规划（SDP）的矩SOS类型层级。

Result: 在边际约束假设下证明了所提层级收敛到全局最优解；在目标为多项式的额外假设下，提出了有限维SDP的矩SOS层级，其最优解收敛到原测度二次优化问题的最优解，并通过数值实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为涉及测度的非线性优化问题提供了SOS方法的推广路线图，展示了如何将原本针对多项式优化开发的SOS思想扩展到更广泛的测度非线性问题类别，为最优传输和能量景观最小化等领域提供了理论支撑和计算框架。

Abstract: We consider solving an optimization instance in which the objective is quadratic and where the decision variable is a probability measure. Our class of problems are motivated by applications arising from optimal transport (with the Gromov-Wasserstein problem being a prominent example) as well as energy landscape minimization. Because the objective depends quadratically on the decision variable, our class of problems fall outside the standard modeling framework of the Generalized Moment Problems (which requires the objective to be linear). To this end, we propose a hierarchy of convex relaxations based on searching over probability measures over products of the base space. These have a natural interpretation with the moment Sum-of-squares hierarchy-a prominent framework for solving polynomial optimization instances, which we adapt to accommodate probability measures. A key conceptual contribution is to introduce a notion of positive-semidefiniteness that extends the usual notion over matrices. Under the assumption that the decision variables satisfy certain marginal constraints (as in the Kantorovich formulation of the optimal transport problem), we establish convergence of our hierarchy towards the globally optimal solution. Under the additional assumption that the objective is a polynomial, we propose a moment-SOS type hierarchy of finite dimensional semidefinite programs whose optimal solution converges to that of the original quadratic optimization over measures. We demonstrate our framework with numerical experiments. More generally, optimization over measures where the objective and/or constraint depends on the decision in a polynomial way is a fundamental problem. It is hoped that our work provides a road-map as to how the ideas of the SOS-ordinarily developed for polynomial optimization-may be applied to a broader class of non-linear problems involving measures.

</details>


### [301] [A Line-Search-Free Coordinate Proximal Predictor-Corrector Method for Monotone Absolute Value Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24346)
*Haotian Wang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有残差单调性的绝对值方程（AVE）$Ax-|x|=b$，通过系数矩阵的对称部分刻画该性质从而允许非对称情形，并提出一种基于AVE特定前向-近端分解的坐标近端预测-校正（CPPC）方法。该方法在最大近端残差坐标上进行精确标量近端更新生成预测点，利用正对齐恒等式确保无需回溯的全残差分离超平面校正。每次迭代仅需一次新的全矩阵-向量乘积（缓存当前矩阵乘积）。在解集非空时证明了Fejér单调性、整序列收敛性和$O(K^{-1/2})$的最优迭代残差界；正单调性余量进一步保证对任意右端项解的唯一存在性及全局线性收敛。数值结果识别出降低每迭代工作量可缩短求解时间的适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对值方程（AVE）在互补问题、二阶锥规划等优化领域有广泛应用，但现有方法要么要求系数矩阵对称，要么收敛理论不完善或每迭代计算成本高。本文旨在放宽对称性限制，设计一种收敛理论完备且每迭代工作量低的求解算法。

Method: 提出CPPC方法：基于AVE特定的前向-近端分解，在最大近端残差坐标上执行精确标量近端更新以生成预测点；借助一个正对齐恒等式构建全残差分离超平面校正步骤，省去回溯过程。通过缓存当前矩阵乘积，使每迭代仅需一次新的全矩阵-向量乘积。

Result: 在解集非空条件下证明了Fejér单调性、整序列收敛以及$O(K^{-1/2})$的最优迭代残差上界；当存在正单调性余量时，进一步证明对任意右端项解的唯一存在性和全局线性收敛。数值实验表明在特定问题区域中降低每迭代计算量能够缩短总求解时间。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对称部分刻画残差单调性扩展了AVE方法的适用范围至非对称系数矩阵，CPPC方法在理论保证（Fejér单调性、收敛率）与计算效率（每迭代仅一次矩阵-向量乘积）之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模AVE问题提供了实用且理论上可靠的求解方案。

Abstract: We consider the absolute value equation (AVE) $Ax-|x|=b$ under residual monotonicity. We characterize this property through the symmetric part of the coefficient matrix, thereby allowing nonsymmetry, and propose a coordinate proximal predictor-corrector (CPPC) method based on an AVE-specific forward-proximal decomposition. An exact scalar proximal update on the largest proximal-residual coordinate generates a predictor point, while a positive-alignment identity certifies the ensuing full-residual separating-hyperplane correction without backtracking. With the current matrix product cached, each iteration requires one new full matrix-vector product. For a nonempty solution set, we prove Fejér monotonicity, whole-sequence convergence, and an $O(K^{-1/2})$ best-iterate residual bound. A positive monotonicity margin further ensures unique solvability for every right-hand side and global linear convergence. Numerical results identify regimes in which the reduced per-iteration work yields shorter solution times.

</details>


### [302] [Coupling-Aware Sales and Operations Planning with Forced Co-Production in Polymer Production Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24399)
*Mansur Arief,Yan Pratama Akhra,Bilal Ahmadi,Pradipta Apsarini,Iwan Vanany*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对聚合物工厂中并行生产线共享单一散装原料的耦合性问题，提出增强型产品集群毛利（AGPPC）框架，将传统的每吨排序指标转化为每耦合小时的排序指标，并附带最优性证明。在印尼聚合物生产商的实际运营数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的盈利能力指标（如单品毛利）假设生产线可以独立承诺，因此提供简单的每吨排序便于规划者使用。但在耦合工厂（尤其是聚合物工厂中并行生产线共享单一散装原料）中，这种假设根本性地失效，可能导致价值破坏的生产计划。因此需要一个考虑耦合约束的新框架。

Method: 提出AGPPC框架，将共生产产品列概念与规划问题的流体松弛相结合；构建了工厂的集成双线性混合整数规划模型，并以McCormick线性化作为基准提供认证界。

Result: 在印尼聚合物生产商的实际运营数据上，仅采用耦合感知指标就能使营业利润提高7.6%，完整优化则比当前实践提高28%。

Conclusion: 耦合感知的排序指标（AGPPC）能够替代传统简单指标，在保持实用性的同时提供最优性保证，且在实际数据中显著提升运营利润，为S&OP中产品优先级排序提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Profitability metrics, such as the single-product gross profit margin, constitute the standard toolbox to prioritize products in sales and operations planning (S&OP). These metrics leverage the premise that production lines can be committed independently, which endows them with a simple per-ton ranking that a planner can compute and use easily in practice. However, coupled plants, especially polymer plants whose parallel lines draw simultaneously on a single bulk feed, can fundamentally undermine this premise and mislead to value-destroying production plans. We propose a framework built on what we call the Augmented Gross Profit for Product Clusters (AGPPC), which converts the per-ton ranking metric into a per-coupled-hour ranking that carries a per-instance optimality certificate. We present the theory of AGPPC, which combines the co-production column concept with a fluid relaxation of the planning problem, alongside an integrated bilinear mixed integer program of the plant and a McCormick-linearized baseline that provides certified bounds for it, and we demonstrate its effectiveness on real operational data from an Indonesian polymer producer, where the coupling-aware metric alone raises operating profit by 7.6% and the full optimization by 28% over current practice.

</details>


### [303] [Mean field game model for human capital accumulation under budget constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24417)
*Saeed Sadeghi Arjmand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个确定性的—阶均值场博弈模型，用于描述人力资本积累过程。大量个体通过投资教育以最短时间达到一定资格水平，其学习生产率同时取决于个人当前人力资本水平和人口分布（体现知识溢出与社会互动）。学习由现有资本产生的收入资助，形成财务资源非负的状态约束。作者证明了最优控制与轨迹的存在性，通过动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析值函数，刻画最优学习努力，并借助Schauder不动点定理证明在有限人口时间范围内至少存在一个均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人力资本积累是经济增长和社会发展的核心问题，但现有模型往往忽略了个体决策与人口分布之间的相互作用（知识溢出、社会互动效应），也较少考虑个体在最短时间内达到目标资格水平这一现实优化目标。此外，学习自我融资带来的状态约束使得问题更具挑战性。

Method: 1) 将个体问题建模为带状态约束的最小时间最优控制问题（含时变动力学）；2) 利用动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析值函数；3) 在适当正则性和凸性假设下刻画最优学习努力；4) 由最优反馈导出连续性方程描述人口分布演化；5) 将均值场博弈构造为人口流的定点问题，通过构造紧致不变集并应用Schauder不动点定理证明均衡存在性。

Result: 证明了最优控制与轨迹的存在性，建立了值函数的动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程刻画，并在正则性与凸性假设下完成了最优学习努力的刻画。进一步在有限人口时间范围上证明了至少存在一个均值场博弈均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为带状态约束的人力资本积累均值场博弈提供了严格的理论基础，证明了均衡的存在性。值得注意的是，个体最小时间问题定义在无限时间范围，而均衡存在性须在有限人口时间范围上建立，这一对比揭示了问题的深层结构特征。

Abstract: We study a deterministic first-order mean field game model for human capital accumulation in which a continuum of agents invest in education and training to reach a certain qualification level in minimum time. The productivity of learning depends on both the individual's current level of human capital and the distribution of the population, modeling knowledge spillovers and social interactions. Learning is financed by the income generated by existing human capital, leading to a state constraint requiring the financial resources to remain nonnegative throughout the evolution. For a fixed population flow, we formulate the individual problem as a state-constrained minimum-time optimal control problem with time-dependent dynamics. We establish the existence of optimal controls and trajectories, analyze the associated value function through the Dynamic Programming Principle and a Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and characterize the optimal learning effort under suitable regularity and convexity assumptions. The optimal feedback induces a continuity equation describing the evolution of the population distribution. The resulting mean field game is formulated as a fixed-point problem for the population flow. By constructing a compact invariant set and applying Schauder's fixed-point theorem, we prove the existence of at least one equilibrium on every finite population horizon, while the individual minimum-time problem remains formulated on the infinite time horizon.

</details>


### [304] [Modelling Renewable Curtailment and Constraints in Ireland's Electricity System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24464)
*Richard Nayer,Sam Hodges,Waqquas Bukhsh,Claver Chitambo,Chanura Wijeratne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱尔兰电力市场的运营流程，并将其转化为一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，用于估算可再生能源的消减（Curtailment）与约束（Constraint）。模型在简单示例和爱尔兰真实输电网模型上进行了验证，结果证明公式化方法有效，同时也指出了模型局限和未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源占比不断提高，准确估算可再生能源的消减与约束对于电力系统规划和市场运营至关重要。爱尔兰电力市场具有独特的运营机制，需要将其转化为可计算的优化模型来进行量化分析。

Method: 将爱尔兰电力市场的运营流程转化为MILP模型，提供完整的数学公式化描述，并在简单示例和爱尔兰实际输电网模型上进行测试验证。

Result: 所提出的MILP公式化能够有效捕捉消减和约束效应，在简单示例和实际网络测试中均表现出预期功能，同时揭示了模型的局限性。

Conclusion: 该MILP模型成功实现了爱尔兰电力市场流程的数学建模，可用于估算可再生能源消减与约束，但模型仍有改进空间，未来工作将针对所识别的局限性进行优化。

Abstract: This paper describes the electricity markets and operational processes in the Irish power system and translates them into a Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model. The model is designed to estimate renewable generation Curtailment and Constraint. A full mathematical formulation is presented and tested on both a simple example and a realistic model of the Irish transmission network. The results show that the proposed formulation works as intended, capturing curtailment and constraint effects, while also highlighting model limitations and directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [305] [A constraint dissolving inexact penalty method for optimization problems with geometric constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24542)
*Xiaoxi Jia,Leander Lerch,Stefan Streif,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于非凸几何约束的约束溶解映射框架，并在此基础上开发了一种约束溶解非精确罚方法，用于求解具有一般集合隶属约束和非凸几何约束的优化问题。该算法收敛，且每个可行聚点是Mordukhovich稳定的，仅需温和的渐近Mordukhovich正则性条件。数值实验表明该方法在解质量上与安全增广拉格朗日方法相当，且显著优于罚分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何约束优化问题在机器学习、金融和控制等领域有广泛应用。约束溶解方法是解决这些几何约束的强大算法工具，但现有的约束溶解方法需要较强的约束规格（constraint qualifications），这限制了其适用性。

Method: 提出约束溶解映射框架来处理非凸几何约束；基于此开发约束溶解非精确罚方法，用于求解一般集合隶属约束与非凸几何约束的优化问题。收敛性分析仅需温和的渐近Mordukhovich正则性条件，比现有文献中的约束规格弱得多。
