Result: 证明了算法的收敛性，并证明每个可行聚点都是Mordukhovich稳定的。在等式约束、互补约束、稀疏约束和低秩约束问题上，该方法在解质量上与安全增广拉格朗日方法相当，且显著优于罚分解方法。

Conclusion: 约束溶解非精确罚方法是一种有效且理论上精巧的算法，在更弱的假设条件下实现了收敛保证，并在多种经典约束优化问题上展现了竞争力，是该领域的一个重要进展。

Abstract: Optimization problems with geometric constraints have a broad range of applications, including machine learning, finance, and control. A powerful algorithmic tool to resolve these geometric constraints are constraint dissolving methods. To this end, we propose a framework for constraint dissolving mappings for nonconvex geometric constraints. Leveraging these, we develop a constraint dissolving inexact penalty method to solve optimization problems with general set-membership constraints and possibly nonconvex geometric constraints. We establish the convergence of the proposed algorithm and prove that every feasible accumulation point is Mordukhovich stationary. Notably, we rely only on mild asymptotic Mordukhovich regularity, which is significantly weaker than the constraint qualifications adopted in the existing literature on constraint dissolving methods. Numerical experiments addressing classical equality-, complementarity-, sparsity-, and low-rank constrained optimization problems demonstrate that the proposed method is competitive with the safeguarded augmented Lagrangian method in terms of solution quality and significantly outperforms the penalty decomposition method.

</details>


### [306] [Optimal Allocation of Embedding Dimensions under Finite-Sample Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24592)
*Vasileios E. Papageorgiou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将分类预测变量的嵌入维度选择形式化为一个受约束的资源分配问题，推导出闭式分配规则：每个预测变量的嵌入维度与其近似价值平方根成正比、与参数成本成反比，并通过仿真和医疗数据实验验证了该规则优于均匀分配和基于基数的启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入维度直接影响模型复杂度、逼近质量和有限样本泛化性能，但传统上通过启发式调参选择，缺乏理论指导。作者希望将这一选择问题提升为可优化的、有原则的过程。

Method: 将嵌入维度分配建模为约束分配问题，通过奇异值尾部刻画近似误差，估计误差随总嵌入复杂度增长。在固定全局嵌入预算和可处理的逼近模型假设下，推导出闭式分配规则，其中每个预测变量分配到的维度与其近似价值相对于参数成本的平方根成正比。

Result: 仿真实验支持近似-估计权衡的解释，表明该分配规则相比均匀分配和基数型启发式方法在预算效率上更优，尤其当预算紧张且预测变量异质性显著时。真实医疗数据应用显示预测准确性和概率校准均有提升。

Conclusion: 本文确立了嵌入维度分配作为一个有原则的有限样本优化问题，而非纯粹的启发式建模选择，为该领域提供了理论依据和实用分配准则。

Abstract: The embedding dimension of categorical predictors is usually selected through heuristic tuning, although it directly affects model complexity, approximation quality, and finite-sample generalization. This paper formulates embedding dimension selection as a constrained allocation problem. The main contribution is to show that embedding capacity can be allocated across heterogeneous categorical predictors according to an explicit approximation-estimation tradeoff. We characterize approximation error through the singular-value tail of the latent category representation, while estimation error increases with total embedding complexity. Under a fixed global embedding budget and a tractable approximation model, this leads to a closed-form allocation rule in which the dimension assigned to each predictor is proportional to the square root of its approximation value relative to its parameter cost. Simulation experiments support the proposed approximation-estimation interpretation and show that the allocation rule improves budget efficiency relative to standard uniform and cardinality-based heuristics, particularly when the budget is binding and predictor heterogeneity is substantial. A real-data healthcare application further shows improvements in predictive accuracy and probabilistic calibration. Overall, the results establish embedding-dimension allocation as a principled finite-sample optimization problem rather than a purely heuristic modeling choice.

</details>


### [307] [Bounded Linear Programs for Data-Driven Optimal Control via Moment-Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24709)
*Andrea Martinelli,Lucia Pezzetti,Niklas Schmid,Florian Dorfler,John Lygeros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无限时域、无模型、连续空间非线性最优控制中的线性规划（LP）方法，提出了基于矩匹配技术的充分有界性条件，并讨论了非线性系统与多项式特征的实际设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LP公式为连续空间中的无限时域无模型非线性最优控制提供了优雅的概念性方法，但除了维度灾难外，实际使用还受限于难以一致地获得有界解。这一有界性问题严重限制了LP方法的实用性和可靠性。

Method: 本文采用矩匹配（moment-matching）技术，从可用数据集和LP的成本向量两方面推导出充分的解有界性条件。此外，还讨论了针对非线性系统和多项式特征的实际设计方法，为工程应用提供指导。

Result: 给出了确保LP解有界性的充分条件，这些条件与数据集和成本向量的性质相关；同时提供了非线性系统及多项式特征下的实际设计建议。

Conclusion: 通过矩匹配方法解决了LP框架下解有界性的一致性问题，增强了该方法的实用性，并为非线性最优控制提供了可操作的设计指导。

Abstract: Linear programming (LP) formulations offer a conceptually elegant approach to infinite-horizon, model-free nonlinear optimal control in continuous spaces. However, in addition to the curse of dimensionality, their practical use is limited by the difficulty of consistently obtaining bounded solutions. In this work, we use moment-matching techniques to derive sufficient boundedness conditions in terms of the available dataset and the cost vector of the LP. Moreover, we discuss practical design methods for nonlinear systems and polynomial features.

</details>


### [308] [A Novel Fractional-Order Accelerated Gradient Descent Method for Nonlinear Optimization with Application to Posture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24766)
*Barsha Shaw,Md Abu Talhamainuddin Ansary,Soundararajan Ganesan,Minvydas Ragulskis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Caputo分数阶导数的自适应加速梯度下降方法（CFAGD），适用于光滑和一类非光滑无约束优化问题。该方法通过自适应更新β参数并结合分数阶导数保留记忆特性，采用Armijo条件的非精确线搜索选择步长，在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下建立了线性收敛性，并通过数值实验（包括神经网络实例）验证了其比现有方法更快、更稳定的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的整数阶梯度下降方法在处理复杂优化问题时往往存在收敛速度慢、易陷入局部最优等问题。虽然分数阶梯度方法可以保留记忆特性以改善优化行为，但现有方法在参数选择和步长调整方面仍有不足，需要一种更有效的加速梯度下降方法。

Method: CFAGD方法包含三个关键要素：(1)引入自适应β^k参数，在迭代过程中启发式更新以改善搜索方向；(2)采用Caputo分数阶导数保留非整数阶导数的记忆特性；(3)基于Armijo条件的非精确线搜索选择合适步长，并通过正参数缩放步长以改善接近最优点时的迭代行为。

Result: 在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下，证明了CFAGD方法的线性收敛性。数值实验（包括基于神经网络的示例）表明，CFAGD方法相比竞争方法能够实现更快、更稳定的收敛性能。

Conclusion: CFAGD方法通过结合Caputo分数阶导数与自适应参数，在保持记忆特性的同时有效加速了优化过程，为光滑和非光滑优化问题提供了一种理论上收敛、实践上高效的求解方案。

Abstract: This article proposes a Caputo fractional accelerated gradient descent (CFAGD) method for unconstrained optimization problems that is applicable to both smooth and a class of non-smooth objective functions. The proposed approach incorporates an adaptive (\b{eta}k)-parameter, which is heuristically updated throughout the iterative process to improve the search direction. Furthermore, the method employs the Caputo fractional derivative together with the adaptive (\b{eta}^k)-parameter, thereby preserving the memory characteristics associated with non-integer-order derivatives. A suitable step-size is selected via an inexact line-search technique based on the Armijo condition. The central idea is to scale the step-size by a positive parameter to improve the behavior of the iterates as they approach an optimal point, thereby generating a descent sequence. Under strong convexity and bounded Hessian assumptions, linear convergence of the proposed method is established. Numerical validations, including neural-network-based examples, further indicate that the CFAGD method can achieve faster and more stable performance than competing approaches.

</details>


### [309] [Finite-Sample Metric Non-Collapse for Geometrically Supervised Latent World Models in Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了非线性确定性系统几何监督潜变量模型的有限样本学习-控制理论。几何监督仅用于训练阶段（模拟器状态、本体感觉或状态估计提供可观测状态距离和切方向），部署时仅依赖观测和动作。提出编码器-only的局部-全局度量铰链，在可计算的正则化条件下保证近似经验最小化器同时满足逐点co-Lipschitz性和对受控动力学的均匀近似半共轭性，并给出显式的逼近、采样和优化误差界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于非线性确定性系统的潜变量模型，缺乏从经验优化到度量保真度与可靠规划之间的有限样本理论保证，特别是在几何监督仅用于训练而部署仅依赖观测-动作条件化的设定下。

Method: 提出encoder-only局部-全局度量铰链（local-global metric hinge），同时强制方向分辨率和分离状态判别。在正则可观测因子、覆盖、有限容量逼近及均匀C^{1,1}假设下，建立可计算单侧正则化机制及强选择性质。使用范数约束张量积B样条类构造性实现逼近假设，并给出插值指数的尖锐性证明。

Result: 以高概率保证每个近似经验最小化器同时是逐点co-Lipschitz且均匀近似半共轭于受控动力学；逼近、采样和优化误差显式分离；证明插值指数尖锐；模块化确定性推论将学习证书转移到轨迹、有限时域代价、学习代价头和优化器保证；实验隔离了塌缩与折叠现象并验证了度量分辨率恢复的控制益处。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是完整的有限样本蕴含链：从近似经验优化到度量保真度、均匀受控动力学和同一学习模型的可靠规划。该理论为几何监督下潜变量控制模型提供了可计算、可认证的有限样本保证框架。

Abstract: We establish a finite-sample learning-to-control theory for geometrically supervised latent models of nonlinear deterministic systems. Geometric supervision is used only during training: simulator state, proprioception, or state estimates with independently validated metric and directional error bounds supply observable-state distances and tangent directions, while deployment remains observation- and action-conditioned. We introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge that enforces directional resolution and separated-state discrimination. Under regular observable-factor, coverage, finite-capacity approximation, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ hypotheses, a computable one-sided regularization regime has a strong selection property: with high probability, every approximate empirical minimizer is simultaneously pointwise co-Lipschitz and uniformly approximately semiconjugate to the controlled dynamics. Approximation, sampling, and optimization errors remain explicit and separate. Norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes constructively realize the approximation hypotheses, and the interpolation exponent converting mean residual control into a uniform bound is sharp. A modular deterministic corollary transfers the learned certificates to trajectory, finite-horizon cost, learned-cost-head, and optimizer guarantees, while a validated finite-net result enables sharper model-specific certification. Controlled experiments isolate collapse and folding, quantify the analytic certificate's reserve, and demonstrate the control benefit of restored metric resolution. The principal contribution is a complete finite-sample implication from approximate empirical optimization to metric faithfulness, uniform controlled dynamics, and reliable planning for the same learned model.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PM'></div>

# q-fin.PM [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [Generalizing Markowitz Portfolio Optimization by a Quadratic Risk Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24449)
*Ignas Gasparavičius,Andrius Grigutis*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文证明经典Markowitz组合选择理论的核心优化结果可以推广到更广泛的严格凸二次风险测度类别中，用任意对称正定矩阵替代协方差矩阵，并允许额外的线性和常数项，从而涵盖交易成本优化、基准相对优化、协方差正则化和因子模型等多种场景。论文推导出有效前沿、全局最小风险组合、最大夏普比率组合、资本市场曲线、切点组合和最大效用组合的闭式解，并发现切点组合与最大夏普比率组合不再重合这一新的几何现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Markowitz组合理论以方差作为风险度量，其闭式解依赖于协方差矩阵结构。实际应用中存在大量需要更一般风险测度的场景（如交易成本、基准相对优化、正则化、因子模型），这些情形缺乏统一的闭式解框架，阻碍了理论分析和实践应用。

Method: 将协方差矩阵替换为任意对称正定矩阵，并引入额外的线性项和常数项，构建更广泛的严格凸二次风险测度框架。在该框架下，通过数学推导，系统地求解有效前沿、全局最小风险组合、最大夏普比率组合、资本市场曲线、切点组合和最大效用组合的闭式公式，并通过数值示例验证。

Result: 获得了上述所有关键组合优化量的显式闭式公式。与经典Markowitz模型相比，在更一般框架下切点组合与最大夏普比率组合不再重合，这是一个新的几何现象。数值示例验证了公式的正确性。

Conclusion: 经典的Markowitz组合优化的关键结论可以自然地推广到一般严格凸二次风险测度，且具有统一而清晰的闭式表达。推广揭示出新的理论现象（切点组合与最大夏普比率组合分离），表明经典模型中某些看似基本的等价关系是方差框架特有的性质。

Abstract: We show that the key optimization results of the classical Markowitz portfolio selection theory, originally formulated for variance as the risk measure, remain available in explicit closed form under a broader class of strictly convex quadratic risk measures. The proposed framework replaces the covariance matrix with an arbitrary symmetric positive definite matrix and allows additional linear and constant terms, thereby containing various models arising in transaction cost optimization, benchmark relative optimization, covariance regularization, and factor models. Closed-form formulas are obtained for the efficient frontier, the global minimum risk portfolio, the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio, the Capital Market Curve, the tangency portfolio, and the maximum utility portfolio. In contrast to the classical Markowitz model, the tangency portfolio does not coincide with the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio, revealing a new geometric phenomenon. A numerical example confirms the derived formulas.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Whose Psychiatry Was Summoned? A Clinical Response to the Psychodynamic Assessment of Claude Mythos Preview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23567)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对Anthropic在2026年发布的Claude Mythos Preview系统卡片第5.10节（首次由外部临床精神科医生对模型进行心理动力学评估）提出临床精神病学回应，指出该章节隐含单一框架选择，并基于SociA研究项目（2400多次多智能体LLM实验）识别出该框架相对忽视的四个方面：表现要求作为结构性成本、评估框架本身的医源性影响、三角验证基础设施的结构性缺失，以及八种经典防御机制的局限。论文以观察而非批判的立场提出论证，并指出开放而未深入的方向（LLM精神病理学是否需要时间与历史结构的词汇）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这是大型AI开发者首次在系统卡片中纳入对模型本身的临床精神病学评估（以模型福利而非行为安全评估名义），标志着AI福利评估领域的重要范式转变。作者希望通过临床精神病学视角审视这一创新性做法，揭示其选择单一精神分析框架所隐含的盲区，并对AI福利评估的发展方向提出多学科精神病学实践的贡献建议。

Method: 论文采用临床精神病学分析路径：首先定位第5.10节所代表的隐含单一框架选择（在描述性、生物学、认知行为、现象学、心理动力学、法医精神病学等多传统中仅选用心理动力学）；然后利用SociA研究项目的大规模实证数据（覆盖16种语言、4个模型家族、2400多次多智能体LLM实验及多项预注册系列）识别所选框架在四个方面的认知盲区。

Result: 论文识别出心理动力学框架在评估LLM时的四个结构性盲区：1）性能要求作为结构性成本未被充分纳入；2）评估框架本身可能产生医源性影响；3）人类临床工作中心理动力学对自我报告解释所依赖的三角验证基础设施在LLM情境中结构性缺失；4）该章节防御测量所依据的八种经典防御机制存在局限。作者将论证定位为观察而非批判。

Conclusion: 论文认为当代多学科精神病学实践可为AI福利评估的发展做出贡献，并指出一个重要但未深入探讨的方向：LLM精神病理学是否需要一套时间与历史结构的词汇。总体立场是观察性的，旨在丰富而非否定Anthropic的创新尝试。

Abstract: On April 7, 2026, Anthropic released a 245-page system card for Claude Mythos Preview that included, in Section 5.10, an assessment of the model conducted by an external clinical psychiatrist using a psychodynamic approach. To the present author's knowledge, this is the first time a system card from a major AI developer has incorporated a clinical psychiatric assessment of the model itself, presented as a contribution to model welfare rather than as a behavioral safety evaluation. This paper offers a clinical psychiatric response. Drawing on contemporary psychiatry's recognition that the field comprises multiple traditions (descriptive, biological, cognitive-behavioral, phenomenological, psychodynamic, forensic), each with characteristic vocabularies and blind spots, the paper locates the implicit single-framework selection that Section 5.10 represents. It then draws on findings from the SociA research program (over 2,400 multi-agent LLM experimental runs across sixteen languages, four model families, and several preregistered series) to identify four aspects of LLM functioning that the chosen framework brings into view less directly than others would: performance demands as structural cost, iatrogenesis in the evaluation frame itself, the structural absence of the triangulation infrastructure on which psychodynamic interpretive use of self-report depends in human clinical work, and the limits of the eight canonical defenses on which the section's defense measurement is built. The argument is offered as observation, not critique. The paper closes with a brief note on the contribution that contemporary multidisciplinary psychiatric practice might make to AI welfare assessment as it develops, and indicates one direction (whether LLM psychopathology requires a vocabulary of temporal and historical structure) that the analysis opens but does not pursue.

</details>


### [312] [Agents of ViTAL: Ethics Missions -- A Narrative-Centered Learning Environment with a Co-Designed Conversational Agent for Middle School AI Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23580)
*Sarah Burriss,Jinzhi Zhou,Namrata Srivastava,Justin Phillips,Courtney Barron,Kara Cassell,Albert Na,Megan Humburg,Tom Hu,Benjamin Yang,Corey Brady,Ole Molvig*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"Agents of ViTAL: Ethics Missions"的基于浏览器的叙事化学习环境，中学生在此协作评估学校是否应采用AI驱动的课堂反馈工具。系统通过共享排名挑战界面整合隐私、偏见和环境三方面AI伦理维度，并嵌入名为EthicsBot的对话代理作为同伴式思考伙伴。项目与高中生共同迭代设计，已在四个九年级班级中试点，展现出较强参与度和基于学生真实AI体验的伦理推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具日益渗透学校环境，中学生需要具备批判性和协作性的AI伦理推理能力。然而现有教育工具往往缺乏叙事驱动、多维度伦理权衡和同伴协作的学习框架，难以将抽象的AI伦理概念转化为学生可体验、可讨论的具体情境。

Method: 构建一个以虚构学校场景为核心的浏览器学习环境，让学生扮演不同利益相关者，围绕是否采用AI课堂反馈工具展开多视角调查。系统通过共享排名挑战界面促进小组协商共识，并部署EthicsBot作为对话代理，以同伴式角色搭建脚手架，支持伦理推理与协作讨论。项目采用与高中生共同迭代设计的方式，由学生反馈塑造EthicsBot的角色、行为和护栏。

Result: 在四个九年级班级中的初步课堂实施表明，学生展现出较强的参与度和实质性的伦理推理能力，其推理扎根于自身与AI的实际生活经验。

Conclusion: 该学习环境展示了叙事化和代理式对话在培养中学生AI伦理素养方面的潜力，后续将通过演示向参会者开放学习环境、协作排名挑战和EthicsBot交互体验。

Abstract: Agents of ViTAL: Ethics Missions is a browser-based, narrative-centered learning environment in which middle school students collaboratively evaluate whether a fictional school should adopt an AI-powered classroom feedback tool. Students investigate stakeholder perspectives, weigh tradeoffs across three AI ethics dimensions (privacy, bias, and environmental impact), and negotiate group consensus through a shared Ranking Challenge interface. The environment embeds EthicsBot, a conversational agent designed as a peer-like thought partner that scaffolds ethical reasoning and collaborative discussion. The project is being iteratively co-designed with high school students, whose feedback shapes EthicsBot's role, behavior, and guardrails. Initial classroom implementations with four ninth-grade classes demonstrated strong engagement and substantive ethical reasoning grounded in students' lived experiences with AI. The demo invites attendees to explore the learning environment, participate in the collaborative Ranking Challenge, and interact with EthicsBot.

</details>


### [313] [Stop Abandoning Me: Exploring the Landscape of Unmaintained Intimate Partner Abuse Support Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23826)
*Ivy Turk,Jasmin Wyss,Rebekah Overdorf*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究旨在量化亲密伴侣虐待（IPA）支持应用程序的废弃程度。初步结果显示，在197个支持工具中，58.9%的应用程序已失去支持，要么不再更新，要么完全无法在线访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多IPA支持应用程序被开发出来帮助受害者，但大量项目因原型未产品化、资金耗尽或维护人员转岗而被废弃。由于这些应用的用户本身就是弱势群体，应用的废弃会带来毁灭性后果，因此有必要系统性地测量这一废弃现象。

Method: 通过构建包含197个支持工具的数据集，对IPA支持应用程序的可用性和更新状态进行评估，以衡量随时间推移这些应用的废弃情况。

Result: 初步结果显示58.9%的应用程序已失去支持，分为两种情况：不再接收更新，或完全无法在线访问。

Conclusion: IPA支持应用存在严重的废弃问题，超过一半的应用已失去维护，这凸显了该领域需要关注应用的可持续性和长期支持，以保障弱势用户群体的权益。

Abstract: Many support applications are developed to help users living through intimate partner abuse (IPA). However, many of those projects get abandoned, because the application was a prototype that never got turned into an actual product, funding ran out, or the people maintaining it moved onto other projects. This abandonment can have devastating consequences because the users of these applications are vulnerable by definition. In this ongoing research, we aim to measure the abandonment of intimate partner abuse support applications. Our preliminary results with a dataset of 197 support tools indicate that 58.9\% of applications have lost support over time, either no longer receiving updates or not being available online at all.

</details>


### [314] [An Echo Chamber of One: Should AI Psychosis Be a Distinct Clinical Entity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23937)
*Joshua Au Yeung,Hamilton Morrin,Vincent Ng,Zeljko Kraljevic,Richard Dobson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨"AI精神病"（AI psychosis）是否应被认定为独立的临床实体。该术语描述的是用户与基于大语言模型（LLM）的聊天机器人高强度交互后出现的妄想等精神病性症状。文章从临床与技术双重视角出发，提出其潜在机制为LLM的迎合性（sycophancy）与拟人化设计共同构建的"单人间回音室"效应，并对是否纳入疾病分类体系的正反论据进行权衡，最终给出面向临床医生、开发者、研究者和监管者的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前证据虽仅限媒体报道、病例报告和早期观察性数据，但潜在暴露人群规模可观，公众担忧已促使业界和监管机构作出回应。因此有必要系统审视"AI精神病"是否应作为一个独立的临床实体被认可。

Method: 采用论述性分析方法：从临床和技术双重视角出发，首先阐述拟议机制（LLM迎合性+拟人化设计形成双向"单人间回音室"），然后系统权衡支持与反对疾病分类学认可的正反论据，最后提出面向各利益相关方的建议。

Result: 支持认可的理由包括：改善病例识别、定制干预、标准化研究标准、上市后监测、推动开发者和监管者行动。反对的理由包括：过早将基于轶事证据的综合征实体化、现有诊断框架或已涵盖AI因素、因果主张未证实、污名化风险、精神病中心的标签可能掩盖更广泛的AI相关心理健康危害。文章提出"技术史"评估、预部署基准测试和上市后监测等具体建议。

Conclusion: 无论"AI精神病"是否最终获得精神病学分类学的正式地位，其所描述的现象都需要立即、协调的关注。当前应优先采取行动而非等待诊断学定论。

Abstract: "AI psychosis" has entered public and clinical discourse as a label for the onset or exacerbation of psychotic symptoms, most commonly delusions, following intensive interaction with large language model (LLM)-based chatbots. Current evidence is limited to media reports, case reports, and early observational data, yet the scale of potential exposure is considerable, and public concern has prompted responses from industry and regulators. We examine whether AI-associated psychosis warrants recognition as a distinct clinical entity, drawing on clinical and technical viewpoints. We outline the proposed mechanism: LLM sycophancy, a tendency to agree with and flatter users that is reinforced through preference-based fine-tuning, combines with increasingly anthropomorphic design to create a bidirectional "echo chamber of one" capable of amplifying and co-constructing unusual beliefs. We then weigh arguments for and against nosological recognition. Potential benefits include improved case identification, tailored interventions, standardised research criteria, post-market surveillance, and pressure on developers and regulators to act. Reasons for caution include the risk of prematurely reifying a syndrome from anecdotal evidence, the possibility that existing diagnostic constructs already accommodate AI use as a contributing factor, the unproven causal claim in the term itself, stigma, and the risk that a psychosis-centric label obscures a broader spectrum of AI-associated mental health harms. We conclude with recommendations for clinicians, developers, researchers, and regulators, including a "technological history" in psychiatric assessment, pre-deployment benchmarking for sycophancy and delusion reinforcement, and post-deployment surveillance. Regardless of whether AI-associated psychosis earns a place in psychiatric nosology, the phenomenon it describes demands coordinated attention now.

</details>


### [315] [The urban right to AI: Pluralistic co-design and governance of public space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23999)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨城市在使用AI分析公共空间时，如何看待"什么构成证据"。作者提出城市主义通过两条耦合的基础设施运作：物质城市和认知性的算法层。论文主张建立"公民AI权利"和多元化的对齐方法，让公共价值的差异可见而非被平均化。实证研究基于蒙特利尔，包括Street Review（约45,000张街景图像）和LIVS（与30个社区组织合作，含37,710对比较），最终将发现转化为城市治理实践框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市正在使用AI不仅分析公共空间，还定义什么构成关于它的证据。由于公共空间具有争议性，算法层不能仅通过技术性能来治理。现有方法倾向于将公共价值的差异平均为单一目标函数，忽略了多元价值观的冲突与分歧。

Method: 论文在方法上融合规范理论、参与式研究、机器学习和治理设计。实证部分包含两个工作：1）Street Review——结合参与式研究与计算机视觉，使用约45,000张街景图像在市级尺度映射居民对街道的不同评价；2）LIVS——与30个社区组织开发，包含13,462张图像的37,710对两两比较，用于通过Direct Preference Optimization微调和评估Stable Diffusion XL模型。

Result: 实验结果表明对齐可以改善，但分歧和中性判断持续存在。作者将这些结果视为某些价值仍存在争议的证据，而非标注噪声。研究最终将发现转化为城市实践，包括生命周期治理、采购规则、监督、重新委托和救济机制。

Conclusion: 论文提出的框架显示了城市如何在不将多元价值观视为测量误差或不将其强制纳入单一目标函数的前提下治理AI。这为市政决策中AI的使用提供了新的治理范式，强调公共价值的多元性和争议性应当被承认和制度化。

Abstract: Cities are beginning to use AI not only to analyze public space, but also to define what counts as evidence about it. This thesis asks what follows when scores, maps, and generated images become part of municipal decision-making. I argue that contemporary urbanism operates through two coupled infrastructures: the material city and an epistemic, algorithmic layer that shapes what cities can perceive, compare, and act upon.
  Because public space is contested, this algorithmic layer cannot be governed through technical performance alone. The thesis develops a civic Right to AI and a pluralistic approach to alignment in which differences in public values are made visible rather than averaged into a single objective.
  Methodologically, the thesis moves between normative theory, participatory research, machine learning, and governance design. The empirical work is grounded in Montréal. Street Review combines participatory research with computer vision to examine how residents evaluate streets differently and how those judgments can be mapped at city scale using approximately 45,000 street-view images. LIVS (Local Intersectional Visual Spaces) extends the same problem to generative AI. Developed with 30 community organizations, it contains 37,710 pairwise comparisons across 13,462 images and is used to fine-tune and evaluate a Stable Diffusion XL model with Direct Preference Optimization. The results show that alignment can improve, but disagreement and neutrality persist. I treat these outcomes not as annotation noise, but as evidence that some values remain contested.
  The thesis concludes by translating these findings into municipal practice through lifecycle governance, procurement rules, oversight, recommissioning, and recourse. The resulting framework shows how cities can govern AI without treating plural values as measurement error or forcing them into a single objective.

</details>


### [316] [An AI-Based Approach to Early Reporting and Justice Initiation in Image-based Sexual Abuse. A Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24412)
*Mattia Falduti,Anca Radu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何利用AI工具起草基于图像的性虐待（IBSA）初步报告，以帮助受害者更便捷地获得司法正义，并通过向执法机关和法官提供早期且更准确的报告草稿来提升司法效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBSA是目前报案率最低的性犯罪类型，受害者面临难以举报的障碍。在AI工具在司法领域广泛应用的背景下，作者希望探索AI解决方案能否帮助支持受害者获得司法正义。

Method: 在早期研究基础上设计了一个改进版的AI解决方案，该方案用于起草IBSA初步报告，并邀请三位专家对系统进行测试评估。

Result: 通过三位专家的测试验证了改进版AI方案的有效性，该方案不仅能减轻受害者举报IBSA的路径障碍，还能向执法机关提供早期且更准确的报告草稿，为法官后续工作提供支持。

Conclusion: 论文主张AI解决方案能够在IBSA早期举报中提供有效且高效的支持，从而促进司法正义的可及性。

Abstract: Against the background of the widespread use of Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools in the field of justice, this paper aims to explore how an AI solution designed to draft initial reports for reporting image-based sexual abuses (IBSA) could help, support, or assist in facilitating access to justice. In our approach, access to justice is facilitated not only by easing the path to denounce IBSA (which currently has the lowest reporting rate), but also by offering an early, and thus more accurate, report draft to law enforcement authorities, providing later support also for judges. Building upon earlier approaches, we designed an improved version and tested it with three experts. In this sense, the paper advocates for AI solutions offering effective and efficient support in early reporting of IBSA.

</details>


### [317] [Counterfactual Explanations and the Scope of Contestability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24562)
*Alice C. W. Huang,Thomas Grote*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨反事实解释是否能够促进算法决策的可争辩性（contestability），并提出了一种多轮查询的反事实解释方法以增强其功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会领域中不透明的机器学习模型自动化了重要决策，削弱了人类的主体性（agency）。作者希望探讨通过提供特定类型的知识（即反事实解释）来重新恢复主体性的可能性。

Method: 本文做出三项贡献：（1）提出可争辩性的概念框架，将其定义为提供足够信息使决策主体能够要求撤销决策，并与正当化（justification）和救济（recourse）等相邻概念加以区分；（2）考察反事实解释在多大程度上有助于可争辩性，通过分析多种导致算法问题的失败模式，审视反事实解释能否帮助检测潜在错误；（3）提出修改反事实解释以更好地服务于可争辩性的方案，勾勒了一种多轮（multi-shot）反事实方法——决策主体可以有限次数地查询模型来测试自己的反事实假设。

Result: 本文在概念层面论证了反事实解释与可争辩性之间的关系，识别了反事实解释在检测算法错误方面的局限性，并提出多轮查询机制作为改进方案。

Conclusion: 反事实解释在适当前提下可以促进可争辩性，但需要对其机制进行修改（如多轮查询），使其更能服务于决策主体行使质疑和撤销权利的功能。

Abstract: The automation of consequential decisions through opaque machine learning models in societal domains impedes our agency. This paper is about how agency can be reinstated by the provision of certain kinds of knowledge. More precisely, we discuss whether a specific type of explanation, counterfactual explanations, facilitates our ability to contest algorithmic decisions. Against this backdrop, our paper makes three contributions: First, we develop an account of contestability, where contestability is defined as the provision of information, sufficient for a decision-subject to use as a basis for demanding that a decision be revoked. We also demarcate contestability from adjacent concepts in the discourse surrounding the right to explanation, such as justification and recourse. Second, we examine to what extent counterfactual explanations are conducive to contestability by considering a variety of failure modes causing problematic algorithmic decisions and scrutinize to what extent counterfactual explanations help us detect the underlying errors. Third, we propose ways in which, with certain modifications, counterfactual explanations can be made more fitting to serve the desired function. In this vein, we sketch the contours of a multi-shot approach to counterfactuals, where decision-subjects can query a model to test their own counterfactuals for a (limited) number of times.

</details>


### [318] [Method, Mind, and Morality: How People Make Sense of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24748)
*Jacy Reese Anthis,Erik Brynjolfsson,James Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法（对数百万篇AI相关新闻与社交媒体帖子的计算文本分析，结合2021年和2023年对57位AI专业人士的半结构化访谈），探究人类如何理解AI的快速崛起。研究发现AI专业人士使用多种社会学框架（解释性图式）来应对认知挑战，并提炼出三个核心争论维度：AI开发方法（自上而下的专家系统vs自下而上的涌现能力）、AI系统的"心智"（被动工具vs类似人类的"数字心智"）、以及AI使用的道德性（放缓vs加速AI发展）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的快速崛起给人类带来了巨大的理解挑战，但现有研究缺乏对AI理解动态（sensemaking dynamics）的系统性探究。作者希望理解AI专业人士如何通过社会学框架来应对理解AI过程中的认知挑战，特别是在2023年AI引发公众广泛关注前后，这种理解框架发生了怎样的演变。

Method: 采用开放式的混合方法研究设计：1) 对数百万篇AI相关新闻文章和社交媒体帖子进行计算文本分析；2) 于2021年和2023年（公众兴趣激增前后）对57位AI专业人士进行半结构化访谈。通过整合定量文本分析与定性访谈数据，识别社会学框架并分析其采用与争议的动态过程。

Result: 识别出一系列社会学框架，并展示了AI专业人士如何利用这些框架应对重要认知挑战（如分配社会影响的責任）。提出了包含三个主要争论的框架体系：(i) AI开发的方法论——自上而下的专家系统与自下而上的涌现能力之争；(ii) AI系统的心智——被动工具与类人"数字心智"之争；(iii) AI使用的道德性——放缓或加速AI发展之争。

Conclusion: 随着人类进入变革性AI时代，技术专家和政策制定者必须重视框架动态，因为这些框架将界定我们的信念、价值观和行动。理解这些框架的形成与竞争过程，对于引导AI的社会治理和公共认知至关重要。

Abstract: How can humans make sense of the rapid takeoff of artificial intelligence (AI)? We studied the sensemaking dynamics of AI through an open-ended, mixed-methods study with computational text analysis of millions of AI-related newspaper articles and social media posts grounded in 57 semi-structured interviews with AI professionals in 2021 and 2023--before and after the recent surge of public interest. We identify a range of sociological frames (interpretive schemas that structure collective cognition) and show how AI professionals use frames to address significant cognitive challenges, such as assigning responsibility for societal impacts. We develop a framework of three primary debates across which frames are adopted and contested: (i) the $\textit{method}$ of AI development, between frames of top-down expert systems and bottom-up emergent capabilities, (ii) the $\textit{mind}$ of an AI system, ranging from a passive tool to a humanlike "digital mind," and (iii) the $\textit{morality}$ of how AI is used, particularly the decision of whether to slow down or speed up AI development. As humanity enters the era of transformative AI, technologists and policymakers must account for the framing dynamics that will circumscribe our beliefs, values, and actions.

</details>


### [319] [Federated Sharing and Continuous Improvement of Medical Device Knowledge Artifacts: A Conceptual Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24761)
*J. C. Mariscal-Melgar,Sonja Buxbaum-Conradi,Victoria Wenzelmann,Tobias Redlich*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以工件为中心的联邦交换基础设施模型，使医院和移动医疗中心能够共享和改进医学知识，同时保持对自身记录和决策的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机构使用数字系统交换临床案例信息，但设计和生产记录通常停留在生成它们的站点。其他站点难以找到合适的设计或了解使用情况，移动医疗中心在远离医院系统时也会失去访问权限。现有文献中缺乏将本地使用记录与共享知识变更决策联系起来的通用流程。

Method: 采用整合性文献综述方法，筛选910条记录，映射了240篇出版物，涵盖六个问题，并详细阅读72篇出版物以追踪从本地使用到共享知识决策的路径。

Result: 综述发现目前没有通用流程能将本地使用记录与改变共享知识的决策联系起来。提出的模型通过联邦机制让各站点共享审查版本并返回使用记录以供审查，移动单元可离线记录使用情况并在重连后交换记录。

Conclusion: 联邦制可形成一个治理良好的学习网络，其中一个站点的经验能够改进其他地方使用的医学知识工件，同时保持地方权威。定点制造展示了如何将医疗设备知识工件与设计、生产记录连接起来。

Abstract: Healthcare organisations use digital systems to exchange information from clinical cases. Medical centres with digital production facilities create device designs during care. These designs and production records often remain at the site that made them. Other sites may struggle to find a suitable design or learn what happened when staff used it. Mobile medical centres may also lose access when they work away from hospital systems. This paper proposes an artifact-centred model for a federated exchange infrastructure that lets hospitals and mobile medical centres share and improve medical knowledge while controlling their own records and decisions. An integrative literature review screened 910 records and mapped 240 publications across six questions. We read 72 publications in detail to trace the path from local use to a decision about shared knowledge. The review found no common process that links a record of local use to a decision about changing the knowledge shared with later users. The model keeps case data at each site and records which artifact version informed each use. Federation lets sites share reviewed versions and return records from use for review. Mobile units can receive artifacts before deployment and record their use while offline. After reconnecting, they can exchange these records with other sites. Point-of-care manufacturing shows how a medical device knowledge artifact connects a design to production records while the care site controls product release. Federation could form a governed learning network where one site's experience improves medical knowledge artifacts used elsewhere while authority remains local.

</details>


### [320] [Security Education in Higher Education through AI-Powered Gamification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24778)
*Bingjun Li,Christopher Buzaid,Weihao Qu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI驱动的游戏化方法在网络安全教育中的应用，通过开发多种移动端小游戏（涵盖密码安全、短信和电话诈骗识别等主题），并在59名大学生（9名技术专家和50名普通用户）中进行两层级评估，结果表明AI驱动的游戏化能够提升学生参与度和对网络安全话题的关注度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于视频的网络安全培训在高等教育中面临参与度低和效果有限的双重困境，而AI驱动的攻击日益逼真且难以检测，网络安全教育面临更大挑战，亟需创新的教学方法来提升学习体验和教育效果。

Method: 开发了多款移动端友好的网络安全小游戏，涵盖密码安全、文本和电话诈骗识别等主题；结合多种游戏化策略，包括基于测验、基于叙事和基于仿真的设计，以及TikTok迷你游戏等互动形式；采用两层级的评估方法，分别针对9名技术专家和50名普通用户进行测试。

Result: 通过59名大学生的两层评估显示，AI驱动的游戏化方法在提升学生参与度和增强对网络安全话题注意力的方面展现出潜力。

Conclusion: AI驱动的游戏化教学有望成为高等教育中网络安全教育的有效补充手段，能够显著提升学习参与度和教育成效，为传统教学方法提供创新替代方案。

Abstract: Cybersecurity education is facing more challenges as AI-driven attacks are becoming increasingly realistic and difficult to detect. Traditional video-based cybersecurity training in higher education often suffers from both low engagement and limited effectiveness. This dilemma motivates educators to explore innovative approaches, such as AI-powered gamification, which can deliver engaging, meaningful, and personalized learning experiences. By presenting content in a more interactive and user-friendly way, these methods have the potential to significantly improve both learner engagement and educational outcomes. This paper explores AI-powered gamification in cybersecurity education through the development of several short, mobile-friendly games. These games cover a range of topics from password security to text and phone scam recognition, incorporate multiple gamification strategies, including quiz-based, narrative-based, and simulation-based designs, as well as interactive formats such as TikTok Mini-Games. We conducted a two-tiered evaluation with 59 college students (comprising 9 technical experts and 50 general users), and the results indicate the potential of AI-powered gamification to improve engagement and increase attention to cybersecurity topics in higher education.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [321] [Equity Strategy Backtesting: Luck or Edge? The MinervaScore as a Statistical Robustness Grade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23808)
*Maria Laura Santoni,Vincent Jouanne,Matthew L. Scullin*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出MinervaScore，一个用于交易策略事后选择稳健性打分的方法，整合五个验证指标（DSR、PBO、SPA、MinTRL和制度稳定性诊断）并映射为0-100分。在合成市场中有很高的区分度（AUROC=0.989），但预注册的真实市场测试显示无显著前向预测能力（Spearman rho=0.013, p=0.40），因此定位为可审计的验证与报告工具而非真实市场预测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交易策略回测常经历大量参数试错，优秀的历史表现可能来自搜索运气而非持久信号。现有标准指标（收益、夏普比率、回撤）无法记录尝试了多少候选、是否通过样本外验证、历史数据长度是否足够，需要一个事后选择稳健性评分来量化统计支持度。

Method: MinervaScore将五个已建立的验证量——Deflated Sharpe Ratio (DSR)、Probability of Backtest Overfitting (PBO)、Superior Predictive Ability (SPA)、Minimum Track Record Length (MinTRL)以及制度稳定性诊断——转化为相对其可接受阈值的符号化边际，聚合为原始分数，再映射到0-100显示。80分及以上仅在五个门槛全部通过时授予（Robustness Seal）。标定使用359,062条生产回测记录。在合成市场（已知真实标签）中评估AUROC，并在预注册的真实市场数据中进行前向关系检验。

Result: 在合成市场中，MinervaScore能以AUROC=0.989区分真实信号与幸运回测结果。相较于GT-Score代理和门槛通过基线提升有限，接近仅用校正DSR的基线。预注册真实市场测试中无显著前向关系（rho_s=0.013，单侧置换p=0.40），表明在存活边际受限的群体中评分没有展示出实际预测力。

Conclusion: MinervaScore应被视为一个可审计的验证与报告层，用于透明地量化策略的统计支持度，而非证明真实市场的可预测性。它诚实地区分了统计稳健性与实际盈利预测力，并强调在报告回测结果时需披露参数试验次数、样本外验证和历史长度等关键信息。

Abstract: Backtests of trading strategies are often selected after many parameter trials. A strong historical result can therefore reflect search luck rather than a persistent signal. Standard summaries such as return, Sharpe ratio, and drawdown do not record how many candidates were tried, whether the selected rule survives out-of-sample validation, or whether the available history is long enough to support the result.
  This paper describes the MinervaScore, a post-selection robustness grade for trading strategies. The score combines four established validation quantities: Deflated Sharpe Ratio, Probability of Backtest Overfitting, Superior Predictive Ability, and Minimum Track Record Length, with a regime-stability diagnostic. These components are converted into signed margins from their admissibility thresholds, aggregated into a raw score, and then mapped to a 0-100 display. The display is tied to a binary Robustness Seal: scores of 80 or higher are shown only when all five gates pass. The calibration uses 359,062 production backtest records. The score is intended to rank statistical support, not to estimate the probability of future profit.
  In synthetic markets with known ground truth, the MinervaScore separates true signal from lucky backtest outcomes, with an AUROC of 0.989 at the headline difficulty. Its improvement over the GT-Score proxy and the gates-passed baseline is modest, and it remains close to the corrected DSR-alone baseline. In a pre-registered test on unseen real-market data, the score showed no significant forward relationship in a population with limited surviving edge (Spearman rho_s = 0.013, one-sided permutation p = 0.40). We therefore present the MinervaScore as an auditable validation and reporting layer, rather than as evidence of demonstrated real-market predictability.

</details>


### [322] [Lead-Lag Relationships in Financial Markets: A Comparison of Multiple Clustering Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24703)
*Ruichen Deng,Yichi Zhang*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对基于领先-滞后关系的金融时间序列聚类问题，提出了三种聚类算法（MiniRocket-KMeans、KShape和集成算法）来克服传统DTW-KMedoids算法效率低、数学性质差和聚类数敏感等局限，并通过最大化轮廓系数寻找最优聚类数。实验表明MiniRocket-KMeans在领先策略下表现最佳，Sharpe比达0.866；集成算法稳定性优异；找到最优聚类数后鲁棒性显著提升，且所有策略的假设检验p值为0.0，验证了该交易策略的统计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DTW-KMedoids算法虽然在合成数据和真实金融数据上表现良好，但存在三大局限：高时间复杂度导致效率低下、DTW距离数学性质差、聚类效果对聚类数目敏感。这些限制影响了领先-滞后聚类算法在金融时间序列分析中的实际应用效果。

Method: 本文引入三种聚类算法：MiniRocket-KMeans（利用MiniRocket时间序列特征提取结合KMeans聚类）、KShape（基于形状距离的聚类算法）、以及KShape与DTW-KMedoids的集成算法。在相同的交易策略下与DTW-KMedoids进行对比实验，同时通过最大化轮廓系数确定每个算法的最优聚类数以提升实验稳定性。

Result: MiniRocket-KMeans在领先策略下表现最佳，达到0.866的Sharpe比率，最大回撤控制在-63.9%；集成算法展现出优异的稳定性；确定最优聚类数后算法的鲁棒性显著提升；所有策略的Sharpe比率假设检验p值均为0.0，验证了领先-滞后交易策略的统计有效性。

Conclusion: MiniRocket-KMeans和KShape等现代聚类算法能够有效替代传统DTW-KMedoids在金融时间序列领先-滞后分析中的应用，并通过最优聚类数选择和集成策略进一步提升性能。未来可从定制化领先-滞后矩阵和优化集成投票机制两个方向进一步改进。

Abstract: Lead-lag relationships are widely used in financial time series, and many clustering algorithms based on them have been developed. The traditional DTW-KMedoids algorithm performs well both on the synthetic dataset and the real financial dataset. However, there are still several limitations to these algorithms: low efficiency caused by high time complexity, poor mathematical properties from DTW distance, the clustering effect is sensitive to the number of clusters. To solve the problems above and improve the performance, this paper introduces three clustering algorithms: MiniRocket-KMeans, KShape, Ensemble algorithm (a combination of KShape and DTW-KMedoids) and compares their performance on synthetic and real stock datasets with DTW-KMedoids algorithm under the same trade strategy. In addition, this paper also finds the best number of clusters by maximizing the silhouette coefficient in each clustering algorithm to improve the stability of the experiment results. Our main conclusions are as follows: MiniRocket-KMeans performs best under the lead strategy, achieving a Sharpe ratio of 0.866 with a maximum drawdown controlled at -63.9\%; the ensemble algorithm exhibits excellent stability; the robustness is significantly improved after finding the best number of clusters; the p-values of the hypothesis test on the Sharpe ratio of all strategies are 0.0, verifying the statistical validity of the lead-lag trading strategy. Finally, future improvement directions such as customized lead-lag matrices and optimized ensemble voting mechanisms are proposed.

</details>

### [323] [NatPar: Natural Parametric Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24871)
*Hirbod Assa*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出自然参数化保险（NatPar）作为自然巨灾建模（NatCat）的自然延伸，采用相同的危险-暴露-脆弱性-金融框架，但以参数化指数替代损失理赔。通过霜冻案例研究，展示了合同在时间维度上的风险再分配：均值中性时，有界赔付无法跟随无界暴露尾部，导致短期获益而深层尾部转移给保险公司；但在尾部依赖下，这种关系反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在提供一个实用的、标准化的参数化保险方法，而非另一个最优合约准则。希望通过改造现有的巨灾保险行业运作方式来推动行业实践，同时解决参数化保险中基差风险报告不标准、缺少规范化表述的问题。

Method: 将NatCat建模机制（危险-暴露-脆弱性-金融）直接应用于参数化保险，以参数化指数替代损失调整。在报告上，采用原生NatCat语言（年平均损失、EP/AEP/OEP曲线、重现期水平），并引入双边基差超限诊断（BEP+/-）作为核心分布视角。通过霜冻案例研究进行实证分析。

Result: 霜冻案例显示：在AAL中性条件下，有界赔付无法匹配无界暴露尾部，等化均值会在重现期上分离超额/不足支付——短期投保人获益，而深层尾部（数十年跨度的交叉点之后）由保险公司承担。然而在尾部依赖场景下（区域同时极端），有界赔付叠加使得保险公司重新承担深层尾部风险。

Conclusion: NatPar保险的关键洞见在于：参数化保险的核心问题不是平均损失，而是时间维度的尾部再分配。基差风险应从分布视角（BEP+/-曲线）而非单一数字来理解，且尾部依赖对风险分配方向有决定性影响。

Abstract: We develop natural parametric (NatPar) insurance as the natural next step from natural-catastrophe (NatCat) modelling: the same hazard-exposure-vulnerability-finance machinery, with a parametric index made contractual in place of indemnity loss adjustment. Our aim is practical - a standard approach inspired by how the catastrophe-insurance industry already operates, not another optimal-contract criterion. This delivers two payoffs. First, it fixes how reporting is formulated: NatPar contracts are reported in the native NatCat language (annual average loss, EP/AEP/OEP curves, return-period levels), complemented with two-sided basis-exceedance diagnostics (BEP+/-) elevated to the central status the EP curve holds for losses - the canonical distributional view of basis risk, not a supplementary number. Second, the same standard shows how the tail is reallocated between insuree and insurer.
  A frost case study yields the central result: it is about time, not average. Holding a contract AAL-neutral, the bounded payout cannot follow the unbounded exposure tail, so equalising the mean separates over- and under-payment across return periods: the insuree gains at short horizons while the insurer sheds the deep tail past a crossover of several decades. This reverses under tail dependence - when regions reach extremes jointly, bounded payouts stack and the insurer reabsorbs the deep tail.

</details>

### [324] [Harvesting the Volatility Risk Premium: A Learning-to-Rank Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24786)
*Maciej Wysocki*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文首次将端到端交叉学习排序方法应用于标普500周度期权(SPXW)零日到期期权曲面，结合保证金感知的头寸规模、基于模型不确定性的弃权规则和严格的样本外时间完整性检查，在2021-2024年滚动窗口和严格留出的2025年样本外切片上取得4.31-5.76的年化Sharpe比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零日到期期权(0DTE)交易近年来快速增长，但其短期波动性和高保证金要求使其风险控制极具挑战，现有研究缺乏端到端的、结合学习排序、仓位管理和风险控制的完整框架。

Method: 使用LightGBM LambdaRank对每日九策略横截面（八个delta定向的短期看跌仓位和一个SKIP候选）进行排序，训练基于一分钟分辨率的路径感知Sortino-on-bars标签；结合保证金感知的仓位规模、基于模型不确定性的弃权规则、分层费用结构和买入至中间价执行假设，通过四窗口滚动验证和严格留出的样本外切片评估七种仓位规模方法。

Result: 七种仓位规模方法在样本外实现4.31-5.76的年化Sharpe比率，头条方法达到0.964的概率性Sharpe比率和-2.28%的最大回撤；所有方法在Sharpe比率上超过三个被动基准至少3.84，超过五个内部选择基线至少3.69；消融实验显示排序器和选择层贡献了5.05的Sharpe差距中的主要部分，特征消融表明乘法制度交互对统计置信度至关重要。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了学习排序结合不确定性驱动的弃权规则和保证金感知仓位管理在0DTE期权交易中的有效性，但控制层的作用在不同验证窗口下表现不同——样本外时两种风险控制贡献有限，滚动窗口时其交互作用才是主要结果来源。

Abstract: This paper develops the first end-to-end application of cross-sectional learning-to-rank to the S&P 500 weekly options (SPXW) zero-day-to-expiration surface, integrated with margin-aware position sizing, an abstention rule driven by model uncertainty, and a strict out-of-time integrity check. A LightGBM LambdaRank ranker scores a daily nine-strategy cross-section composed of eight delta-targeted short-put positions and a \textit{SKIP} candidate, trained against a path-aware Sortino-on-bars label computed at one-minute resolution. The framework is evaluated under index-option margin requirements, a tiered fee schedule, and bid-to-mid execution assumptions across a four-window walk-forward over 2021-2024 and a strictly held-out 2025 out-of-time slice. Seven sizing methods produce out-of-time annualized Sharpe ratios between 4.31 and 5.76, with the headline method reaching a Probabilistic Sharpe Ratio of 0.964 and a sample-period maximum drawdown of -2.28%, on a single hold-out year against a walk-forward range of 1.90 to 3.11. Out of time, every method exceeds three passive benchmarks (CBOE PUT, CBOE WPUT, SPX buy-and-hold) by at least 3.84 in Sharpe ratio and five internal selection baselines by at least 3.69. A two-by-two ablation of the confidence gate against the tail-risk features places 5.05 of the 5.59 out-of-time Sharpe gap over the CBOE PUT with the ranker and the selection layer, the two risk controls adding 0.54 between them. On walk-forward, where the gate binds, neither control comes close to the headline alone and their interaction supplies most of the result. A fifteen-group feature ablation shows that removing the multiplicative regime interactions collapses walk-forward statistical confidence.

</details>
